# A+B 类按逻辑链串题：271 道原题

> 生成日期：2026-09-10。A 类 124 题与 B 类 147 题合并分组；不按等级重复建节。共收录 271 题，已写 269 篇答案，2 题待补素材。全部为待练材料。

来源：[核心主题库](../part3-topic-bank.md) · [逐题迁移审计](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md) · [Part 2 答案题库](../part2/topic-bank.md)。A 类保留已有短答案，B 类优先复用现有 Part 2 的人物和场景；本稿不采用网页检索材料。

**怎么学：** 先看每节共用链，再看逐题桥梁，遮住英文自行口述。英文加粗处是因果机制。主链相同的 A/B 题放在同一节；需要另一条链时链接到对应节，不复制答案。

**素材口径：** Part 2 是已有练习素材，不是对真实经历的再次核实；一般人物场景迁移用 can / could / may。人数、全国偏好和排名不能由个人例子推出。P061 补用已存 Part 1 的中学回忆。P049、P306 缺关键事实，保留待补条目，不计入已写答案。

**维护：** 后续 A/B 答案只在本文件修改；旧 `01–04` 文件保留作历史参考，不要求同步修改。P 编号沿用审计，A/B 标签不因生成而升级。

<a id="ab01"></a>

## AB01 练习与反馈 → 实际能力提高

**怎么串：** 老师、同伴或工具负责提供反馈；学习者负责尝试和修正。地点、年龄、技能可以换，不能省去主动实践。

- **主链：** regular practice and feedback → stronger practical skills → better performance or employability
- **反向 / 对照：** passive or exam-driven learning → limited application → weaker independent thinking

| 原题 | 等级 | 这题必须补出的桥梁 |
|---|---|---|
| [P005](#p005) | B | 认真纠错帮助提升能力 → 可能给学生带来当下压力 → 好老师不一定总讨喜。 |
| [P006](#p006) | A | 列举善于反馈和倾听；分别接技能提升与理解学生。 |
| [P010](#p010) | A | 持续练习、接受反馈和合理规划→更好表现；不必新增天赋论。 |
| [P018](#p018) | A | 练习反馈和规划→稳定表现；可直接迁移到运动员品质。 |
| [P019](#p019) | B | 技术记录训练 → 回看发现具体问题 → 针对性练习改进。 |
| [P020](#p020) | B | 运动员回看动作 → 找到弱点 → 下一轮训练针对修正。 |
| [P035](#p035) | A | 练习新技能→实际能力→更好表现；换成儿童生活技能。 |
| [P036](#p036) | B | 学校、家庭与兴趣班 → 都可提供示范和反馈 → 学习地点通过实践发挥作用。 |
| [P037](#p037) | A | 提供练习与反馈、开放探索→能力与独立思考。 |
| [P038](#p038) | A | 设定步骤、规律练习、获取反馈→技能提升。 |
| [P039](#p039) | B | 老师及时指出问题；自学由自己检查和安排 → 反馈来源与进度控制不同。 |
| [P040](#p040) | A | 自律落实规律练习与计划→能力提升；用每天练语言例子。 |
| [P042](#p042) | A | 动手实践与自由探索→实用技能、创造力。 |
| [P074](#p074) | A | 练习反馈和现实规划→能力与执行→更好结果。 |
| [P075](#p075) | B | 认识能使用英语和日语的人 → 反复真实使用形成能力 → 不从个例推出全国人数。 |
| [P077](#p077) | B | Stella 也学日语 → 通过语言交换持续使用 → 学习选择不只有英语。 |
| [P079](#p079) | A | 练习、反馈与探索→理解和实用能力。 |
| [P080](#p080) | A | 规律语言练习与有效反馈→更好实际运用。 |
| [P086](#p086) | A | 实践学习→生活技能；有意义活动→减压，换种植例子。 |
| [P153](#p153) | B | 学校教孩子规划并回看花费 → 学会合理分配有限的钱 → 减少乱花钱、为需要的东西存钱 → 因此值得教。 |
| [P161](#p161) | B | 练习清楚表达需要并获得指导 → 提高沟通能力 → 入学后更容易向老师求助。 |
| [P162](#p162) | B | 小组可互相反馈，独学要自己检查 → 反馈方式不同 → 两侧都需要主动实践。 |
| [P165](#p165) | A | 同伴示范、反馈与支持→练习改进→学会技能。 |
| [P166](#p166) | A | 实用专长→工作表现、提供有用服务；加并非人人需极窄专长的限定。 |
| [P200](#p200) | A | 实践反馈与开放探索→实际能力和独立思考。 |
| [P206](#p206) | A | 要求先独立尝试并提供反馈→保留练习→减轻依赖。 |
| [P297](#p297) | B | 视频示范便于观察动作，书籍便于按需阅读 → 都需实践检验 → 有用程度取决于任务。 |
| [P298](#p298) | B | 烹饪、绘画或动作示范 → 看后自己尝试并修正 → 学到实际技能。 |

<a id="p005"></a>

### P005 · B｜Do you think good teachers are always popular among students?

- [x] 不看稿口述

主题：T01 学习、创造与执行 ＋ T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M06 长期能力与短期便利

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB01 主链 / 对照](#ab01)

**串法：** 认真纠错帮助提升能力 → 可能给学生带来当下压力 → 好老师不一定总讨喜。

**答案：**

> Not necessarily. A good teacher isn't always popular with students because good teachers often give useful feedback, even when students don't want to hear it.
> For example, a teacher who asks students to rewrite their weak essays may help them improve but may also receive plenty of complaints.
> That said, explaining the purpose of the feedback can make it easier to accept.
> Overall, effective teaching and immediate popularity don't always go together.

**学习提示：** 反例必须是“教得好却不受欢迎”，不能倒过来。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a new skill you learned when you were a child](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20new%20skill%20you%20learned%20when%20you%20were%20a%20child.md)

论点回查：[E22](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#22-what-is-the-most-important-factor-in-learning-well)、[E29](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#29-what-does-effective-communication-involve-有效沟通)、[E50](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#5051-constant-pressure-and-well-being-持续压力与身心健康) · [审计 P005](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p005)。

<a id="p006"></a>

### P006 · A｜What are the qualities of being a good teacher?

- [x] 不看稿口述

主题：T01 学习、创造与执行 ＋ T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M06 长期能力与短期便利

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB01 主链 / 对照](#ab01)；辅助链：[AB06](#ab06)

**串法：** 列举善于反馈和倾听；分别接技能提升与理解学生。

**答案：**

> I think a good teacher should explain knowledge clearly, listen carefully, and most importantly, g<mark style="background: #ABF7F7A6;">ive useful feedback</mark>.
> These qualities help students understand their mistakes and build practical skills through regular practice.
> For example, a language teacher can listen to a short answer and suggest a specific improvement for the next try.
> So, good teachers help students understand what they need to learn and how they can improve step by step.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a new skill you learned when you were a child](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20new%20skill%20you%20learned%20when%20you%20were%20a%20child.md) · [Describe a person who is good at learning and speaking new languages](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20person%20who%20is%20good%20at%20learning%20and%20speaking%20new%20languages.md)

论点回查：[E22](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#22-what-is-the-most-important-factor-in-learning-well)、[E29](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#29-what-does-effective-communication-involve-有效沟通)、[E30](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#30-example-of-active-listening-积极倾听的例子) · [审计 P006](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p006)。

<a id="p010"></a>

### P010 · A｜What qualities should an athlete have?

- [x] 不看稿口述

主题：T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M06 长期能力与短期便利

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB01 主链 / 对照](#ab01)；辅助链：[AB03](#ab03)

**串法：** 持续练习、接受反馈和合理规划→更好表现；不必新增天赋论。

**答案：**

> In my view, an athlete needs more than just talent.
> They need to be disciplined, willing to accept feedback, and able to plan their training.
> Regular practice builds practical skills, while a realistic schedule helps them focus on what needs to improve.
> For example, if a runner is willing to listen to their coach's advice and follow a manageable training plan, they can adjust their technique and make steady progress.
> So, steady effort and smart planning are important qualities for an athlete.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a new skill you learned when you were a child](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20new%20skill%20you%20learned%20when%20you%20were%20a%20child.md) · [Describe a person who is good at learning and speaking new languages](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20person%20who%20is%20good%20at%20learning%20and%20speaking%20new%20languages.md)

论点回查：[E22](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#22-what-is-the-most-important-factor-in-learning-well)、[E26](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#26-what-helps-people-make-an-informed-decision--version-1) · [审计 P010](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p010)。

<a id="p018"></a>

### P018 · A｜What qualities does a successful sportsperson need?

- [x] 不看稿口述

主题：T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M06 长期能力与短期便利

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB01 主链 / 对照](#ab01)；辅助链：[AB03](#ab03)

**串法：** 练习反馈和规划→稳定表现；可直接迁移到运动员品质。

**答案：**

> In my view, a successful sportsperson needs more than talent.
> What truly matters is being disciplined and willing to accept feedback from their coach.
> First, discipline and consistent practice help them build practical skills.
> Second, a willingness to improve and accept suggestions from their coach also matters.
> For example, a runner can make steady progress by adjusting their technique based on feedback from their coach.
> So, steady effort and smart planning are important for a successful sportsperson.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a new skill you learned when you were a child](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20new%20skill%20you%20learned%20when%20you%20were%20a%20child.md) · [Describe a person who is good at learning and speaking new languages](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20person%20who%20is%20good%20at%20learning%20and%20speaking%20new%20languages.md)

论点回查：[E22](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#22-what-is-the-most-important-factor-in-learning-well)、[E26](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#26-what-helps-people-make-an-informed-decision--version-1) · [审计 P018](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p018)。

<a id="p019"></a>

### P019 · B｜How is technology used in sports?

- [x] 不看稿口述

主题：T01 学习、创造与执行 ＋ T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响

机制：M06 长期能力与短期便利

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB01 主链 / 对照](#ab01)

**串法：** 技术记录训练 → 回看发现具体问题 → 针对性练习改进。

**答案：**

> Well, technology is used to record training data and help athletes review their performance.
> Seeing what happened during an attempt gives them something specific to improve next time.
> For example, a tennis player can watch a recording of their serve and work on the part their coach points out.
> They can then review the recording again and see whether they have improved.
> So, recording can turn training into a clear process of feedback and continuous improvement.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a sportsperson who did well in an event](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20sportsperson%20who%20did%20well%20in%20an%20event.md) · [Describe an advertisement with a famous person in it](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20an%20advertisement%20with%20a%20famous%20person%20in%20it.md)

### P020 · B｜How do athletes use technology to improve their performance?

- [x] 不看稿口述

主题：T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M06 长期能力与短期便利

题型：Q6

逻辑链：[本节 AB01 主链 / 对照](#ab01)

**串法：** 运动员回看动作 → 找到弱点 → 下一轮训练针对修正。

**答案：**

> Athletes can use video recordings to spot weaknesses that are difficult to notice while moving.
> **Reviewing an attempt with a coach** helps them decide what to practise next.
> For example, a swimmer can watch a turn, identify where they lose time and work on that movement in the next session.
> So, technology is useful when it leads to focused practice.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

<a id="p035"></a>

### P035 · A｜Why should children learn new skills?

- [x] 不看稿口述

主题：T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M06 长期能力与短期便利

题型：Q1

逻辑链：[本节 AB01 主链 / 对照](#ab01)

**串法：** 练习新技能→实际能力→更好表现；换成儿童生活技能。

**答案：**

> I think children should learn new skills because these skills help them handle practical problems beyond textbooks.
> Regular practice and useful feedback help them improve step by step.
> For example, a child learning to cook can try a simple meal, get tips from a parent, and adjust next time.
> That feedback loop builds independence.
> So, learning new skills makes kids more capable in daily life.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a new skill you learned when you were a child](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20new%20skill%20you%20learned%20when%20you%20were%20a%20child.md) · [Describe a person who is good at learning and speaking new languages](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20person%20who%20is%20good%20at%20learning%20and%20speaking%20new%20languages.md)

论点回查：[E22](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#22-what-is-the-most-important-factor-in-learning-well) · [审计 P035](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p035)。

<a id="p036"></a>

### P036 · B｜Where can children learn new skills?

- [x] 不看稿口述

主题：T01 学习、创造与执行 ＋ T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M06 长期能力与短期便利

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB01 主链 / 对照](#ab01)

**串法：** 学校、家庭与兴趣班 → 都可提供示范和反馈 → 学习地点通过实践发挥作用。

**答案：**

> Children can learn new skills at school, at home, or in activity classes.
> What matters is having the chance to practice and someone who can give useful feedback.
> For example, a child can learn how to cook with their parents.
> A parent can give advice based on the child's first try, and the child can adjust their approach according to that feedback.
> This can help them improve more efficiently.
> So, where children learn new skills is less important than having enough practice and practical guidance.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

<a id="p037"></a>

### P037 · A｜How can parents and teachers teach children new skills?

- [x] 不看稿口述

主题：T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M06 长期能力与短期便利

题型：Q6

逻辑链：[本节 AB01 主链 / 对照](#ab01)；辅助链：[AB02](#ab02)

**串法：** 提供练习与反馈、开放探索→能力与独立思考。

**答案：**

> Parents and teachers can give children enough time to practice, useful feedback, and room to explore.
> Children develop stronger skills when they try things themselves and understand how to improve, instead of simply following rigid instructions.
> For example, a teacher can ask children to build a paper bridge or solve a problem using their own method.
> This allows children to test their ideas, see what works, and make adjustments when necessary.
> So, practical activities with guidance are a useful way to teach children new skills.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a new skill you learned when you were a child](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20new%20skill%20you%20learned%20when%20you%20were%20a%20child.md) · [Describe a person who is good at learning and speaking new languages](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20person%20who%20is%20good%20at%20learning%20and%20speaking%20new%20languages.md)

论点回查：[E22](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#22-what-is-the-most-important-factor-in-learning-well)、[E25](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#25-how-should-education-promote-open-mindedness--version-3) · [审计 P037](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p037)。

<a id="p038"></a>

### P038 · A｜How do adults learn a new skill?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M06 长期能力与短期便利

题型：Q6

逻辑链：[本节 AB01 主链 / 对照](#ab01)；辅助链：[AB03](#ab03)

**串法：** 设定步骤、规律练习、获取反馈→技能提升。

**答案：**

> Adults can learn a new skill by setting realistic steps and practising regularly.
> **Feedback helps them identify weaknesses and focus their next attempt on something specific.**
> For example, an adult learning English can record a short answer, get corrections and try the same question again.
> So, steady practice with feedback is a practical approach to adult learning.

<a id="p039"></a>

### P039 · B｜What are the differences between learning from a teacher and learning by oneself?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M06 长期能力与短期便利

题型：Q2

逻辑链：[本节 AB01 主链 / 对照](#ab01)

**串法：** 老师及时指出问题；自学由自己检查和安排 → 反馈来源与进度控制不同。

**答案：**

> Learning with a teacher usually offers more direct feedback, while studying alone gives the learner more control over the pace.
> A teacher can **point out a weakness immediately**, whereas an independent learner has to review their own attempts more carefully.
> For example, a language teacher can correct pronunciation, while someone studying alone might record themselves and compare it with a model.
> So, the main difference is how feedback and progress are managed.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a person who is good at learning and speaking new languages](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20person%20who%20is%20good%20at%20learning%20and%20speaking%20new%20languages.md) · [Describe a new skill you learned when you were a child](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20new%20skill%20you%20learned%20when%20you%20were%20a%20child.md)

论点回查：[E22](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#22-what-is-the-most-important-factor-in-learning-well)、[E26](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#26-what-helps-people-make-an-informed-decision--version-1) · [审计 P039](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p039)。

<a id="p040"></a>

### P040 · A｜Why is self-discipline important when learning a new skill?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M06 长期能力与短期便利

题型：Q1

逻辑链：[本节 AB01 主链 / 对照](#ab01)；辅助链：[AB03](#ab03)

**串法：** 自律落实规律练习与计划→能力提升；用每天练语言例子。

**答案：**

> Self-discipline is important because skills usually develop through regular practice.
> **Following a realistic plan helps people keep working on their weaknesses instead of practising only occasionally.**
> For example, a language learner can spend a short time speaking every evening and review one recurring mistake.
> So, self-discipline helps turn a learning plan into <mark style="background: #ABF7F7A6;">consistent progress.</mark>

> That said, useful guidance still matters during these activities.

> So, consistent effort with a sensible plan can lead to better results.

**串法：** 规律语言练习与有效反馈→更好实际运用。

> That said, only preparing for tests may limit practical language use.
> Overall, consistent practice matters most when it improves real communication.

> Yes, I think students can benefit from learning to grow plants.
> **It gives them practical tasks to practise and a meaningful activity outside their usual schoolwork.**
> For example, students can take turns caring for a classroom plant and get guidance from their teacher.
> That said, the tasks should be manageable alongside their studies.
> Overall, growing plants can be a useful practical learning activity.

<a id="p153"></a>

### P153 · B｜Should schools teach children how to manage money?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M06 长期能力与短期便利

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB01 主链 / 对照](#ab01)

**串法：** 学校教孩子规划并回看花费 → 学会合理分配有限的钱 → 减少乱花钱、为需要的东西存钱 → 因此值得教。

**答案：**

> Yes, I think schools should teach children how to manage money.
> **Learning to plan and review their spending** can help them avoid wasting money and save for things they need.
> For example, after a budgeting lesson, a child might spend less of their pocket money on snacks so they can afford a school bag.
> That said, lessons should stay simple and suit the children's age.
> Overall, money management is a useful life skill that schools should teach.

**学习提示：** Should schools teach… 问的是应不应该教；主理由要说明学会理财的好处，预算练习只作例子。AB01 必须接到实际生活中的结果，不能停在“练习后学会了”。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when you saved money to buy something you wanted](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20saved%20money%20to%20buy%20something%20you%20wanted.md)

论点回查：[E22](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#22-what-is-the-most-important-factor-in-learning-well)、[E27](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#27-what-helps-people-make-an-informed-decision--version-2--example) · [审计 P153](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p153)。

机制：M06 长期能力与短期便利

逻辑链：[本节 AB01 主链 / 对照](#ab01)

**串法：** 练习清楚表达需要并获得指导 → 提高沟通能力 → 入学后更容易向老师求助。

**答案：**

> Children should learn basic communication skills before they go to school.
> **Practising how to express their needs with guidance** helps them explain problems clearly and ask for help.
> For example, a child could practise telling an adult that they do not understand an instruction and asking them to explain it again.
> So, these skills can make it easier for children to communicate with their teachers.

<a id="p162"></a>

### P162 · B｜What are the differences between learning in a group and learning by yourself?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T01 学习、创造与执行 ＋ T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M06 长期能力与短期便利

题型：Q2

逻辑链：[本节 AB01 主链 / 对照](#ab01)；辅助链：[AB08](#ab08)

**串法：** 小组可互相反馈，独学要自己检查 → 反馈方式不同 → 两侧都需要主动实践。

**答案：**

> Group learning offers feedback from other people, while learning alone requires more self-review.
> **Explaining an attempt and hearing another view** can reveal problems that an independent learner has to find themselves.
> For example, classmates can comment on a presentation together, while someone studying alone might record it and review the result.
> So, both can work, but they provide feedback in different ways.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when you worked in a group](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20worked%20in%20a%20group.md) · [Describe a person who is good at learning and speaking new languages](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20person%20who%20is%20good%20at%20learning%20and%20speaking%20new%20languages.md)

论点回查：[E22](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#22-what-is-the-most-important-factor-in-learning-well)、[E35](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#35-what-does-effective-teamwork-mean-有效团队合作)、[E37](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#37-what-happens-when-responsibilities-arent-clear-职责不清的后果) · [审计 P162](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p162)。

<a id="p165"></a>

### P165 · A｜Can a child learn skills from another child?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T01 学习、创造与执行 ＋ T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M06 长期能力与短期便利

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB01 主链 / 对照](#ab01)；辅助链：[AB08](#ab08)

**串法：** 同伴示范、反馈与支持→练习改进→学会技能。

**答案：**

> Yes, children can learn skills from one another.
> **They can practise together, give simple feedback and support each other when something is difficult.**
> For example, a child who can draw a simple shape can demonstrate it and help a classmate try it.
> That said, some tasks still require guidance from an adult.
> Overall, learning with another child can be useful and practical.

<a id="p166"></a>

### P166 · A｜Is it important for everyone to have a specialized skill?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T01 学习、创造与执行 ＋ T05 经济与就业

机制：M06 长期能力与短期便利

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB01 主链 / 对照](#ab01)；辅助链：[AB12](#ab12)

**串法：** 实用专长→工作表现、提供有用服务；加并非人人需极窄专长的限定。

**答案：**

> I think a useful specialised skill is valuable, although not essential for everyone.
> **Regular practice in one area can improve their performance and help them provide a service others need.**
> For example, someone skilled at repairing bicycles can solve practical problems for local customers.
> That said, people also need skills beyond their main speciality.
> Overall, a useful specialism can be a strong advantage.

<a id="p200"></a>

### P200 · A｜How important is it for schools to help children become smarter?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M06 长期能力与短期便利

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB01 主链 / 对照](#ab01)；辅助链：[AB02](#ab02)

**串法：** 实践反馈与开放探索→实际能力和独立思考。

**答案：**

> I think schools have an important role in helping children develop their abilities.
> **Practice, useful feedback and opportunities to explore can strengthen practical skills and independent thinking.**
> For example, a teacher can let students try different solutions to a problem and discuss which ones work.
> That said, test preparation alone may not develop these abilities fully.
> Overall, schools should help children become more capable thinkers and learners.

<a id="p206"></a>

### P206 · A｜What can teachers do to stop students relying too much on AI?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T01 学习、创造与执行 ＋ T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响

机制：M06 长期能力与短期便利

题型：Q6

逻辑链：[本节 AB01 主链 / 对照](#ab01)；辅助链：[AB13](#ab13)

**串法：** 要求先独立尝试并提供反馈→保留练习→减轻依赖。

**答案：**

> Teachers can ask students to attempt tasks independently before using AI.
> **Regular practice and useful feedback help students build their own ability instead of depending on immediate answers.**
> For example, a teacher can ask students to explain their first attempt before allowing them to compare it with AI suggestions.
> So, teaching should keep independent thinking at the centre of learning.

<a id="p297"></a>

### P297 · B｜Which is more helpful, watching videos or reading books?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M06 长期能力与短期便利

题型：Q2

逻辑链：[本节 AB01 主链 / 对照](#ab01)

**串法：** 视频示范便于观察动作，书籍便于按需阅读 → 都需实践检验 → 有用程度取决于任务。

**答案：**

> Videos can be helpful for seeing a process, while books can explain something at the reader’s own pace.
> **The useful format is the one that supports practice and understanding for the task**.
> For example, a cooking learner could follow a video demonstration, while a software learner could read instructions and test each step.
> So, neither is more helpful for every kind of learning.

<a id="p298"></a>

### P298 · B｜What skills can people learn from watching videos?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M06 长期能力与短期便利

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB01 主链 / 对照](#ab01)

**串法：** 烹饪、绘画或动作示范 → 看后自己尝试并修正 → 学到实际技能。

**答案：**

> People can learn parts of skills such as cooking, drawing or playing a sport from videos.
> **Watching a demonstration gives them something to try**, but they still need practice and feedback.
> For example, someone could watch how a badminton player moves, practise the movement and review what needs improving.
> So, videos are most useful as a starting point for active practice.

<a id="ab02"></a>

## AB02 开放探索与创作 → 新想法和表达

**怎么串：** 手工、摄影、科学探索和媒介比较共用主动尝试的机制；艺术题可走表达分支。先说明怎样尝试、观察或表达，再接新视角、创造或放松，不按题目主题重复建节。

- **主链：** exposure to ideas and open-ended practice → new perspectives → greater creativity
- **反向 / 对照：** rigid instruction or fear of mistakes → less exploration → weaker creativity
- **艺术表达分支：** artistic exposure and participation → emotional expression and new ideas → creativity or relaxation

| 原题 | 等级 | 这题必须补出的桥梁 |
|---|---|---|
| [P016](#p016) | A | 尝试不同活动→探索兴趣；规律参与→健康，换成两种运动例子。 |
| [P021](#p021) | A | 把摄影作为艺术参与：表达感受、探索视角→创意与放松；无需改成记忆保存链。 |
| [P022](#p022) | B | 人物、风景和日常街景 → 用构图表达观察 → 照片类别服务不同表达。 |
| [P024](#p024) | B | 旅行接触新景象 → 选择画面表达所见 → 拍照带来创作乐趣。 |
| [P043](#p043) | A | 开放制作→表达自己的想法→创意与乐趣；换成儿童手工作品。 |
| [P044](#p044) | A | 艺术参与和开放探索→情绪表达、新视角、创造力。 |
| [P045](#p045) | B | 提供可自由创作的手工活动 → 有表达乐趣 → 部分儿童仍喜欢；不推断全国比例。 |
| [P050](#p050) | A | 建筑可用性→生活质量；艺术体验→创意与放松。 |
| [P064](#p064) | A | 艺术体验→情绪表达与共鸣→放松和积极感受。 |
| [P067](#p067) | B | 观察生命现象并亲自探索 → 有机会发现新东西 → 生物学习有趣。 |
| [P114](#p114) | A | 僵化教学、重复与压力→少探索、无聊；不认同所有儿童都如此。 |
| [P117](#p117) | A | 僵化重复→少探索与无聊；开放活动→主动参与和创意。 |
| [P139](#p139) | B | 亲自设计可表达自己的想法 → 更贴合审美与日常需求 → 愿意自己装修。 |
| [P256](#p256) | B | 电影画面与书籍文字都能引发探索 → 主动想象和尝试才产生新想法 → 不按媒介保证创造力。 |

### P016 · A｜Do you think children should learn different kinds of sports?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T01 学习、创造与执行 ＋ T02 健康、习惯与休闲

机制：M06 长期能力与短期便利

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB02 主链 / 对照](#ab02)；辅助链：[AB04](#ab04)

**串法：** 尝试不同活动→探索兴趣；规律参与→健康，换成两种运动例子。

**答案：**

> Yes, I think trying different sports is useful for children.
> **It gives them opportunities to explore their interests while developing a healthy exercise routine.**
> For example, a child can try swimming and basketball before deciding which activity they would like to practise regularly.
> That said, trying too many sports at once may be tiring.
> Overall, some variety can help children find an activity they enjoy.

### P021 · A｜Why are some people keen on taking photos?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

> Some people enjoy taking photos because it lets them express their ideas and try different ways of seeing things.
> **Choosing different subjects and angles can encourage new perspectives and creativity.**
> For example, someone might photograph the same street from several angles to show what they find interesting about it.
> So, photography can be a creative and relaxing activity.

### P043 · A｜Why do many children like to make things by hand?

题型：Q1

逻辑链：[本节 AB02 主链 / 对照](#ab02)

**串法：** 开放制作→表达自己的想法→创意与乐趣；换成儿童手工作品。

**答案：**

> Many children like making things by hand because they can explore their own ideas.
> **Activities without a single correct answer give them room to express themselves and create something different.**
> For example, children making cards can choose their own colours and drawings instead of copying one fixed design.
> So, the freedom to create can make handcraft activities enjoyable.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a person who likes to make things by hand (e.g. toys, furniture)](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20person%20who%20likes%20to%20make%20things%20by%20hand%20%28e.g.%20toys%2C%20furniture%29.md) · [Describe a person you know who really likes taking photos](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20person%20you%20know%20who%20really%20likes%20taking%20photos.md)

<a id="p044"></a>

### P044 · A｜Do you think art classes are important for children?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T08 艺术、文化与传统 ＋ T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB02 主链 / 对照](#ab02)

**串法：** 艺术参与和开放探索→情绪表达、新视角、创造力。

**答案：**

> Yes, I think art classes are important for children.
> **Artistic activities give them a way to express their feelings and look at things from different perspectives.**
> For example, children can paint the same scene in different ways and explain what they wanted to express.
> That said, overly rigid instructions can limit their creativity.
> Overall, art classes are valuable when children have room to explore.

论点回查：[E7](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#7-why-families-visit-museums)、[E25](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#25-how-should-education-promote-open-mindedness--version-3) · [审计 P044](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p044)。

<a id="p045"></a>

### P045 · B｜Do children nowadays still like doing arts and crafts?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T08 艺术、文化与传统 ＋ T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M03 自主权与责任感

**串法：** 提供可自由创作的手工活动 → 有表达乐趣 → 部分儿童仍喜欢；不推断全国比例。

**答案：**

> Yes, I think some children still enjoy arts and crafts.
> **Making choices about colours, shapes and materials** gives them a way to express their own ideas.
> For example, a child making a festival decoration can enjoy creating a design rather than copying a screen.
> That said, children have different interests.
> Overall, arts and crafts can still appeal to children who enjoy making things.

### P050 · A｜What impact can artistic buildings have on society?

> **Their value is greater when attractive design also includes features that people can use easily.**
> For example, a community library with interesting artwork and comfortable reading areas can offer both inspiration and a practical place to read.
> So, artistic buildings can improve cultural life as well as daily surroundings.

### P067 · B｜Do you think learning biology is interesting for children?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M03 自主权与责任感

题型：Q5

**串法：** 观察生命现象并亲自探索 → 有机会发现新东西 → 生物学习有趣。

**答案：**

> **Observing something and trying to explain what happens** gives them room for curiosity and discovery.
> For example, a class could grow seeds and discuss why some plants develop differently from others.
> That said, lessons based only on memorising terms may be less engaging.
> Overall, biology is more interesting when children can investigate it themselves.

### P114 · A｜Why do most children think education is boring?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T01 学习、创造与执行 ＋ T02 健康、习惯与休闲

机制：M06 长期能力与短期便利

逻辑链：[本节 AB02 主链 / 对照](#ab02)；辅助链：[AB05](#ab05)

**串法：** 僵化教学、重复与压力→少探索、无聊；不认同所有儿童都如此。

**答案：**

> I wouldn't say most children feel this way, but repetitive classes can make education seem boring.
> **Rigid instruction gives children little room to explore, and constant pressure can leave them tired.**
> For example, copying the same answers every lesson gives students fewer chances to try their own ideas.
> So, teaching can feel boring when it involves little exploration or variety.

<a id="p256"></a>

### P256 · B｜Are movies more likely to help people become more creative than books?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T01 学习、创造与执行 ＋ T08 艺术、文化与传统

机制：M03 自主权与责任感

题型：Q2

逻辑链：[本节 AB02 主链 / 对照](#ab02)

**串法：** 电影画面与书籍文字都能引发探索 → 主动想象和尝试才产生新想法 → 不按媒介保证创造力。

**答案：**

> I don’t think movies automatically make people more creative than books.
> **Exploring ideas actively** matters more than simply choosing one format over another.
> For example, a viewer could sketch a new scene, while a reader could imagine a different ending to a novel.
> So, either format can encourage creativity when people do something with the ideas.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

<a id="ab03"></a>

## AB03 明确优先级与现实步骤 → 计划可执行

**怎么串：** 日常、学习、储蓄和旅行共用这一条链。把抽象计划换成具体的任务顺序、时间余量或备用活动。

- **主链：** clear priorities and realistic sequencing → actionable plan → lower risk and better results
- **反向 / 对照：** unclear priorities or rigid scheduling → impractical plan → delays or poor results

| 原题 | 等级 | 这题必须补出的桥梁 |
|---|---|---|
| [P026](#p026) | A | 明确优先级与顺序→可执行计划→少延误、结果更好。 |
| [P027](#p027) | A | 儿童用简单任务清单练组织→行动更清晰；加适龄限定即可。 |
| [P028](#p028) | A | 父母帮助拆任务并反馈→可执行安排与组织能力。 |
| [P029](#p029) | A | 合理规划→减少延误→改善学业或工作结果；不承诺必然成功。 |
| [P030](#p030) | A | 优先级与明确分工→协调顺畅→团队结果更好。 |
| [P031](#p031) | A | 排序重要任务、拆步骤、留灵活性→计划可执行。 |
| [P073](#p073) | B | 取得某领域成功者往往把目标转成具体步骤和持续努力 → 更有进展；不是给所有普通人贴标签。 |
| [P128](#p128) | B | 时长能容纳主要活动和休息 → 行程可执行 → 一周可作个人建议但非统一标准。 |
| [P129](#p129) | A | 排序预算、时间与目的地→安排步骤→可行旅程。 |
| [P130](#p130) | A | 提前明确优先级与顺序→减少延误→行程更顺利。 |
| [P132](#p132) | B | 核对假期、预算和旅途用时 → 安排真实可行的窗口 → 决定何时出行。 |
| [P147](#p147) | B | 海外交通和体验需要时间 → 安排一周到十天有余地 → 按目的地和假期调整。 |
| [P149](#p149) | A | 现实日程→执行顺畅；均衡活动→减压与专注。 |
| [P151](#p151) | A | 明确优先级并安排运动休息→可执行、健康的日程。 |
| [P152](#p152) | A | 过度僵化、重复与压力→无聊疲惫→不喜欢固定日程。 |
| [P156](#p156) | B | 花钱可一次完成，储蓄需持续安排 → 长期执行更费努力 → 通常花钱更容易。 |
| [P157](#p157) | B | 课程、设备或其他大额需要 → 设定目标并分期留钱 → 让购买可承担。 |
| [P191](#p191) | B | 饮食、任务顺序和休息安排 → 比较当天需要 → 作日常小选择。 |
| [P198](#p198) | A | 现实排序把工作、运动和休息纳入日程→计划可执行；用谨慎表述。 |
| [P207](#p207) | B | 其他任务或个人问题更紧急 → 先处理这些事 → 回复被延后。 |
| [P220](#p220) | B | 早班、出行或特定活动需要时间 → 把准备安排在前 → 早起避免错过。 |
| [P221](#p221) | B | 乘车、考试等有固定开始时间 → 预留准备余地 → 降低误点风险。 |
| [P223](#p223) | B | 早到可留余地，但私人拜访有对方安排 → 时间应适度 → 不是任何场合越早越好。 |
| [P239](#p239) | B | 天气或日程改变 → 原计划不再可行 → 有时需要调整。 |
| [P242](#p242) | B | 日历提醒或时间信息 → 减少遗漏并校对步骤 → 计划更易执行。 |
| [P243](#p243) | B | 学习、工作和旅行安排 → 确定先后与时间 → 日常计划类别。 |
| [P246](#p246) | B | 天气、任务冲突或延误 → 原安排无法执行 → 调整顺序或活动。 |
| [P247](#p247) | A | 优先级清晰、顺序现实、适度灵活→可执行计划。 |
| [P271](#p271) | B | 学习、职业或独立项目等愿望 → 转成分步目标 → 列举目标并说明可执行性。 |
| [P272](#p272) | A | 鼓励并帮助确定步骤、持续反馈→练习与成长；不需推入全新成就动机论。 |
| [P277](#p277) | A | 现实计划、规律练习和反馈→目标进展。 |

<a id="p026"></a>

### P026 · A｜Why is it important for people to be organized?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M02 时间与精力成本

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB03 主链 / 对照](#ab03)

**串法：** 明确优先级与顺序→可执行计划→少延误、结果更好。

**答案：**

> Being organised is important because it makes plans easier to carry out.
> **Clear priorities and realistic steps help people avoid delays and focus their efforts.**
> For example, a student can divide a project into research, writing and checking instead of leaving everything until the last evening.
> That said, a timetable that is too rigid can become impractical.
> Overall, good organisation helps people turn plans into results.

<a id="p027"></a>

### P027 · A｜Do you think children should learn to be organized from a young age?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M06 长期能力与短期便利

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB03 主链 / 对照](#ab03)；辅助链：[AB01](#ab01)

**串法：** 儿童用简单任务清单练组织→行动更清晰；加适龄限定即可。

**答案：**

> Yes, I think children should start learning simple organisational habits early.
> **Planning small tasks gives them practice in deciding what to do first and following realistic steps.**
> For example, a child can make a short checklist for packing their school bag and check it before leaving home.
> That said, young children still need some help with planning.
> Overall, simple daily tasks are a useful starting point.

<a id="p028"></a>

### P028 · A｜Should parents teach their children to be well-organized?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M06 长期能力与短期便利

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB03 主链 / 对照](#ab03)；辅助链：[AB01](#ab01)

**串法：** 父母帮助拆任务并反馈→可执行安排与组织能力。

**答案：**

> Yes, parents should help children learn to organise their daily tasks.
> **They can show them how to set priorities and give feedback as they practise following a plan.**
> For example, a parent can help a child divide a homework task into smaller steps and check how it went afterwards.
> That said, parents should avoid making every small decision for them.
> Overall, guidance and practice can help children become more organised.

<a id="p029"></a>

### P029 · A｜Are organized people more likely to be successful at work or in their studies?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M02 时间与精力成本

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB03 主链 / 对照](#ab03)

**串法：** 合理规划→减少延误→改善学业或工作结果；不承诺必然成功。

**答案：**

> I think organised people often have a better chance of doing well.
> **Clear priorities help them use realistic steps and avoid unnecessary delays in their work or studies.**
> For example, a student who schedules time for research and revision is less likely to rush an assignment at the end.
> That said, being organised does not guarantee success.
> Overall, organisation can improve people's chances of getting good results.

<a id="p030"></a>

### P030 · A｜Do you think it is important for managers to be well-organized?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T01 学习、创造与执行 ＋ T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB03 主链 / 对照](#ab03)；辅助链：[AB08](#ab08)

**串法：** 优先级与明确分工→协调顺畅→团队结果更好。

**答案：**

> Yes, managers need to be organised because their plans affect the whole team.
> **Clear priorities and responsibilities help everyone coordinate their work and avoid confusion.**
> For example, a manager can divide a project into smaller tasks and make sure each person knows what to complete first.
> That said, managers also need to adjust plans when circumstances change.
> Overall, good organisation makes a team easier to manage.

<a id="p031"></a>

### P031 · A｜What can people do to become more organized?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M02 时间与精力成本

题型：Q6

逻辑链：[本节 AB03 主链 / 对照](#ab03)

**串法：** 排序重要任务、拆步骤、留灵活性→计划可执行。

**答案：**

> People can become more organised by ranking their priorities and breaking large tasks into smaller steps.
> **A realistic sequence makes a plan easier to follow and reduces the chance of delays.**
> For example, someone preparing a presentation can schedule separate time for research, slides and a practice run.
> So, the key is to make a clear plan that is manageable.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

<a id="p073"></a>

### P073 · B｜What's the difference between ordinary people and successful people?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M02 时间与精力成本

题型：Q2

逻辑链：[本节 AB03 主链 / 对照](#ab03)；辅助链：[AB01](#ab01)

**串法：** 取得某领域成功者往往把目标转成具体步骤和持续努力 → 更有进展；不是给所有普通人贴标签。

**答案：**

> People who achieve a particular goal may be more consistent in turning it into practical steps.
> **Clear priorities and regular work** can separate steady progress from an ambition that never gets started.
> For example, two people may want to start a business, but the one who researches customer needs and tests a product has a clearer route forward.
> So, I’d compare their habits rather than divide people into permanently successful and ordinary groups.

**学习提示：** 比较习惯与特定目标；不把普通人定义为懒惰，也不保证规划必然成功。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a well-organized person you know](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20well-organized%20person%20you%20know.md) · [Describe an important decision that you made](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20an%20important%20decision%20that%20you%20made.md)

论点回查：[E22](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#22-what-is-the-most-important-factor-in-learning-well)、[E26](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#26-what-helps-people-make-an-informed-decision--version-1) · [审计 P073](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p073)。

<a id="p128"></a>

### P128 · B｜What is the ideal length for a holiday?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T01 学习、创造与执行 ＋ T02 健康、习惯与休闲

机制：M02 时间与精力成本

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB03 主链 / 对照](#ab03)

**串法：** 时长能容纳主要活动和休息 → 行程可执行 → 一周可作个人建议但非统一标准。

**答案：**

> For a proper break, I think about a week can be a reasonable length.
> It allows **a few main activities with time left to rest**, instead of rushing through every day.
> For example, on a week-long beach holiday, travellers can spend a few days sightseeing and still have time to relax by the sea.
> That said, the ideal length also depends on work and travel time.
> Overall, the holiday should be long enough for a manageable pace.

**学习提示：** 使用一般旅客的假设例子，不讲个人经历；一周只是建议，并非统一标准。

**串法：** 排序预算、时间与目的地→安排步骤→可行旅程。

**答案：**

> People often plan holidays by deciding what matters most and arranging realistic steps.
> **They can compare their available time and budget before choosing activities that fit the trip.**
> For example, someone with a short weekend can plan one main activity each day instead of creating an overcrowded schedule.
> So, useful holiday planning starts with clear priorities.

<a id="p130"></a>

### P130 · A｜Is it important to plan a holiday ahead?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M02 时间与精力成本

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB03 主链 / 对照](#ab03)

**串法：** 提前明确优先级与顺序→减少延误→行程更顺利。

**答案：**

> Yes, planning a holiday ahead can make the trip easier to manage.
> **Realistic priorities and a clear sequence reduce the risk of delays and impractical arrangements.**
> For example, a family can check travel times before deciding how many places to visit in one day.
> That said, leaving some free time allows plans to stay flexible.
> Overall, advance planning is useful without needing to control every minute.

> So, the timing should fit both the person’s schedule and the trip.

### P147 · B｜How much time do you think people should spend on a trip abroad?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T01 学习、创造与执行 ＋ T02 健康、习惯与休闲

机制：M02 时间与精力成本

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB03 主链 / 对照](#ab03)

**串法：** 海外交通和体验需要时间 → 安排一周到十天有余地 → 按目的地和假期调整。

**答案：**

> For a trip with several activities, I’d suggest roughly a week to ten days.
> **Allowing enough time for travel and rest** makes the schedule easier to manage.
> For example, our ten-day Malaysia trip included about a week around Semporna rather than changing places every day.
> That said, a nearby destination may need much less time.
> Overall, the length should allow a comfortable pace within the traveller’s available leave.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

> **A balanced schedule can also lower stress and improve concentration.**

> So, a sensible routine can support both daily organisation and well-being.

<a id="p151"></a>

### P151 · A｜How can people establish a useful daily routine?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

机制：M02 时间与精力成本

逻辑链：[本节 AB03 主链 / 对照](#ab03)；辅助链：[AB05](#ab05)

**串法：** 明确优先级并安排运动休息→可执行、健康的日程。

**答案：**

> People can establish a useful routine by setting priorities and including both necessary tasks and time to relax.
> **Realistic steps make the schedule easier to follow, while meaningful activities help reduce stress.**
> For example, someone can plan a short walk after finishing work and leave time to prepare for the next day.
> So, a good routine should be practical and balanced.

<a id="p152"></a>

### P152 · A｜Why do some people dislike following routines?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T01 学习、创造与执行 ＋ T02 健康、习惯与休闲

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q1

逻辑链：[本节 AB03 主链 / 对照](#ab03)；辅助链：[AB05](#ab05)

**串法：** 过度僵化、重复与压力→无聊疲惫→不喜欢固定日程。

**答案：**

> Some people dislike routines because they find them too rigid or repetitive.
> **Following the same demanding schedule can make them feel bored, tired or unable to adjust their plans.**
> For example, someone with every evening planned in detail may struggle to make time for an unexpected family visit.
> So, routines can become unattractive when they leave little flexibility.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

### P156 · B｜Which one do you think is easier, saving money or spending money?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T01 学习、创造与执行 ＋ T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响

机制：M02 时间与精力成本

题型：Q2

逻辑链：[本节 AB03 主链 / 对照](#ab03)

**串法：** 花钱可一次完成，储蓄需持续安排 → 长期执行更费努力 → 通常花钱更容易。

**答案：**

> Spending money is usually easier because a purchase can be completed quickly, while saving requires a continuing plan.
> **Setting an amount aside repeatedly** takes more organisation than deciding to buy something once.
> For example, buying a laptop could happen in an afternoon, but I saved towards mine over several months.
> So, saving generally requires a more sustained effort.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

> People may save for useful equipment, education or a larger personal goal.
> **Having a clear purpose and a realistic budget** helps them turn that aim into manageable steps.
> For example, I saved a regular amount towards a laptop for courses and small software projects.
> So, saving helps people afford larger purchases without paying for everything immediately.

### P198 · A｜Do people often make plans around their regular routines?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

> **Fitting new tasks around existing priorities makes a schedule more realistic and manageable.**
> For example, someone can plan a language practice session after their usual evening meal rather than during working hours.
> So, regular routines can provide a practical framework for planning.

### P207 · B｜In what situations do people spend a long time responding to others' messages?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

**答案：**

> People may take a long time to respond when a demanding task or personal problem needs their attention first.
> **Competing priorities can push a message down the list**, even when the sender considers it important.
> For example, Stella once replied late to a project message because she was dealing with a personal issue.
> So, a delayed reply can reflect other pressures rather than a lack of interest.

**答案：**

> People may get up early because their plans require an early start.
> **Leaving enough time to get ready and travel** helps them avoid missing an activity.
> For example, Stella and I got up before dawn to catch a boat for a sunrise trip near Semporna.
> So, an early start can be a practical response to the day’s schedule.

### P221 · B｜What kinds of occasions need people to arrive early?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M02 时间与精力成本

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB03 主链 / 对照](#ab03)

**串法：** 乘车、考试等有固定开始时间 → 预留准备余地 → 降低误点风险。

**答案：**

> People may need to arrive early for trains, exams or organised trips.
> **A little spare time allows for preparation and unexpected delays**.
> For example, reaching a busy railway station early gives travellers time to find the right platform before boarding.
> So, arriving early is useful when missing a fixed start would cause problems.

<a id="p223"></a>

### P223 · B｜Is it good to arrive early in any situation?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T01 学习、创造与执行 ＋ T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M02 时间与精力成本

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB03 主链 / 对照](#ab03)

**串法：** 早到可留余地，但私人拜访有对方安排 → 时间应适度 → 不是任何场合越早越好。

**答案：**

> Arriving early can be useful, but it isn’t the best choice in every situation.
> **Extra time can prevent delays**, while arriving far ahead of a private invitation may interrupt the host’s preparations.
> For example, arriving early for a train is sensible, but visiting a friend an hour ahead of the invitation may be awkward.
> So, the right arrival time depends on the occasion.

**学习提示：** 两面已包含，不硬加第三面；私人拜访为直接限制的常识性对照。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a noisy place you have been to](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20noisy%20place%20you%20have%20been%20to.md) · [Describe a home that you like to visit but do not want to live in](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20home%20that%20you%20like%20to%20visit%20but%20do%20not%20want%20to%20live%20in.md)

### P239 · B｜Do people often change their plans?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M02 时间与精力成本

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB03 主链 / 对照](#ab03)

**串法：** 天气或日程改变 → 原计划不再可行 → 有时需要调整。

**答案：**

> People do change plans when circumstances make the original arrangement impractical.
> **Adjusting the steps to fit what is possible** can prevent a disappointing or unworkable day.
> For example, we replaced an island trip with a market visit when strong wind led to our boat being cancelled.
> So, changes are a normal response when the situation no longer fits the plan.

**学习提示：** 频率采用有限判断，例子不证明全国“经常”的比例。

### P242 · B｜How does technology help people make plans?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T01 学习、创造与执行 ＋ T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响

逻辑链：[本节 AB03 主链 / 对照](#ab03)

**串法：** 日历提醒或时间信息 → 减少遗漏并校对步骤 → 计划更易执行。

**答案：**

> **Keeping priorities and reminders in one place** can make a plan easier to follow.
> For example, someone could put their three main study tasks into a calendar and leave gaps for work that takes longer.
> So, digital tools are useful when they support a realistic schedule.

### P246 · B｜What are the common reasons when people need to change plans?

主题：T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M02 时间与精力成本

> So, people often change plans because something important has changed around them.

<a id="p247"></a>

### P247 · A｜What kind of plans are practical?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M02 时间与精力成本

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB03 主链 / 对照](#ab03)

**串法：** 优先级清晰、顺序现实、适度灵活→可执行计划。

**答案：**

> Practical plans have clear priorities, realistic steps and enough flexibility.
> **They help people know what to do next without forcing them to follow an impossible schedule.**
> For example, a study plan can focus on one weak area each evening instead of trying to cover everything in one night.
> So, a practical plan is one that people can actually carry out.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a way or change that helps you save a lot of time](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20way%20or%20change%20that%20helps%20you%20save%20a%20lot%20of%20time.md) · [Describe a plan that you had to change recently](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20plan%20that%20you%20had%20to%20change%20recently.md)

<a id="p271"></a>

### P271 · B｜What kinds of ambitions do people have?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M02 时间与精力成本

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB03 主链 / 对照](#ab03)

**串法：** 学习、职业或独立项目等愿望 → 转成分步目标 → 列举目标并说明可执行性。

**答案：**

> People can have ambitions related to learning, careers or creating something of their own.
> **A broad aim becomes more useful when it is broken into manageable steps**.
> For example, becoming an independent software consultant can involve learning new tools and completing a few small projects first.
> So, ambitions range widely, but they need practical steps to move forward.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a long-term goal or ambition you would like to achieve](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20long-term%20goal%20or%20ambition%20you%20would%20like%20to%20achieve.md)

论点回查：[E22](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#22-what-is-the-most-important-factor-in-learning-well)、[E27](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#27-what-helps-people-make-an-informed-decision--version-2--example) · [审计 P271](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p271)。

<a id="p272"></a>

### P272 · A｜Why should parents encourage children to have ambitions?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M06 长期能力与短期便利

题型：Q1

逻辑链：[本节 AB03 主链 / 对照](#ab03)；辅助链：[AB01](#ab01)

**串法：** 鼓励并帮助确定步骤、持续反馈→练习与成长；不需推入全新成就动机论。

**答案：**

> Parents should encourage ambitions because a clear goal can give children something practical to work towards.
> **Breaking that goal into realistic steps makes it easier to practise regularly and improve.**
> For example, a child hoping to play a song can plan short practice sessions and get useful feedback from a teacher.
> So, encouragement can help children turn an ambition into steady effort.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a way or change that helps you save a lot of time](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20way%20or%20change%20that%20helps%20you%20save%20a%20lot%20of%20time.md) · [Describe a plan that you had to change recently](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20plan%20that%20you%20had%20to%20change%20recently.md)

论点回查：[E22](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#22-what-is-the-most-important-factor-in-learning-well)、[E26](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#26-what-helps-people-make-an-informed-decision--version-1)、[E30](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#30-example-of-active-listening-积极倾听的例子) · [审计 P272](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p272)。

<a id="p277"></a>

### P277 · A｜What should people do to achieve their goals?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M06 长期能力与短期便利

题型：Q6

逻辑链：[本节 AB03 主链 / 对照](#ab03)；辅助链：[AB01](#ab01)

**串法：** 现实计划、规律练习和反馈→目标进展。

**答案：**

> People should set clear priorities, plan realistic steps and practise the skills their goals require.
> **Feedback helps them adjust their efforts and make progress instead of following an impractical plan.**
> For example, someone aiming to improve their English can practise short answers regularly and work on one recurring mistake at a time.
> So, achieving goals takes a workable plan and consistent effort.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a way or change that helps you save a lot of time](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20way%20or%20change%20that%20helps%20you%20save%20a%20lot%20of%20time.md) · [Describe a plan that you had to change recently](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20plan%20that%20you%20had%20to%20change%20recently.md)

论点回查：[E22](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#22-what-is-the-most-important-factor-in-learning-well)、[E26](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#26-what-helps-people-make-an-informed-decision--version-1)、[E27](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#27-what-helps-people-make-an-informed-decision--version-2--example) · [审计 P277](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p277)。

<a id="ab04"></a>

## AB04 健康习惯与压力管理 → 身心状态

**怎么串：** 解释习惯或压力怎样影响状态。使用既有工作疲惫和休息素材，不把个案缓解写成治疗保证。

- **主链：** healthy habits and early support → fewer serious problems → better long-term well-being
- **反向 / 对照：** inactivity or chronic pressure → poor sleep and anxiety → illness or burnout

| 原题 | 等级 | 这题必须补出的桥梁 |
|---|---|---|
| [P002](#p002) | A | 持续压力→睡眠与情绪问题→较差健康；换成工作压力例子即可。 |
| [P009](#p009) | A | 规律运动→健康、减压与专注；场景换成学校体育课。 |
| [P066](#p066) | B | 持续学习和工作责任 → 有较大压力与疲惫可能 → 医生工作有难度。 |
| [P104](#p104) | B | 持续噪声干扰休息或专注 → 更疲惫和烦躁 → 日常任务变难。 |

<a id="p002"></a>

### P002 · A｜What effect does stress have on health?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T02 健康、习惯与休闲

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q1

逻辑链：[本节 AB04 主链 / 对照](#ab04)

**串法：** 持续压力→睡眠与情绪问题→较差健康；换成工作压力例子即可。

**答案：**

> Constant stress can harm both physical and mental health.
> **People under pressure may sleep poorly and feel anxious, leaving them tired and unable to concentrate.**
> For example, someone worrying about work every night may struggle to sleep and feel exhausted the next morning.
> So, ongoing stress can seriously affect people's well-being.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a person whose health and fitness you have helped to improve](🧡🥕%20Describe%20a%20person%20whose%20health%20and%20fitness%20you%20have%20helped%20to%20improve.md) · [Describe a person who met difficulties but succeeded](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20person%20who%20met%20difficulties%20but%20succeeded.md)

论点回查：[E39](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#3940-balanced-routines-and-health-均衡作息与健康)、[E40](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#3940-balanced-routines-and-health-均衡作息与健康)、[E50](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#5051-constant-pressure-and-well-being-持续压力与身心健康)、[E51](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#5051-constant-pressure-and-well-being-持续压力与身心健康) · [审计 P002](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p002)。

<a id="p009"></a>

### P009 · A｜Should students have physical education and do sports at school?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T02 健康、习惯与休闲

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB04 主链 / 对照](#ab04)；辅助链：[AB05](#ab05)

**串法：** 规律运动→健康、减压与专注；场景换成学校体育课。

**答案：**

> Yes, I think students should have PE lessons and regular opportunities to exercise.
> **Healthy routines can reduce stress and help students feel better and concentrate more easily.**
> For example, a short basketball session can give students a break from studying and help them return to class feeling refreshed.
> That said, school sports should suit students' different abilities.
> Overall, physical activity is a useful part of school life.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

论点回查：[E39](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#3940-balanced-routines-and-health-均衡作息与健康)、[E40](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#3940-balanced-routines-and-health-均衡作息与健康)、[E46](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#4649-exercise-mood-and-concentration-运动情绪与专注力) · [审计 P009](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p009)。

<a id="p066"></a>

### P066 · B｜Do you think being a doctor is easy or difficult?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T02 健康、习惯与休闲 ＋ T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB04 主链 / 对照](#ab04)

**串法：** 持续学习和工作责任 → 有较大压力与疲惫可能 → 医生工作有难度。

**答案：**

> I think being a doctor can be difficult.
> **Demanding work and continuing learning** can create pressure and leave little room to recover during a busy period.
> For example, a doctor dealing with a full day of appointments may finish work feeling mentally exhausted.
> That said, a manageable routine and support can make the work less stressful.
> Overall, the demands of the job can make it challenging.

**学习提示：** 只谈日常工作压力，不作诊疗或专业医学判断。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a person you know who would like to choose a career in the medical field (e.g. a doctor, a nurse)](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%20Describe%20a%20person%20you%20know%20who%20would%20like%20to%20choose%20a%20career%20in%20the%20medical%20field%20%28e.g.%20a%20doctor%2C%20a%20nurse%29.md) · [Describe a person who met difficulties but succeeded](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20person%20who%20met%20difficulties%20but%20succeeded.md)

<a id="p104"></a>

### P104 · B｜How can noise affect people's daily lives?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T02 健康、习惯与休闲

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q1

逻辑链：[本节 AB04 主链 / 对照](#ab04)

**串法：** 持续噪声干扰休息或专注 → 更疲惫和烦躁 → 日常任务变难。

**答案：**

> Noise can make everyday life more tiring by interrupting rest and concentration.
> **Repeated sounds can keep people tense**, especially when they are already exhausted.
> For example, announcements and loud conversations in a busy railway station may make a tired traveller feel more overwhelmed.
> So, noise can add strain to otherwise ordinary daily activities.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a noisy place you have been to](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20noisy%20place%20you%20have%20been%20to.md)

论点回查：[E39](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#3940-balanced-routines-and-health-均衡作息与健康)、[E40](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#3940-balanced-routines-and-health-均衡作息与健康)、[E50](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#5051-constant-pressure-and-well-being-持续压力与身心健康)、[E51](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#5051-constant-pressure-and-well-being-持续压力与身心健康) · [审计 P104](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p104)。

<a id="ab05"></a>

## AB05 有意义的日常活动 → 放松与专注

**怎么串：** 散步、种植、运动和安静环境要通过实际活动接到放松；不能直接用“有益”证明哪项最流行。

- **主链：** balanced routines and meaningful activities → lower stress → better concentration and mood
- **反向 / 对照：** repetition, inactivity or constant pressure → boredom and fatigue → poorer well-being

| 原题 | 等级 | 这题必须补出的桥梁 |
|---|---|---|
| [P013](#p013) | B | 本人圈子中的羽毛球兴趣 → 主动休闲和共同活动 → 只说明熟悉的流行运动。 |
| [P015](#p015) | B | 羽毛球与骑行来自既有经历 → 活动给人休闲与相处机会 → 用熟悉例子而不做全国排名。 |
| [P081](#p081) | A | 种植作为有意义活动→减压、情绪改善；可加共同照料→联系。 |
| [P082](#p082) | B | 母亲在家种菜 → 日常照料提供放松活动 → 用真实素材例子，数量保持未知。 |
| [P084](#p084) | A | 有意义的日常种植活动→减压、放松与情绪改善。 |
| [P085](#p085) | B | 自种有照料与观察的过程，直接购买没有 → 获得休闲体验 → 即使能买也愿意自己种。 |
| [P101](#p101) | A | 减少压力、改善专注→偏好安静环境；用学习或休息例子。 |
| [P103](#p103) | B | 安静环境减少干扰 → 更容易专注 → 可回顾感受与检查目标。 |
| [P107](#p107) | A | 自然场所提供放松和活动→减压、改善情绪。 |
| [P116](#p116) | A | 参加运动或有意义的社交活动→減压、改善情绪。 |
| [P150](#p150) | A | 均衡作息有益，僵化重复或压力使人疲惫；已有两面。 |
| [P195](#p195) | A | 改变过度重复、增加有意义活动→减压和情绪改善。 |
| [P219](#p219) | B | Stella 愿为清晨摄影早起 → 放慢节奏观察光线 → 享受有意义的早晨活动。 |
| [P224](#p224) | B | 晨练者或喜欢安静活动的人 → 早晨有适合活动的时间 → 愿意早起。 |
| [P225](#p225) | B | 睡眠与日常安排适合 → 早晨安静活动有益；缺觉时未必舒服 → 不统一称最佳。 |

<a id="p081"></a>

### P081 · A｜What are the advantages of growing vegetables or flowers at home?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T02 健康、习惯与休闲 ＋ T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB05 主链 / 对照](#ab05)；辅助链：[AB07](#ab07)

**串法：** 种植作为有意义活动→减压、情绪改善；可加共同照料→联系。

**答案：**

> Growing vegetables or flowers can give people a meaningful activity at home.
> **Taking a break from work to care for plants can help them relax and improve their mood.**
> For example, family members can spend a little time watering plants together and chatting about their day.
> That said, plant care should fit comfortably into their routine.
> Overall, gardening can offer relaxation and shared time at home.

> For example, she grows spring onions, lettuce and tomatoes in containers on her balcony in Zhanjiang.
> So, home growing is a familiar leisure activity in my family.

<a id="ab06"></a>

## AB06 清楚表达与倾听 → 理解和信任

**怎么串：** 学校、商店和朋友交流共用减少误解的机制。判断题给可观察的倾听与回应，不泛说态度友好。

- **主链：** clear expression and active listening → fewer misunderstandings → stronger trust
- **反向 / 对照：** unclear or impersonal communication → uncertainty → weaker relationships

| 原题 | 等级 | 这题必须补出的桥梁 |
|---|---|---|
| [P003](#p003) | A | 倾听、支持同学→信任与亲近→受欢迎；用帮助同学的普通例子。 |
| [P004](#p004) | A | 学生信任老师→更愿沟通；配合反馈促进学习，限定受欢迎并非唯一标准。 |
| [P062](#p062) | B | 发现行为变化 → 温和询问并倾听 → 了解感受；外表不能直接确定情绪。 |
| [P076](#p076) | A | 语言技能与清楚沟通→减少误解、改善工作表现。 |
| [P097](#p097) | B | 朋友可能有共同工作背景，家人掌握长期个人情况 → 话题与所需解释不同 → 沟通方式随关系变化。 |
| [P159](#p159) | A | 不倾听→误解与不被理解→关系受损。 |
| [P160](#p160) | B | 复述与相关追问 → 表明理解了意思 → 比仅看眼神更能判断倾听。 |
| [P168](#p168) | B | 小店可能便于直接交流，但选择和设施可能有限 → 服务体验与实际需求两面。 |
| [P174](#p174) | A | 倾听与清楚解释→解决关切、建立信任；换顾客场景。 |
| [P175](#p175) | A | 服务看实际倾听与解决关切；价格不是充分证据，可核实评价。 |
| [P176](#p176) | A | 及时回应→减少顾客不确定与误解→维持信任。 |
| [P192](#p192) | A | 倾听分歧、表达理由、协调分工→共同决定。 |
| [P209](#p209) | A | 礼貌追问、清楚说明急迫程度→减少误解；不用假定对方故意忽视。 |
| [P212](#p212) | A | 没有回应→不确定/觉得不被理解→关系距离与负面情绪。 |
| [P214](#p214) | A | 倾听支持、明确分工→信任与稳定合作→愿意跟随。 |
| [P230](#p230) | A | 选择愿意倾听和提供支持的人→理解与信任；可列朋友或家人。 |
| [P240](#p240) | A | 及时清楚说明变化→少误解→便于共同协调。 |
| [P245](#p245) | A | 清楚解释原因、更新安排→减少误解；用改见面时间例子。 |
| [P250](#p250) | A | 找有经验且愿倾听的人，再核实信息→较好决定。 |
| [P292](#p292) | B | 认真倾听、清楚回应和尊重他人 → 减少误解 → 建立信任。 |

<a id="p003"></a>

### P003 · A｜Why are some students popular in school?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q1

逻辑链：[本节 AB06 主链 / 对照](#ab06)；辅助链：[AB07](#ab07)

**串法：** 倾听、支持同学→信任与亲近→受欢迎；用帮助同学的普通例子。

**答案：**

> Some students are popular because they listen to others and offer support.
> **When classmates feel understood and know they can ask for help, they develop greater trust in that person.**
> For example, a student who listens to a worried classmate and helps them with homework can build a strong friendship.
> So, being approachable and supportive can make students well liked.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when a person did something to help you solve a problem](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20a%20person%20did%20something%20to%20help%20you%20solve%20a%20problem.md) · [Describe a time when you sent a message or an email to someone but received no reply for a long time](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20sent%20a%20message%20or%20an%20email%20to%20someone%20but%20received%20no%20reply%20for%20a%20long%20time.md)

论点回查：[E29](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#29-what-does-effective-communication-involve-有效沟通)、[E30](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#30-example-of-active-listening-积极倾听的例子)、[E34](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#34-why-real-interaction-builds-relationships-真实互动如何建立关系) · [审计 P003](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p003)。

<a id="p004"></a>

### P004 · A｜Is it important for a teacher to be popular?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作 ＋ T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB06 主链 / 对照](#ab06)；辅助链：[AB01](#ab01)

**串法：** 学生信任老师→更愿沟通；配合反馈促进学习，限定受欢迎并非唯一标准。

**答案：**

> I think being well liked can help a teacher, especially when it reflects a good relationship with students.
> **Clear explanations and careful listening can build trust, making it easier for students to discuss their difficulties.**
> For example, a student may ask an approachable teacher to explain a mistake and then practise correcting it.
> That said, being entertaining alone does not make someone a good teacher.
> Overall, popularity is useful when it supports effective teaching.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when a person did something to help you solve a problem](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20a%20person%20did%20something%20to%20help%20you%20solve%20a%20problem.md) · [Describe a time when you sent a message or an email to someone but received no reply for a long time](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20sent%20a%20message%20or%20an%20email%20to%20someone%20but%20received%20no%20reply%20for%20a%20long%20time.md)

论点回查：[E29](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#29-what-does-effective-communication-involve-有效沟通)、[E30](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#30-example-of-active-listening-积极倾听的例子)、[E22](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#22-what-is-the-most-important-factor-in-learning-well) · [审计 P004](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p004)。

<a id="p062"></a>

### P062 · B｜Can teachers tell when a child is unhappy?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB06 主链 / 对照](#ab06)

**串法：** 发现行为变化 → 温和询问并倾听 → 了解感受；外表不能直接确定情绪。

**答案：**

> Teachers can sometimes notice that a child seems unhappy, but they can’t always be sure.
> **A change in behaviour can prompt a gentle conversation**, and listening helps the teacher understand what is happening.
> For example, a usually talkative child who becomes quiet may need someone to ask how they are feeling.
> That said, quiet behaviour alone doesn’t prove that a child is unhappy.
> Overall, noticing changes and listening are more useful than making assumptions.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when a person did something to help you solve a problem](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20a%20person%20did%20something%20to%20help%20you%20solve%20a%20problem.md)

论点回查：[E29](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#29-what-does-effective-communication-involve-有效沟通)、[E30](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#30-example-of-active-listening-积极倾听的例子)、[E32](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#32-example-of-poor-listening-缺乏倾听的例子) · [审计 P062](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p062)。

<a id="p076"></a>

### P076 · A｜Does speaking other languages help at work?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作 ＋ T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB06 主链 / 对照](#ab06)；辅助链：[AB01](#ab01)

**串法：** 语言技能与清楚沟通→减少误解、改善工作表现。

**答案：**

> Yes, speaking another language can be useful at work.
> **It helps people communicate clearly with others who use that language and reduces misunderstandings.**
> For example, an employee can explain a customer's problem directly instead of struggling to understand the request.
> That said, its usefulness depends on the work someone does.
> Overall, language skills can make workplace communication easier.

<a id="p097"></a>

### P097 · B｜What's the difference between talking to friends and family?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作 ＋ T09 年龄与代际

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q2

逻辑链：[本节 AB06 主链 / 对照](#ab06)

**串法：** 朋友可能有共同工作背景，家人掌握长期个人情况 → 话题与所需解释不同 → 沟通方式随关系变化。

**答案：**

> Talking to friends and family can involve different topics because the shared background is different.
> **A friend from work may understand a project immediately**, while a relative may need more explanation but know my personal worries well.
> For example, I could discuss a software deadline with Stella and explain the pressure more generally when talking to my mother.
> So, I adjust the conversation to what the other person already understands.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when a person did something to help you solve a problem](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20a%20person%20did%20something%20to%20help%20you%20solve%20a%20problem.md) · [Describe a person who taught you a new skill](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20person%20who%20taught%20you%20a%20new%20skill.md)

<a id="p159"></a>

### P159 · A｜What might happen if someone does not listen carefully during a conversation?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q1

逻辑链：[本节 AB06 主链 / 对照](#ab06)；辅助链：[AB07](#ab07)

**串法：** 不倾听→误解与不被理解→关系受损。

**答案：**

> Poor listening can lead to misunderstandings and make the other person feel ignored.
> **When someone gives a response without understanding the problem, trust can become weaker.**
> For example, a friend may offer advice about studying when the speaker is actually worried about a family issue.
> So, not listening carefully can damage the quality of a relationship.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

<a id="p160"></a>

### P160 · B｜How can we tell whether someone is listening attentively?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB06 主链 / 对照](#ab06)

**串法：** 复述与相关追问 → 表明理解了意思 → 比仅看眼神更能判断倾听。

**答案：**

> Useful signs include asking relevant questions and checking that they understood the main point.
> **A response that fits what was actually said** suggests the listener is following the conversation.
> For example, someone hearing about work pressure might ask which deadline is causing the problem instead of changing the subject.
> So, I’d judge attention by the quality of the response, not eye contact alone.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when a person did something to help you solve a problem](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20a%20person%20did%20something%20to%20help%20you%20solve%20a%20problem.md) · [Describe a time when you sent a message or an email to someone but received no reply for a long time](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20sent%20a%20message%20or%20an%20email%20to%20someone%20but%20received%20no%20reply%20for%20a%20long%20time.md)

论点回查：[E29](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#29-what-does-effective-communication-involve-有效沟通)、[E30](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#30-example-of-active-listening-积极倾听的例子) · [审计 P160](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p160)。

<a id="p168"></a>

### P168 · B｜What are the advantages and disadvantages of shopping in small shops?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作 ＋ T10 地点、建筑与设施

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB06 主链 / 对照](#ab06)；辅助链：[AB21](#ab21)

**串法：** 小店可能便于直接交流，但选择和设施可能有限 → 服务体验与实际需求两面。

**答案：**

> A small shop can offer direct, personal communication, but its range of products may be limited.
> **Being able to explain a need clearly to one member of staff** can make choosing something easier.
> For example, a customer buying a laptop may appreciate a patient explanation, yet find that only a few models are available.
> So, a small shop works well when its service and selection meet the customer’s needs.

**学习提示：** Li 的耐心服务迁移到假设小店；原故事未确定店铺规模。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when you received good service from a staff member in a shop](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20received%20good%20service%20from%20a%20staff%20member%20in%20a%20shop.md)

论点回查：[E29](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#29-what-does-effective-communication-involve-有效沟通)、[E4](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#4-building-design) · [审计 P168](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p168)。

<a id="p174"></a>

### P174 · A｜Do you think good service is important?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB06 主链 / 对照](#ab06)

**串法：** 倾听与清楚解释→解决关切、建立信任；换顾客场景。

**答案：**

> Yes, good service is important because it helps customers feel understood.
> **Clear explanations and careful listening can reduce misunderstandings and build trust.**
> For example, a shop assistant can listen to a customer's problem with a product before suggesting a suitable response.
> That said, friendly language alone is not enough to resolve every problem.
> Overall, good service depends on understanding and addressing customers' concerns.

<a id="p175"></a>

### P175 · A｜Do the most expensive shops have the best service?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB06 主链 / 对照](#ab06)

**串法：** 服务看实际倾听与解决关切；价格不是充分证据，可核实评价。

**答案：**

> No, expensive shops do not necessarily offer the best service.
> **Good service depends on whether staff listen carefully and respond clearly to customers' concerns.**
> For example, an assistant in a small, affordable shop may explain a product patiently and answer the customer's questions.
> That said, some expensive shops do provide excellent service.
> Overall, the way staff treat customers matters more than price alone.

### P176 · A｜Why should companies react quickly when customers have difficulties?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q1

逻辑链：[本节 AB06 主链 / 对照](#ab06)

**串法：** 及时回应→减少顾客不确定与误解→维持信任。

**答案：**

> Companies should respond quickly because leaving customers uncertain can weaken trust.
> **A clear reply helps people understand what is happening and feel that their concerns have been heard.**
> For example, a company can acknowledge a delivery problem and explain the next step instead of leaving the customer without information.
> So, a prompt and useful response can protect the customer relationship.

<a id="p192"></a>

### P192 · A｜What should people do when members of a group have different opinions about a decision?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q6

逻辑链：[本节 AB06 主链 / 对照](#ab06)；辅助链：[AB08](#ab08)

**串法：** 倾听分歧、表达理由、协调分工→共同决定。

**答案：**

> Group members should explain their views clearly and listen to the reasons behind other opinions.
> **This can reduce misunderstandings and help them coordinate a shared decision.**
> For example, a project team can discuss each proposed plan and agree on what each member would need to do.
> So, patient discussion is more useful than simply repeating opposing positions.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when a person did something to help you solve a problem](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20a%20person%20did%20something%20to%20help%20you%20solve%20a%20problem.md) · [Describe a time when you sent a message or an email to someone but received no reply for a long time](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20sent%20a%20message%20or%20an%20email%20to%20someone%20but%20received%20no%20reply%20for%20a%20long%20time.md)

论点回查：[E29](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#29-what-does-effective-communication-involve-有效沟通)、[E30](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#30-example-of-active-listening-积极倾听的例子)、[E35](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#35-what-does-effective-teamwork-mean-有效团队合作) · [审计 P192](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p192)。

<a id="p209"></a>

### P209 · A｜What would you do if you haven't received a reply after sending out a message?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q6

逻辑链：[本节 AB06 主链 / 对照](#ab06)

**串法：** 礼貌追问、清楚说明急迫程度→减少误解；不用假定对方故意忽视。

**答案：**

> I would send a polite follow-up and explain whether the message was urgent.
> **Clear communication can reduce uncertainty without immediately assuming that the other person is ignoring me.**
> For example, I could ask whether they had seen my question and say when I needed an answer.
> So, I would try to clarify the situation calmly.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when a person did something to help you solve a problem](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20a%20person%20did%20something%20to%20help%20you%20solve%20a%20problem.md) · [Describe a time when you sent a message or an email to someone but received no reply for a long time](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20sent%20a%20message%20or%20an%20email%20to%20someone%20but%20received%20no%20reply%20for%20a%20long%20time.md)

论点回查：[E29](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#29-what-does-effective-communication-involve-有效沟通)、[E30](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#30-example-of-active-listening-积极倾听的例子)、[E31](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#31-effects-of-unclear-communication-沟通不清的影响) · [审计 P209](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p209)。

<a id="p212"></a>

### P212 · A｜Why do some people feel angry when others don't reply to their message?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q1

逻辑链：[本节 AB06 主链 / 对照](#ab06)；辅助链：[AB07](#ab07)

**串法：** 没有回应→不确定/觉得不被理解→关系距离与负面情绪。

**答案：**

> Some people feel angry because a missing reply leaves them uncertain and makes them feel ignored.
> **They may think the other person does not understand or value their concerns.**
> For example, someone asking a close friend for support may feel hurt when there is no response at all.
> So, the anger can come from feeling disconnected or misunderstood.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when a person did something to help you solve a problem](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20a%20person%20did%20something%20to%20help%20you%20solve%20a%20problem.md) · [Describe a time when you sent a message or an email to someone but received no reply for a long time](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20sent%20a%20message%20or%20an%20email%20to%20someone%20but%20received%20no%20reply%20for%20a%20long%20time.md)

论点回查：[E31](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#31-effects-of-unclear-communication-沟通不清的影响)、[E32](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#32-example-of-poor-listening-缺乏倾听的例子)、[E34](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#34-why-real-interaction-builds-relationships-真实互动如何建立关系) · [审计 P212](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p212)。

<a id="p214"></a>

### P214 · A｜What should a leader do to make team members want to follow him or her?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q6

逻辑链：[本节 AB06 主链 / 对照](#ab06)；辅助链：[AB08](#ab08)

**串法：** 倾听支持、明确分工→信任与稳定合作→愿意跟随。

**答案：**

> A leader should listen carefully, offer support and make everyone's responsibilities clear.
> **People can work together more confidently when they understand their roles and feel their concerns are taken seriously.**
> For example, a team leader can divide a project according to members' strengths and help anyone who runs into difficulties.
> So, clear guidance and genuine support can encourage people to follow a leader.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

论点回查：[E29](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#29-what-does-effective-communication-involve-有效沟通)、[E30](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#30-example-of-active-listening-积极倾听的例子)、[E35](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#35-what-does-effective-teamwork-mean-有效团队合作) · [审计 P214](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p214)。

<a id="p230"></a>

### P230 · A｜Who do young people like to share opinions with?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB06 主链 / 对照](#ab06)；辅助链：[AB07](#ab07)

**串法：** 选择愿意倾听和提供支持的人→理解与信任；可列朋友或家人。

**答案：**

> Young people often want to share opinions with someone who listens and understands their concerns.
> **That could be a friend, a parent or a teacher who has built a trusting relationship with them.**
> For example, a teenager may discuss a school problem with a friend who listens carefully before giving advice.
> So, trust and understanding can matter more than the person's formal role.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。
