<a id="p240"></a>

### P240 · A｜Would you tell others if you change your plan?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB06 主链 / 对照](#ab06)；辅助链：[AB08](#ab08)

**串法：** 及时清楚说明变化→少误解→便于共同协调。

**答案：**

> Yes, I would tell people who are affected by the change.
> **Clear communication helps them adjust their own arrangements and prevents misunderstandings.**
> For example, I would message a friend about a new meeting time instead of leaving them following the old plan.
> That said, unrelated people do not need every update.
> Overall, sharing relevant changes helps everyone coordinate better.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when a person did something to help you solve a problem](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20a%20person%20did%20something%20to%20help%20you%20solve%20a%20problem.md) · [Describe a time when you sent a message or an email to someone but received no reply for a long time](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20sent%20a%20message%20or%20an%20email%20to%20someone%20but%20received%20no%20reply%20for%20a%20long%20time.md)

论点回查：[E29](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#29-what-does-effective-communication-involve-有效沟通)、[E31](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#31-effects-of-unclear-communication-沟通不清的影响)、[E35](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#35-what-does-effective-teamwork-mean-有效团队合作) · [审计 P240](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p240)。

<a id="p245"></a>

### P245 · A｜How would you tell your friends when you must change plans?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q6

逻辑链：[本节 AB06 主链 / 对照](#ab06)

**串法：** 清楚解释原因、更新安排→减少误解；用改见面时间例子。

**答案：**

> I would explain the change clearly and suggest a realistic new arrangement.
> **Giving the reason and listening to my friends' responses can help avoid misunderstandings.**
> For example, I could say that I needed to move our meeting and ask whether another time would work for them.
> So, I would aim to be clear, considerate and willing to discuss the change.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

论点回查：[E29](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#29-what-does-effective-communication-involve-有效沟通)、[E31](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#31-effects-of-unclear-communication-沟通不清的影响) · [审计 P245](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p245)。

<a id="p250"></a>

### P250 · A｜Who can children turn to for help when making a decision?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作 ＋ T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB06 主链 / 对照](#ab06)；辅助链：[AB14](#ab14)

**串法：** 找有经验且愿倾听的人，再核实信息→较好决定。

**答案：**

> Children can turn to people who listen carefully and have relevant experience, such as parents or teachers.
> **Useful advice can help them understand their options and make a more informed decision.**
> For example, a child choosing a school activity can ask a teacher to explain what each option involves.
> So, the best support comes from someone who understands both the child and the choice.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

<a id="p292"></a>

### P292 · B｜What kinds of behavior are considered as good behavior?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作 ＋ T04 公平、规则与公共资源

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB06 主链 / 对照](#ab06)

**串法：** 认真倾听、清楚回应和尊重他人 → 减少误解 → 建立信任。

**答案：**

> Listening carefully, responding respectfully and being clear with others are examples of good behaviour.
> **Taking another person’s concerns seriously** helps avoid misunderstandings and build trust.
> For example, someone can explain a delay politely instead of leaving a friend to guess what has happened.
> So, good behaviour includes making everyday interactions more considerate and understandable.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when you sent a message or an email to someone but received no reply for a long time](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20sent%20a%20message%20or%20an%20email%20to%20someone%20but%20received%20no%20reply%20for%20a%20long%20time.md)

论点回查：[E19](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#19-can-rules-teach-children-responsibility)、[E30](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#30-example-of-active-listening-积极倾听的例子)、[E35](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#35-what-does-effective-teamwork-mean-有效团队合作) · [审计 P292](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p292)。

<a id="ab07"></a>

## AB07 共同相处与支持 → 关系和归属

**怎么串：** 拜访、聚会、社区和一起看比赛都说明共同活动怎样增进联系；只有人多或联系方便还不等于关系更好。

- **主链：** shared time and mutual support → emotional connection → stronger relationships
- **反向 / 对照：** limited interaction or constant distraction → emotional distance → weaker belonging

| 原题 | 等级 | 这题必须补出的桥梁 |
|---|---|---|
| [P008](#p008) | A | 希望与人更亲近 → 更多共同活动和支持 → 追求受欢迎带来的联系感。 |
| [P061](#p061) | B | 与同学聊天和共餐 → 共享轻松时光 → 中学里值得回忆的快乐。 |
| [P065](#p065) | A | 共同活动、支持与联系→更好情绪；给一个共享活动例子。 |
| [P093](#p093) | B | 跨城仍联系并返乡见面 → 保持相处与支持 → 童年友谊继续。 |
| [P094](#p094) | A | 陪伴与支持→联系、归属和更好情绪。 |
| [P096](#p096) | A | 共享经历与支持形成真实联系→线上工具不能完全替代。 |
| [P112](#p112) | B | 与人群共同参与有兴趣的活动 → 分享情绪和互动 → 感到联系与快乐。 |
| [P120](#p120) | B | 以本人拜访朋友的素材作有限观察 → 喝咖啡聊天共享时间 → 部分人喜欢到家中拜访。 |
| [P121](#p121) | B | 聊天、喝茶咖啡或共餐 → 一起相处并听近况 → 拜访增进联系。 |
| [P124](#p124) | B | 带可分享的小礼物 → 为共同活动提供内容 → 帮助自然互动。 |
| [P125](#p125) | B | 每隔几个月拜访 Stella → 一起喝咖啡聊近况 → 在各自生活安排中维持联系。 |
| [P141](#p141) | B | 生日、重聚或重要事件 → 有理由聚在一起 → 共享时间强化联系。 |
| [P177](#p177) | A | 共享时间与支持→更强关系和归属。 |
| [P178](#p178) | B | 晚上聊天并分享书籍和生活 → 有倾听与支持 → 简单活动也愉快。 |
| [P180](#p180) | A | 保持交流、共同活动和互相支持→维持联系。 |
| [P229](#p229) | B | 在校与在家的实际相处机会不同 → 和老师或父母的交流量不同 → 不用关系价值证明频率。 |
| [P231](#p231) | A | 共同观看、分享感受→联系与情绪改善；不必引入竞技悬念链。 |
| [P232](#p232) | B | 体育场、家中等提供共同观看场景 → 分享感受 → 地点服务观赛互动。 |
| [P234](#p234) | B | 现场与观众同步互动，电视在家省出行 → 社交气氛与便利不同 → 明确两侧。 |
| [P235](#p235) | B | 用本人圈子的羽毛球兴趣和观赛例子 → 共同观看有参与感 → 不声称全国赛事第一。 |
| [P264](#p264) | B | 地方事件与共同生活有关 → 熟人会交流 → 用海岸清理故事限定范围。 |
| [P267](#p267) | A | 共同参与与互助→联系、归属和积极情绪。 |
| [P268](#p268) | B | 过去当面相处与现在远距联系方式不同 → 联系强弱取决于实际共同时间与支持 → 不断言过去必然更强。 |

<a id="p008"></a>

### P008 · A｜Why do people want to be popular?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q1

逻辑链：[本节 AB07 主链 / 对照](#ab07)

**串法：** 希望与人更亲近 → 更多共同活动和支持 → 追求受欢迎带来的联系感。

**答案：**

> I think many people want to be popular because they want to feel connected to others.
> **Spending time together and receiving support can give them a stronger sense of belonging.**
> For example, a student may want to be included in a friendly group that studies and spends weekends together.
> So, the wish to be popular can really be a wish for closer relationships.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

### P061 · B｜What was the happiest thing about your secondary school life?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

**答案：**

> One of the happiest parts of secondary school was spending ordinary breaks with my classmates.
> **Chatting and eating together helped us feel close** without needing a special event.
> For example, I remember talking between lessons and having lunch with people I saw every day.
> So, the company of classmates is what I remember most warmly.

- [ ] 不看稿口述

**答案：**

> I think people are often happiest when they spend meaningful time with people who support them.
> **Shared activities can strengthen relationships and improve their mood.**
> For example, friends taking a walk together can talk about their worries and enjoy each other's company.
> So, supportive relationships and meaningful activities can be important sources of happiness.

<a id="p093"></a>

### P093 · B｜Do you still keep in touch with your friends from childhood? Why or why not?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB07 主链 / 对照](#ab07)

**串法：** 跨城仍联系并返乡见面 → 保持相处与支持 → 童年友谊继续。

**答案：**

> Yes, I still keep in touch with my childhood friend Ming.
> **Continued contact and the support we share** help the friendship stay close even though we live in different cities.
> For example, we meet when I return home and can pick up our conversation even after a long gap.
> That said, we don’t need to send messages every day.
> Overall, I keep the friendship because that connection still matters to me.

<a id="p094"></a>

### P094 · A｜How important is childhood friendship to children?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作 ＋ T02 健康、习惯与休闲

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB07 主链 / 对照](#ab07)；辅助链：[AB05](#ab05)

**串法：** 陪伴与支持→联系、归属和更好情绪。

**答案：**

> Childhood friendship is important because it gives children companionship and support.
> **Spending time together can build trust and help them feel that they belong.**
> For example, a child who feels worried at school may feel better after talking and playing with a close friend.
> That said, the quality of a friendship matters more than its length.
> Overall, supportive friendships can make childhood a happier experience.

<a id="p096"></a>

### P096 · A｜Do you think online communication through social media will replace face-to-face communication?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB07 主链 / 对照](#ab07)

**串法：** 共享经历与支持形成真实联系→线上工具不能完全替代。

**答案：**

> No, I don't think social media will completely replace face-to-face communication.
> **Strong relationships depend on shared experiences and mutual support, as well as exchanging messages.**
> For example, spending an afternoon helping a friend through a problem offers shared time that short online replies may not provide.
> That said, online messages can still help people stay in contact.
> Overall, digital communication can support relationships without replacing every real interaction.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a friend from your childhood](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20friend%20from%20your%20childhood.md) · [Describe an enjoyable evening with your friends](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20an%20enjoyable%20evening%20with%20your%20friends.md)

论点回查：[E33](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#33-can-technology-replace-real-human-interaction-科技能否取代真实互动)、[E34](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#34-why-real-interaction-builds-relationships-真实互动如何建立关系) · [审计 P096](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p096)。

- [ ] 不看稿口述

**串法：** 与人群共同参与有兴趣的活动 → 分享情绪和互动 → 感到联系与快乐。

**答案：**

> Some people enjoy crowded places when they can share an activity with the people around them.
> **Reacting together can create a sense of connection** rather than just feeling surrounded by strangers.
> For example, spectators at a badminton final may cheer the same point and enjoy the excitement together.
> So, a crowd can be enjoyable when it is part of a shared experience.

**答案：**

> Some do, and visiting a friend at home is something I enjoy myself.
> **Spending unhurried time together** makes it easier to catch up and maintain a close relationship.
> For example, I visit Stella’s apartment, where we make coffee and talk by the window.
> That said, one person’s habits don’t represent everyone in China.
> Overall, home visits can be a pleasant way for people to stay connected.

**答案：**

> Food or drinks that people can share are sensible examples of gifts to bring on a visit.
> **Sharing something simple** can make the time together feel warm and relaxed.
> For example, a visitor could bring some snacks to enjoy while everyone talks over coffee.
> So, a small gift that fits the visit can be more useful than something elaborate.

### P125 · B｜How often do you visit your relatives or friends?

> **Having time to sit and talk properly** helps us stay connected between our other responsibilities.

> So, occasional visits are an important way for me to maintain that friendship.

**串法：** 共享时间与支持→更强关系和归属。

**答案：**

> Yes, communicating with family is important for maintaining close relationships.
> **Shared time and mutual support help people feel connected, while limited interaction can create emotional distance.**

> Overall, regular, meaningful communication helps families stay close.

题型：Q2

逻辑链：[本节 AB07 主链 / 对照](#ab07)

**串法：** 在校与在家的实际相处机会不同 → 和老师或父母的交流量不同 → 不用关系价值证明频率。

**答案：**

> I wouldn’t assume children communicate more with one group in every case.
> **The time they spend together and the attention they receive** affect how much conversation happens.
> For example, a child might discuss schoolwork with teachers but have a longer personal conversation with a parent at home.
> So, the pattern depends on the child’s daily arrangements and relationships.

**学习提示：** 素材没有交流频率数据，采用场景比较，不声称全国调查。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a new skill you learned when you were a child](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20new%20skill%20you%20learned%20when%20you%20were%20a%20child.md)

论点回查：[E34](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#34-why-real-interaction-builds-relationships-真实互动如何建立关系) · [审计 P229](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p229)。

<a id="p231"></a>

### P231 · A｜Why do some people like to watch sports events?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作 ＋ T02 健康、习惯与休闲

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q1

逻辑链：[本节 AB07 主链 / 对照](#ab07)；辅助链：[AB05](#ab05)

**串法：** 共同观看、分享感受→联系与情绪改善；不必引入竞技悬念链。

**答案：**

> Some people enjoy watching sports because it gives them meaningful time with others.
> **Sharing an activity can strengthen relationships and improve their mood.**
> For example, friends can watch a match together and spend time talking about what is happening.
> So, watching sport can be an enjoyable social activity.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a friend from your childhood](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20friend%20from%20your%20childhood.md) · [Describe an enjoyable evening with your friends](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20an%20enjoyable%20evening%20with%20your%20friends.md)

论点回查：[E34](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#34-why-real-interaction-builds-relationships-真实互动如何建立关系)、[E48](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#48-balanced-routines-and-social-connection-均衡作息与人际联系) · [审计 P231](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p231)。

<a id="p232"></a>

### P232 · B｜Where do people normally watch sports events?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作 ＋ T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB07 主链 / 对照](#ab07)

**串法：** 体育场、家中等提供共同观看场景 → 分享感受 → 地点服务观赛互动。

**答案：**

> People can watch sports in an arena, at home or somewhere showing the match on a screen.
> **Watching with other people lets them share reactions and conversation**.
> For example, Stella and I watched badminton in a Beijing arena, while friends could also enjoy the same match together at home.
> So, several places can provide a shared viewing experience.

<a id="p234"></a>

### P234 · B｜What are the main differences between watching a sports event live in a stadium and watching it on TV?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作 ＋ T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q2

逻辑链：[本节 AB07 主链 / 对照](#ab07)；辅助链：[AB13](#ab13)

**串法：** 现场与观众同步互动，电视在家省出行 → 社交气氛与便利不同 → 明确两侧。

**答案：**

> Watching in a stadium offers a shared atmosphere, while television is usually easier to fit into daily life.
> **Reacting alongside a crowd** can feel different from watching without travelling anywhere.
> For example, at a badminton final I could hear everyone cheer together, whereas watching at home would avoid the journey.
> So, the main trade-off is the live atmosphere against convenience.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a live sport match you have ever watched](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95Describe%20a%20live%20sport%20match%20you%20have%20ever%20watched.md)

论点回查：[E34](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#34-why-real-interaction-builds-relationships-真实互动如何建立关系)、[E2](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#2-technology--efficiency-and-over-reliance-科技提高效率--过度依赖) · [审计 P234](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p234)。

- [ ] 不看稿口述

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q7

**串法：** 用本人圈子的羽毛球兴趣和观赛例子 → 共同观看有参与感 → 不声称全国赛事第一。

**答案：**

> Badminton is popular to watch among people I know, although I can’t rank events across China.
> **Following a match together gives fans a shared experience** and something to discuss.
> For example, Stella and I enjoyed a Beijing final and talked about the player’s tactics afterwards.
> So, badminton is a clear example from my own circle rather than a national ranking.

<a id="p264"></a>

### P264 · B｜Are there a lot of people talking about local news?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作 ＋ T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB07 主链 / 对照](#ab07)

**串法：** 地方事件与共同生活有关 → 熟人会交流 → 用海岸清理故事限定范围。

**答案：**

> Some local stories do get people talking when they concern a place the community knows.
> **Sharing news about a common concern** gives neighbours and families something meaningful to discuss.
> For example, the coastal clean-up in my Zhanjiang story interested people because it involved a beach they used.
> That said, one story doesn’t show how often everyone follows local news.
> Overall, community relevance can encourage local discussion.

**学习提示：** 沿用地方新闻练习，不把“引起关注”扩写为本轮核实的统计。

论点回查：[E34](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#34-why-real-interaction-builds-relationships-真实互动如何建立关系)、[E15](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#15-why-should-consumers-fact-check-advertisements) · [审计 P264](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p264)。

机制：M01 实用价值

逻辑链：[本节 AB07 主链 / 对照](#ab07)；辅助链：[AB05](#ab05)

**串法：** 共同参与与互助→联系、归属和积极情绪。

**答案：**

> Some people get involved in their community because they want stronger connections with those around them.
> **Shared activities and mutual support can give them a sense of belonging and improve their mood.**
> For example, neighbours can organise a regular walk where they spend time together and discuss everyday concerns.
> So, community participation can help people feel less distant from one another.

论点回查：[E34](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#34-why-real-interaction-builds-relationships-真实互动如何建立关系)、[E48](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#48-balanced-routines-and-social-connection-均衡作息与人际联系) · [审计 P267](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p267)。

<a id="p268"></a>

### P268 · B｜Do you think local communities were stronger in the past than they are today?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作 ＋ T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q3

逻辑链：[本节 AB07 主链 / 对照](#ab07)

**串法：** 过去当面相处与现在远距联系方式不同 → 联系强弱取决于实际共同时间与支持 → 不断言过去必然更强。

**答案：**

> I wouldn’t say local communities were always stronger in the past.
> **Regular interaction and mutual support** matter more than the period itself, although the ways people meet can change.
> For example, neighbours may once have talked mainly in person, while a group chat now helps them arrange a shared clean-up.
> So, community strength depends on continued participation rather than age alone.

**学习提示：** 过去/现在为常识性方式对照，不声称全国社区关系变化调查。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a piece of local news that people are interested in](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20piece%20of%20local%20news%20that%20people%20are%20interested%20in.md) · [Describe a friend from your childhood](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20friend%20from%20your%20childhood.md)

论点回查：[E3](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#3-technology--internet-reshaping-habits-互联网重塑习惯)、[E34](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#34-why-real-interaction-builds-relationships-真实互动如何建立关系) · [审计 P268](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p268)。

<a id="ab08"></a>

## AB08 明确分工与互助 → 合作结果

**怎么串：** 把软件项目里的分工、检查与遗漏迁移到学校或服务场景。原因题、解决题、领导判断题都落在职责是否清楚。

- **主链：** clear roles and mutual support → stable cooperation → stronger collective results
- **反向 / 对照：** unclear responsibility or reluctance to communicate → coordination problems → weaker teamwork

| 原题 | 等级 | 这题必须补出的桥梁 |
|---|---|---|
| [P017](#p017) | A | 团队运动→分工合作，或规律运动→健康习惯；用球类训练例子。 |
| [P173](#p173) | B | 店员职责不清 → 重复处理或遗漏需求 → 顾客收到差服务。 |
| [P213](#p213) | A | 重复分工和沟通协调问题→降低效率；独立任务可避免这类成本，限定任务适合独做。 |
| [P215](#p215) | A | 学分工与支持→协调顺畅→集体结果更好。 |
| [P216](#p216) | B | 小组展示和制作项目 → 按任务分工并互相衔接 → 学校中的合作任务。 |
| [P217](#p217) | A | 实践分工与沟通→合作技能与集体表现。 |
| [P218](#p218) | A | 看是否倾听、明确职责和维持协调→团队实际结果。 |
| [P236](#p236) | B | 规则易理解且能看到配合的比赛 → 观察分工与互助 → 适合儿童理解合作。 |

<a id="p017"></a>

### P017 · A｜What can children learn from doing sports?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作 ＋ T02 健康、习惯与休闲

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB08 主链 / 对照](#ab08)；辅助链：[AB04](#ab04)

**串法：** 团队运动→分工合作，或规律运动→健康习惯；用球类训练例子。

**答案：**

> Children can learn how to cooperate with others and develop healthy habits through sport.
> **Team activities involve clear roles and mutual support, while regular exercise becomes part of their routine.**
> For example, children playing football can practise taking different positions and helping teammates during a match.
> So, sport can teach useful habits as well as teamwork.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when you worked in a group](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20worked%20in%20a%20group.md)

论点回查：[E35](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#35-what-does-effective-teamwork-mean-有效团队合作)、[E39](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#3940-balanced-routines-and-health-均衡作息与健康)、[E40](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#3940-balanced-routines-and-health-均衡作息与健康) · [审计 P017](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p017)。

<a id="p173"></a>

### P173 · B｜Why is customer service not good in some shops?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q1

逻辑链：[本节 AB08 主链 / 对照](#ab08)

**串法：** 店员职责不清 → 重复处理或遗漏需求 → 顾客收到差服务。

**答案：**

> Customer service can be poor when staff responsibilities are unclear.
> **Not knowing who should handle a request** can lead to delays or leave a customer’s problem unanswered.
> For example, two assistants might each assume the other is checking a laptop warranty, so neither gives the customer a reply.
> So, clear responsibility is an important part of reliable service.

**学习提示：** 将小组重复与遗漏任务迁移到店员场景，不增写“店员缺培训”等独立原因。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when you worked in a group](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20worked%20in%20a%20group.md) · [Describe a time when you received good service from a staff member in a shop](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20received%20good%20service%20from%20a%20staff%20member%20in%20a%20shop.md)

论点回查：[E37](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#37-what-happens-when-responsibilities-arent-clear-职责不清的后果)、[E31](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#31-effects-of-unclear-communication-沟通不清的影响) · [审计 P173](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p173)。

<a id="p213"></a>

### P213 · A｜Why do some people prefer to work by themselves?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M02 时间与精力成本

题型：Q1

逻辑链：[本节 AB08 主链 / 对照](#ab08)

**串法：** 重复分工和沟通协调问题→降低效率；独立任务可避免这类成本，限定任务适合独做。

**答案：**

> Some people prefer working alone because coordinating with others can be difficult.
> **Unclear responsibilities can lead to duplicated work, delays and weaker results.**
> For example, two people may waste time writing the same report section because neither knows who is responsible.
> So, working alone can appeal to people who want to avoid coordination problems.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when you worked in a group](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20worked%20in%20a%20group.md)

论点回查：[E36](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#36-why-are-clear-roles-important-in-teamwork-明确分工的重要性)、[E37](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#37-what-happens-when-responsibilities-arent-clear-职责不清的后果) · [审计 P213](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p213)。

<a id="p215"></a>

### P215 · A｜Should students learn to do group work?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB08 主链 / 对照](#ab08)

**串法：** 学分工与支持→协调顺畅→集体结果更好。

**答案：**

> Yes, students should learn to do group work.
> **Clear roles and mutual support help them coordinate tasks and achieve better results together.**
> For example, a class project can give each student responsibility for research, writing or presenting.
> That said, unclear responsibilities can leave some work duplicated or unfinished.
> Overall, learning to cooperate is a useful part of education.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

<a id="p216"></a>

### P216 · B｜What group tasks are there in schools?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB08 主链 / 对照](#ab08)

**串法：** 小组展示和制作项目 → 按任务分工并互相衔接 → 学校中的合作任务。

**答案：**

> Schools can give students group presentations, model-building tasks or shared research projects.
> **Clear roles help them combine their work** instead of repeating the same part.
> For example, a presentation group could divide research, slide preparation and speaking, then check how the parts fit together.
> So, group tasks work well when students share a clear goal and responsibilities.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

论点回查：[E35](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#35-what-does-effective-teamwork-mean-有效团队合作) · [审计 P216](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p216)。

<a id="p217"></a>

### P217 · A｜What advantages are there for students experiencing teamwork at school?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB08 主链 / 对照](#ab08)；辅助链：[AB06](#ab06)

**串法：** 实践分工与沟通→合作技能与集体表现。

**答案：**

> Teamwork at school gives students practice in sharing responsibilities and communicating with others.
> **They can learn to coordinate their efforts and support classmates working towards the same result.**
> For example, students preparing a presentation can divide the sections and discuss how to connect their ideas.
> That said, group tasks need clear roles to work well.
> Overall, these experiences can help students become better team members.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when you worked in a group](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20worked%20in%20a%20group.md)

论点回查：[E35](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#35-what-does-effective-teamwork-mean-有效团队合作)、[E37](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#37-what-happens-when-responsibilities-arent-clear-职责不清的后果) · [审计 P217](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p217)。

<a id="p218"></a>

### P218 · A｜How can you tell if a person is a good leader?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q6

逻辑链：[本节 AB08 主链 / 对照](#ab08)；辅助链：[AB06](#ab06)

**串法：** 看是否倾听、明确职责和维持协调→团队实际结果。

**答案：**

> I would look at how clearly the person guides the team and listens to its members.
> **A good leader helps people understand their responsibilities and work together without unnecessary confusion.**
> For example, during a group project, they can notice overlapping tasks and help members agree on a clearer division of work.
> So, effective coordination and supportive communication are useful signs of good leadership.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when you worked in a group](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20worked%20in%20a%20group.md)

论点回查：[E29](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#29-what-does-effective-communication-involve-有效沟通)、[E35](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#35-what-does-effective-teamwork-mean-有效团队合作)、[E37](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#37-what-happens-when-responsibilities-arent-clear-职责不清的后果) · [审计 P218](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p218)。

<a id="p236"></a>

### P236 · B｜What types of sports matches are suitable for children to watch?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作 ＋ T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB08 主链 / 对照](#ab08)

**串法：** 规则易理解且能看到配合的比赛 → 观察分工与互助 → 适合儿童理解合作。

**答案：**

> Matches with understandable rules and visible teamwork can be suitable for children.
> **Seeing players coordinate their roles** gives them something useful to notice beyond the score.
> For example, a simple doubles badminton match can show how two players cover the court and support each other.
> So, I’d choose a match children can follow and discuss afterwards.

**学习提示：** 从羽毛球观赛迁移为双打示例，不声称已有素材那场决赛是双打。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a live sport match you have ever watched](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95Describe%20a%20live%20sport%20match%20you%20have%20ever%20watched.md)

论点回查：[E35](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#35-what-does-effective-teamwork-mean-有效团队合作)、[E22](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#22-what-is-the-most-important-factor-in-learning-well) · [审计 P236](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p236)。

<a id="ab09"></a>

## AB09 扩大资源获取 → 更多学习机会

**怎么串：** 说明谁原本不便获得资源、怎样获得机会。线上学习只是渠道，核心是原先的获取障碍减少。

- **主链：** wider access to basic resources → narrower opportunity gaps → greater social mobility
- **反向 / 对照：** unequal access → fewer opportunities → entrenched disadvantage

| 原题 | 等级 | 这题必须补出的桥梁 |
|---|---|---|
| [P299](#p299) | A | 扩大教育获取机会、节省时间→学习更便利；已有线上课程语境。 |

<a id="p299"></a>

### P299 · A｜What are the advantages of online learning?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T04 公平、规则与公共资源 ＋ T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响

机制：M02 时间与精力成本

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB09 主链 / 对照](#ab09)；辅助链：[AB13](#ab13)

**串法：** 扩大教育获取机会、节省时间→学习更便利；已有线上课程语境。

**答案：**

> Online learning can make education easier to access and save learners time.
> **People can reach learning resources without having to travel to every lesson.**
> For example, someone living far from a course provider can attend an online class from home.
> That said, access alone does not guarantee effective learning.
> Overall, online courses can open up useful learning opportunities.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a person who is good at learning and speaking new languages](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20person%20who%20is%20good%20at%20learning%20and%20speaking%20new%20languages.md) · [Describe an important decision that you made](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20an%20important%20decision%20that%20you%20made.md)

论点回查：[E17](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#17-can-online-education-reduce-inequality)、[E2](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#2-technology--efficiency-and-over-reliance-科技提高效率--过度依赖) · [审计 P299](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p299)。

<a id="ab10"></a>

## AB10 清晰公平的规则 → 可预测行为与责任

**怎么串：** 正向解释规则和后果，反向解释过度控制。具体校规可作假设，实际法规的名称和实施状态不能从建议中生成。

- **主链：** clear and fair rules → predictable consequences → more responsible behaviour
- **反向 / 对照：** excessive or inconsistent rules → resistance and unfairness → weaker trust

| 原题 | 等级 | 这题必须补出的桥梁 |
|---|---|---|
| [P063](#p063) | A | 可主张不必监控所有活动：过度控制→抵触与不信任；倾听支持可替代全面监控。自主权链可深化但不是必需。 |
| [P273](#p273) | A | 可用过度控制→抵触与信任下降，倾听建议→支持来直接回答；自主选择→责任链是可补深化，不是本题必需前提。 |
| [P290](#p290) | B | 按约完成任务、尊重发言和公共物品 → 行为可预测 → 学校合作更顺畅。 |
| [P291](#p291) | B | 实际守法率不能由规则价值推断 → 只表达有限看法 → 例子说明规则如何指导行为。 |
| [P293](#p293) | B | 家人或生活场景解释规则 → 理解要求和后果 → 学校外也能学习法律意识。 |
| [P294](#p294) | A | 可预测行为与明确责任→信任、合作顺畅。 |
| [P295](#p295) | A | 解释清楚规则、保持一致后果→孩子理解并承担责任。 |
| [P303](#p303) | B | 借小组任务的清晰要求 → 列按时完成、尊重同伴等示例 → 不声称全国统一校规。 |

<a id="p063"></a>

### P063 · A｜Do you think teachers should always know what their students are doing?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T04 公平、规则与公共资源 ＋ T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M03 自主权与责任感

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB10 主链 / 对照](#ab10)；辅助链：[AB06](#ab06)

**串法：** 可主张不必监控所有活动：过度控制→抵触与不信任；倾听支持可替代全面监控。自主权链可深化但不是必需。

**答案：**

> No, I don't think teachers need to know everything students are doing.
> **Too much control can make students feel unfairly treated and weaken trust.**
> For example, constantly questioning a student about every activity after school may make them resist talking openly.
> That said, teachers should still listen when students need support.
> Overall, supportive communication is more useful than trying to monitor everything.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when you worked in a group](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20worked%20in%20a%20group.md) · [Describe something you do regularly that you think is a waste of time](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20something%20you%20do%20regularly%20that%20you%20think%20is%20a%20waste%20of%20time.md)

论点回查：[E20](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#20-can-excessive-or-inconsistent-rules-have-negative-effects)、[E30](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#30-example-of-active-listening-积极倾听的例子) · [审计 P063](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p063)。

<a id="p273"></a>

### P273 · A｜Should parents interfere with their children's ambitions?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T04 公平、规则与公共资源 ＋ T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M03 自主权与责任感

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB10 主链 / 对照](#ab10)；辅助链：[AB06](#ab06)

**串法：** 可用过度控制→抵触与信任下降，倾听建议→支持来直接回答；自主选择→责任链是可补深化，不是本题必需前提。

**答案：**

> Parents should offer guidance, but I don't think they should control their children's ambitions.
> **Excessive control can create resistance and weaken trust between parents and children.**
> For example, repeatedly dismissing a child's interest without listening may make the child unwilling to discuss their plans.
> That said, parents can still explain their concerns calmly.
> Overall, listening and giving advice are better than controlling every choice.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when you worked in a group](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20worked%20in%20a%20group.md) · [Describe something you do regularly that you think is a waste of time](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20something%20you%20do%20regularly%20that%20you%20think%20is%20a%20waste%20of%20time.md)

论点回查：[E20](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#20-can-excessive-or-inconsistent-rules-have-negative-effects)、[E30](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#30-example-of-active-listening-积极倾听的例子) · [审计 P273](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p273)。

<a id="p290"></a>

### P290 · B｜What rules should students follow at school?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T04 公平、规则与公共资源 ＋ T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M05 社会影响与模仿

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB10 主链 / 对照](#ab10)

**串法：** 按约完成任务、尊重发言和公共物品 → 行为可预测 → 学校合作更顺畅。

**答案：**

> Students should follow rules about completing agreed work, respecting others and looking after shared things.
> **Clear expectations help everyone understand their responsibilities**.
> For example, a project group can agree when each member must finish their part so the next person can continue.
> So, useful school rules make shared learning more organised and considerate.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when you worked in a group](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20worked%20in%20a%20group.md)

论点回查：[E19](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#19-can-rules-teach-children-responsibility)、[E35](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#35-what-does-effective-teamwork-mean-有效团队合作) · [审计 P290](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p290)。

<a id="p291"></a>

### P291 · B｜Do people in your country usually obey the law?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T04 公平、规则与公共资源

机制：M05 社会影响与模仿

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB10 主链 / 对照](#ab10)

**串法：** 实际守法率不能由规则价值推断 → 只表达有限看法 → 例子说明规则如何指导行为。

**答案：**

> I think many people try to follow the law, but I wouldn’t claim to know the overall rate in China.
> **Clear expectations and consistent consequences** can help people understand what they are responsible for.
> For example, a clearly explained rule about waste disposal can guide how someone deals with rubbish.
> That said, having a rule doesn’t mean everyone follows it.
> Overall, actual behaviour can vary, even when the expectations are clear.

**学习提示：** Part 2 的环保法是提议；这里的规则例子是假设，不称其已经实施。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe an environmental law you would like your country to introduce](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20an%20environmental%20law%20you%20would%20like%20your%20country%20to%20introduce.md)

论点回查：[E19](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#19-can-rules-teach-children-responsibility)、[E20](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#20-can-excessive-or-inconsistent-rules-have-negative-effects) · [审计 P291](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p291)。

<a id="p293"></a>

### P293 · B｜Do you think children can learn about the law outside of school?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T04 公平、规则与公共资源 ＋ T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M05 社会影响与模仿

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB10 主链 / 对照](#ab10)

**串法：** 家人或生活场景解释规则 → 理解要求和后果 → 学校外也能学习法律意识。

**答案：**

> Yes, children can learn about the law outside school through conversations and everyday situations.
> **A clear explanation of a rule and its purpose** can help them understand responsibility.
> For example, a parent could use a discussion about protecting a beach to explain why communities create rules about harmful behaviour.
> That said, adults should distinguish an existing law from a rule they would like introduced.
> Overall, everyday discussion can support children’s understanding of rules and responsibility.

**学习提示：** 不把拟议海岸塑料禁令当现行法；具体法名仍需真实材料。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe an environmental law you would like your country to introduce](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20an%20environmental%20law%20you%20would%20like%20your%20country%20to%20introduce.md)

论点回查：[E19](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#19-can-rules-teach-children-responsibility)、[E30](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#30-example-of-active-listening-积极倾听的例子) · [审计 P293](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p293)。

<a id="p294"></a>

### P294 · A｜What are the benefits for people if they obey the rules?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T04 公平、规则与公共资源 ＋ T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB10 主链 / 对照](#ab10)；辅助链：[AB08](#ab08)

**串法：** 可预测行为与明确责任→信任、合作顺畅。

**答案：**

> Following clear, fair rules can make everyday cooperation easier.
> **Predictable behaviour helps people understand their responsibilities and coordinate with others.**
> For example, students following an agreed schedule for group work know when each person should finish their part.
> That said, inconsistent rules can weaken trust rather than support it.
> Overall, sensible rules can help people work together more smoothly.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when you worked in a group](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20worked%20in%20a%20group.md) · [Describe something you do regularly that you think is a waste of time](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20something%20you%20do%20regularly%20that%20you%20think%20is%20a%20waste%20of%20time.md)

论点回查：[E19](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#19-can-rules-teach-children-responsibility)、[E35](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#35-what-does-effective-teamwork-mean-有效团队合作) · [审计 P294](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p294)。

<a id="p295"></a>

### P295 · A｜How can parents teach children to obey rules?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T04 公平、规则与公共资源

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q6

逻辑链：[本节 AB10 主链 / 对照](#ab10)

**串法：** 解释清楚规则、保持一致后果→孩子理解并承担责任。

**答案：**

> Parents can explain rules clearly and keep the consequences consistent.
> **Children are more likely to understand responsibility when their behaviour leads to predictable outcomes.**
> For example, parents can explain a household rule and respond consistently when the child follows or breaks it.
> So, clear explanations and fair treatment can help children learn to follow rules.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when you worked in a group](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20worked%20in%20a%20group.md) · [Describe something you do regularly that you think is a waste of time](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20something%20you%20do%20regularly%20that%20you%20think%20is%20a%20waste%20of%20time.md)

论点回查：[E19](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#19-can-rules-teach-children-responsibility)、[E20](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#20-can-excessive-or-inconsistent-rules-have-negative-effects) · [审计 P295](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p295)。

<a id="p303"></a>

### P303 · B｜What kind of rules do schools in China have?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T04 公平、规则与公共资源

机制：M05 社会影响与模仿

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB10 主链 / 对照](#ab10)

**串法：** 借小组任务的清晰要求 → 列按时完成、尊重同伴等示例 → 不声称全国统一校规。

**答案：**

> I can give ordinary school-rule examples, but I wouldn’t assume every school in China uses exactly the same rules.
> **Clear expectations about work and respectful behaviour** help students understand their responsibilities.
> For example, a school might require group-project tasks to be completed on time and disagreements to be discussed respectfully.
> So, the details can differ while the aim is orderly and considerate learning.

**学习提示：** Part 2 没有具体学校校规；例子是示意规则，不冒充全国统一规定或本人学校事实。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when you worked in a group](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20worked%20in%20a%20group.md)

论点回查：[E19](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#19-can-rules-teach-children-responsibility) · [审计 P303](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p303)。

<a id="ab11"></a>

## AB11 针对性公共投入 → 更好的服务

**怎么串：** 把钱如何形成服务和公共收益说清楚。研究资金必须有研究结果到支持的桥梁，不直接等同日常医疗服务。

- **主链：** targeted spending → better essential services → wider public benefit
- **反向 / 对照：** high costs or poor allocation → financial pressure → less funding for other needs

| 原题 | 等级 | 这题必须补出的桥梁 |
|---|---|---|
| [P069](#p069) | B | 研究投入 → 改善发现和应对健康问题的方法 → 潜在公共收益；仍需比较其他服务需求。 |

<a id="p069"></a>

### P069 · B｜Do you think governments should put a large amount of money into medical research?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T04 公平、规则与公共资源 ＋ T02 健康、习惯与休闲

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB11 主链 / 对照](#ab11)；辅助链：[AB04](#ab04)

**串法：** 研究投入 → 改善发现和应对健康问题的方法 → 潜在公共收益；仍需比较其他服务需求。

**答案：**

> Governments should fund worthwhile medical research, but the amount needs careful thought.
> **Research that improves ways to identify or respond to health problems** can eventually benefit the public.
> For example, a project examining earlier signs of illness could help services offer support sooner if the findings are useful.
> That said, research funding also competes with other essential services.
> Overall, the expected public benefit should guide the level of investment.

**学习提示：** 这是投入评价；例子是研究到服务的潜在路径，不声称某项研究已成功。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a person you know who would like to choose a career in the medical field (e.g. a doctor, a nurse)](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%20Describe%20a%20person%20you%20know%20who%20would%20like%20to%20choose%20a%20career%20in%20the%20medical%20field%20%28e.g.%20a%20doctor%2C%20a%20nurse%29.md)

论点回查：[E21](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#21-how-should-governments-spend-public-money)、[E57](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#57-how-can-governments-support-peoples-healthwell-being) · [审计 P069](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p069)。

<a id="ab12"></a>

## AB12 满足需求 → 企业收入与岗位

**怎么串：** 软件企业、旅游和消费换具体产品或服务；先说谁需要它，再接收入或就业。

- **主链：** useful products or services → business revenue and jobs → economic growth
- **反向 / 对照：** unstable demand or job loss → income pressure → wider inequality

| 原题 | 等级 | 这题必须补出的桥梁 |
|---|---|---|
| [P032](#p032) | A | 供给不符合实际需求→需求不稳、收入受影响；据此解释经营有难度。 |
| [P034](#p034) | A | 可用创造实际价值、保持健康作为成功标准；金钱不是这些结果的充分条件。 |
| [P046](#p046) | A | 传统产品→销售收入与岗位，并保留文化价值；不夸大整个产业占比。 |
| [P087](#p087) | A | 出售有用产品或服务→收入与就业；可把获得收入作为创业动机。 |
| [P088](#p088) | B | 创业项目满足真实需求 → 资金帮助提供服务和岗位 → 可支持，但应核对用途和机会成本。 |
| [P090](#p090) | A | 满足真实需求→稳定销售收入→经营成功。 |
| [P091](#p091) | A | 产品不符合需求→需求不稳→经营失败。 |
| [P113](#p113) | B | 城市发展增加服务需求 → 企业收入和岗位增加；公共设施也承受压力 → 评价有条件。 |
| [P131](#p131) | A | 游客购买服务→商家收入与就业→地方经济受益。 |
| [P133](#p133) | A | 旅游服务收入→就业→经济增长；用住宿餐饮例子。 |
| [P172](#p172) | A | 购买有用产品→收入与岗位→经济增长。 |
| [P276](#p276) | B | 部分更高职位提供实际收入或发展空间 → 满足生活和职业需要 → 愿意争取。 |

<a id="p032"></a>

### P032 · A｜Is it difficult to run a business in today’s world?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T05 经济与就业

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB12 主链 / 对照](#ab12)

**串法：** 供给不符合实际需求→需求不稳、收入受影响；据此解释经营有难度。

**答案：**

> Yes, I think running a business can be difficult.
> **Companies need to provide things people actually want, because weak demand can threaten their income.**
> For example, a shop selling products that local customers rarely need may struggle to make enough sales.
> That said, a business meeting a clear need has a better chance.
> Overall, keeping customers interested is a major challenge for businesses.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a person you know who has a successful business](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%20Describe%20a%20person%20you%20know%20who%20has%20a%20successful%20business.md)

论点回查：[E16](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#16-how-can-successful-companies-contribute-to-society--the-local-economy) · [审计 P032](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p032)。

<a id="p034"></a>

### P034 · A｜Does having money necessarily mean being successful?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T05 经济与就业 ＋ T02 健康、习惯与休闲

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB12 主链 / 对照](#ab12)；辅助链：[AB04](#ab04)

**串法：** 可用创造实际价值、保持健康作为成功标准；金钱不是这些结果的充分条件。

**答案：**

> No, I don't think money is the only measure of success.
> **Creating something useful and maintaining good health are also meaningful achievements.**
> For example, someone running a small, useful local service may be successful without becoming extremely rich.
> That said, a stable income still matters in daily life.
> Overall, success involves more than the amount of money someone has.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

论点回查：[E16](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#16-how-can-successful-companies-contribute-to-society--the-local-economy)、[E39](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#3940-balanced-routines-and-health-均衡作息与健康)、[E40](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#3940-balanced-routines-and-health-均衡作息与健康) · [审计 P034](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p034)。

<a id="p046"></a>

### P046 · A｜How important are traditional handicrafts to a country's industry?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T05 经济与就业 ＋ T08 艺术、文化与传统

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB12 主链 / 对照](#ab12)；辅助链：[AB19](#ab19)

**串法：** 传统产品→销售收入与岗位，并保留文化价值；不夸大整个产业占比。

**答案：**

> Traditional handicrafts can make a useful contribution, especially to local businesses.
> **They provide products people can buy while keeping traditional skills in use.**
> For example, a small workshop can sell handmade baskets and provide work for local craftspeople.
> That said, handicrafts are only one part of a country's industry.
> Overall, they can support both livelihoods and cultural traditions.

<a id="p087"></a>

### P087 · A｜Why do some people start their own business?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T05 经济与就业

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q1

逻辑链：[本节 AB12 主链 / 对照](#ab12)

**串法：** 出售有用产品或服务→收入与就业；可把获得收入作为创业动机。

**答案：**

> Some people start a business because they see a chance to earn money by meeting a real need.
> **Useful products or services can attract customers and bring in revenue.**
> For example, someone may open a small repair service to help local residents fix everyday household items.
> So, turning a useful service into an income can motivate people to start businesses.

<a id="p088"></a>

### P088 · B｜Should governments provide financial support to start-ups?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T05 经济与就业 ＋ T04 公平、规则与公共资源

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB12 主链 / 对照](#ab12)；辅助链：[AB11](#ab11)

**串法：** 创业项目满足真实需求 → 资金帮助提供服务和岗位 → 可支持，但应核对用途和机会成本。

**答案：**

> Governments can support start-ups that have a clear plan to meet a real need.
> **Funding useful services can help a small business earn revenue and provide work**.
> For example, a software team helping small firms manage their records could use support to develop and test its service.
> That said, public money also has to cover other important needs.
> Overall, support should depend on a credible use for the money.

**学习提示：** 借用 Chen 的小企业软件服务场景；资助是建议，不声称他的企业实际获资助。

<a id="p090"></a>

### P090 · A｜What makes a business successful?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T05 经济与就业

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q1

逻辑链：[本节 AB12 主链 / 对照](#ab12)

**串法：** 满足真实需求→稳定销售收入→经营成功。

**答案：**

> A business is more likely to succeed when it provides something people actually need.
> **Useful products and services give customers a reason to buy, which brings the business revenue.**
> For example, a reliable local cleaning service can earn money by helping residents with a task they need done.
> So, creating real value for customers is central to business success.

<a id="p091"></a>

### P091 · A｜What makes a business fail?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T05 经济与就业

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q1

逻辑链：[本节 AB12 主链 / 对照](#ab12)

**串法：** 产品不符合需求→需求不稳→经营失败。

**答案：**

> A business can fail when its products or services do not match what people need.
> **Without enough demand, its income becomes unstable and it may struggle to keep operating.**
> For example, a shop may keep stocking products that very few of its customers want to buy.
> So, losing touch with customers' needs can put a business at risk.

### P113 · B｜Do you think the continued growth of large cities is a positive trend?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB12 主链 / 对照](#ab12)；辅助链：[AB11](#ab11)

**串法：** 城市发展增加服务需求 → 企业收入和岗位增加；公共设施也承受压力 → 评价有条件。

**答案：**

> The growth of large cities can be positive when it brings useful businesses and employment.
> **More demand for goods and services** can give people opportunities to earn a living.
> For example, small software consultancies can serve businesses operating in a growing city.
> However, public services may struggle when expansion outpaces available resources.
> Overall, growth is beneficial when jobs and essential services develop together.

> **Tourists pay for useful services such as accommodation, meals and transport, bringing money into the local economy.**

> So, attracting tourists can bring economic benefits.

> That said, tourist demand is not always stable.
> Overall, tourism can make a useful contribution to a city's economy.

> Overall, useful consumption can support the economy.

**串法：** 部分更高职位提供实际收入或发展空间 → 满足生活和职业需要 → 愿意争取。

**答案：**

> Some young people want higher positions because those roles can offer better income or opportunities to develop.
> **A role that helps them earn a stable living and use their skills** may fit their longer-term goals.
> For example, an experienced developer might seek a role with better pay and responsibility for a useful project.
> That said, a higher title doesn’t guarantee better conditions.
> So, the attraction depends on what the position actually offers.

<a id="ab13"></a>

## AB13 减少时间精力 → 便利；过度代劳 → 依赖

**怎么串：** 比较线上线下时给两侧；涉及 AI 学习时保留主动尝试。不能把“科技发达”本身当作原因。

- **主链：** save time and effort → complete tasks more easily → improve efficiency
- **反向 / 对照：** excessive convenience → put in less active effort → greater dependence

| 原题 | 等级 | 这题必须补出的桥梁 |
|---|---|---|
| [P048](#p048) | B | 过去手工处理部分重复步骤 → 现在工具可辅助 → 节省时间；手艺人的判断仍需要保留。 |
| [P058](#p058) | B | 过去联系受距离限制时较不方便 → 现在远程交流可减少等待与奔波 → 只评价便利的这一面。 |
| [P095](#p095) | A | 远程消息减少联系障碍 → 易于分享近况 → 有利维持联系；过度使用也可能挤占当面相处。 |
| [P098](#p098) | A | 跨城即时交换消息 → 相比等待见面更易保持联系 → 友谊维持方式改变。 |
| [P170](#p170) | B | 线上免出行节省时间，线下可亲自检查 → 便利与信息获取不同 → 根据购买需要选择。 |
| [P171](#p171) | A | 线上省时→效率；信息不可靠或数据滥用→错误选择/不信任，选择一组利弊。 |
| [P201](#p201) | A | 省时高效与减少主动投入→依赖，已练完整利弊。 |
| [P202](#p202) | A | 学会使用工具→效率；保留独立练习→避免依赖。 |
| [P203](#p203) | A | 适度使用提高效率，独立练习避免能力弱化；用儿童学习例子。 |
| [P204](#p204) | A | 自动化省时省力→任务更高效；软件开发例子可直接使用。 |
| [P205](#p205) | A | 过度便利→减少主动努力→依赖与独立思考弱化；限定部分学生。 |
| [P233](#p233) | A | 快捷获取赛事、省时省力→观看方便；换观看场景即可。 |
| [P255](#p255) | B | 在家观看省出行，影院提供专门设施 → 便利与观影环境不同 → 双侧比较。 |
| [P309](#p309) | B | 家庭传统延续，而当前日常也可选择方便的外卖 → 获取方式改变但并非旧饮食全消失。 |
| [P310](#p310) | B | 外出就餐省准备，家中一起做饭有共享过程 → 不同场合偏好不同。 |

<a id="p048"></a>

### P048 · B｜How does modern technology change the handicraft industry?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响 ＋ T05 经济与就业

机制：M02 时间与精力成本

题型：Q3

逻辑链：[本节 AB13 主链 / 对照](#ab13)

**串法：** 过去手工处理部分重复步骤 → 现在工具可辅助 → 节省时间；手艺人的判断仍需要保留。

**答案：**

> Modern technology can make some parts of handicraft work quicker than before.
> **Tools can assist with repetitive preparation**, leaving makers more time for the parts that need their own judgement.
> For example, a craftsperson might prepare a repeated pattern digitally instead of drawing every copy from scratch.
> So, technology can change the process without removing every element of handwork.

**学习提示：** 工具仅作常识性示意，不声称某行业普遍采用或完全机械化。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a person who likes to make things by hand (e.g. toys, furniture)](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20person%20who%20likes%20to%20make%20things%20by%20hand%20%28e.g.%20toys%2C%20furniture%29.md) · [Describe a person you know who has a successful business](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%20Describe%20a%20person%20you%20know%20who%20has%20a%20successful%20business.md)

论点回查：[E2](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#2-technology--efficiency-and-over-reliance-科技提高效率--过度依赖)、[E3](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#3-technology--internet-reshaping-habits-互联网重塑习惯)、[E16](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#16-how-can-successful-companies-contribute-to-society--the-local-economy) · [审计 P048](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p048)。

<a id="p058"></a>

### P058 · B｜Has the quality of life for old people improved compared to the past?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响 ＋ T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M02 时间与精力成本

题型：Q3

逻辑链：[本节 AB13 主链 / 对照](#ab13)；辅助链：[AB07](#ab07)

**串法：** 过去联系受距离限制时较不方便 → 现在远程交流可减少等待与奔波 → 只评价便利的这一面。

**答案：**

> In some ways, life can be more convenient for older people now, especially in keeping contact with distant family.
> **Quick communication can make asking for help easier** than depending entirely on the next visit.
> For example, a parent in Zhanjiang can discuss a problem with a child in Beijing without travelling to meet them.
> That said, easier contact doesn’t mean every part of life has improved.
> Overall, communication can improve convenience, but it isn’t the whole picture of quality of life.

**学习提示：** 借用异地家庭与联系素材作有限比较；没有使用网页上的养老服务数据。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a friend from your childhood](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20friend%20from%20your%20childhood.md) · [Describe a person who loves to grow plants (e.g. vegetables, flowers) at home or in the garden](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20person%20who%20loves%20to%20grow%20plants%20%28e.g.%20vegetables%2C%20flowers%29%20at%20home%20or%20in%20the%20garden.md)

论点回查：[E21](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#21-how-should-governments-spend-public-money)、[E57](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#57-how-can-governments-support-peoples-healthwell-being) · [审计 P058](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p058)。

<a id="p095"></a>

### P095 · A｜What do you think of communicating with others through social media?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响 ＋ T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M02 时间与精力成本

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB13 主链 / 对照](#ab13)；辅助链：[AB07](#ab07)

**串法：** 远程消息减少联系障碍 → 易于分享近况 → 有利维持联系；过度使用也可能挤占当面相处。

**答案：**

> I think social media is a useful way to keep in touch.
> **It allows people to exchange messages quickly even when they cannot meet in person.**
> For example, friends living in different cities can share updates and arrange their next visit through a group chat.
> However, constant phone use can reduce the time they spend interacting face to face.
> Overall, social media works best when it supports real relationships.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

<a id="p098"></a>

### P098 · A｜Has technology changed people's friendships? How?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响 ＋ T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M02 时间与精力成本

题型：Q3

逻辑链：[本节 AB13 主链 / 对照](#ab13)；辅助链：[AB07](#ab07)

**串法：** 跨城即时交换消息 → 相比等待见面更易保持联系 → 友谊维持方式改变。

**答案：**

> Yes, technology has made it easier for friends to stay in contact across distances.
> **Instant messages allow regular updates, although constant phone use can also reduce real interaction.**
> For example, friends in different cities can exchange news daily instead of waiting until their next visit.
> So, technology has changed how friendships are maintained, with both benefits and limits.

<a id="p170"></a>

### P170 · B｜What are the differences between online shopping and in-store shopping?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响

机制：M02 时间与精力成本

题型：Q2

逻辑链：[本节 AB13 主链 / 对照](#ab13)；辅助链：[AB14](#ab14)

**串法：** 线上免出行节省时间，线下可亲自检查 → 便利与信息获取不同 → 根据购买需要选择。

**答案：**

> Online shopping can save a trip, while in-store shopping lets people examine an item directly.
> **Convenience and the chance to check details** are the main trade-off.
> For example, ordering a laptop from home is easy, but visiting a shop allows someone to test the keyboard before choosing.
> So, online shopping saves effort, while a store can offer useful first-hand information.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when you received good service from a staff member in a shop](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20received%20good%20service%20from%20a%20staff%20member%20in%20a%20shop.md)

论点回查：[E2](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#2-technology--efficiency-and-over-reliance-科技提高效率--过度依赖)、[E15](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#15-why-should-consumers-fact-check-advertisements) · [审计 P170](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p170)。

<a id="p171"></a>

### P171 · A｜What are the advantages and disadvantages of shopping online?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响

机制：M02 时间与精力成本

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB13 主链 / 对照](#ab13)；辅助链：[AB14](#ab14)

**串法：** 线上省时→效率；信息不可靠或数据滥用→错误选择/不信任，选择一组利弊。

**答案：**

> Online shopping can save people time and effort, but the information they see is not always reliable.
> **They can complete a purchase easily without having to visit a shop.**
> For example, someone can order a household item from home instead of making a separate shopping trip.
> However, an exaggerated product description may lead them to buy something unsuitable.
> Overall, online shopping is convenient, but it requires careful judgement.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

论点回查：[E2](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#2-technology--efficiency-and-over-reliance-科技提高效率--过度依赖)、[E15](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#15-why-should-consumers-fact-check-advertisements)、[E52](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#52-how-can-technology-affect-peoples-privacytrust)、[E53](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#53-technologyprivacy--developed-version) · [审计 P171](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p171)。

<a id="p201"></a>

### P201 · A｜What are the advantages and disadvantages of AI?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响

机制：M06 长期能力与短期便利

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB13 主链 / 对照](#ab13)

**串法：** 省时高效与减少主动投入→依赖，已练完整利弊。

**答案：**

> AI can save people time and effort, but it can also make them overly dependent on assistance.
> **Automated tools help people complete tasks more quickly and easily.**
> For example, a software developer can use AI to help with a routine coding task and finish it sooner.
> However, relying on it for every step can reduce independent practice and weaken skills.
> Overall, AI is useful when people remain actively involved in their work.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a challenging technological problem you faced](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20challenging%20technological%20problem%20you%20faced.md) · [Describe a person who is good at learning and speaking new languages](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20person%20who%20is%20good%20at%20learning%20and%20speaking%20new%20languages.md)

论点回查：[E2](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#2-technology--efficiency-and-over-reliance-科技提高效率--过度依赖) · [审计 P201](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p201)。

<a id="p202"></a>

### P202 · A｜Do you think people today should learn about AI technology?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响 ＋ T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M06 长期能力与短期便利

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB13 主链 / 对照](#ab13)；辅助链：[AB01](#ab01)

**串法：** 学会使用工具→效率；保留独立练习→避免依赖。

**答案：**

> Yes, I think people should learn enough about AI to use it sensibly.
> **Learning how to work with these tools can help them complete tasks more efficiently.**
> For example, an employee can practise using an AI tool to help organise a draft while reviewing the result themselves.
> That said, people still need to develop their own skills.
> Overall, learning about AI can be useful alongside independent practice.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

<a id="p203"></a>

### P203 · A｜Should children learn to use AI?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响 ＋ T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M06 长期能力与短期便利

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB13 主链 / 对照](#ab13)；辅助链：[AB01](#ab01)

**串法：** 适度使用提高效率，独立练习避免能力弱化；用儿童学习例子。

**答案：**

> Yes, children can learn to use AI, but they should still do their own thinking.
> **Too much assistance can reduce the practice they need to build stronger skills.**
> For example, a child can attempt a question first and then use an AI explanation to review their answer.
> That said, AI should not complete all their schoolwork for them.
> Overall, children should learn to use AI as support for their own efforts.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

论点回查：[E2](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#2-technology--efficiency-and-over-reliance-科技提高效率--过度依赖)、[E22](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#22-what-is-the-most-important-factor-in-learning-well) · [审计 P203](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p203)。

### P204 · A｜How can AI help in our lives?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响

机制：M02 时间与精力成本

题型：Q6

**串法：** 自动化省时省力→任务更高效；软件开发例子可直接使用。

**答案：**

> AI can help people complete everyday work with less time and effort.
> **Automated assistance can make routine parts of a task easier to manage.**
> For example, a software engineer can use an AI tool to help draft a small piece of code more quickly.
> So, AI can be a useful way to improve efficiency.

<a id="p205"></a>

### P205 · A｜Do you think students are overly reliant on AI?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响 ＋ T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M06 长期能力与短期便利

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB13 主链 / 对照](#ab13)；辅助链：[AB01](#ab01)

**串法：** 过度便利→减少主动努力→依赖与独立思考弱化；限定部分学生。

**答案：**

> I think some students are overly reliant on AI.
> **When they let a tool do most of the work, they put in less active effort and get less practice.**
> For example, a student who always copies an AI answer may avoid working through the question independently.
> That said, occasional help is not the same as complete dependence.
> Overall, the problem is replacing independent effort with constant assistance.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a challenging technological problem you faced](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20challenging%20technological%20problem%20you%20faced.md) · [Describe a person who is good at learning and speaking new languages](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20person%20who%20is%20good%20at%20learning%20and%20speaking%20new%20languages.md)

<a id="p233"></a>

### P233 · A｜What are the advantages of watching sports events online?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响 ＋ T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M02 时间与精力成本

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB13 主链 / 对照](#ab13)；辅助链：[AB07](#ab07)

**串法：** 快捷获取赛事、省时省力→观看方便；换观看场景即可。

**答案：**

> Watching sports online can be convenient and save time.
> **People can access a match without making a separate trip to a place showing it.**
> For example, someone can watch a game at home after work instead of travelling across town to see it elsewhere.
> That said, watching alone offers less shared time with other fans.
> Overall, online viewing is a practical way to fit sport into daily life.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a challenging technological problem you faced](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20challenging%20technological%20problem%20you%20faced.md) · [Describe a person who is good at learning and speaking new languages](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20person%20who%20is%20good%20at%20learning%20and%20speaking%20new%20languages.md)

论点回查：[E2](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#2-technology--efficiency-and-over-reliance-科技提高效率--过度依赖)、[E3](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#3-technology--internet-reshaping-habits-互联网重塑习惯) · [审计 P233](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p233)。

<a id="p255"></a>

### P255 · B｜What are the differences between watching movies at home and in a cinema?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响 ＋ T10 地点、建筑与设施

机制：M02 时间与精力成本

题型：Q2

逻辑链：[本节 AB13 主链 / 对照](#ab13)；辅助链：[AB21](#ab21)

**串法：** 在家观看省出行，影院提供专门设施 → 便利与观影环境不同 → 双侧比较。

**答案：**

> Watching at home is convenient, while a cinema provides a space designed specifically for films.
> **Avoiding a journey saves effort**, whereas cinema seating and a large screen may make the setting more appealing.
> For example, someone can start a film after work at home, while a cinema visit involves a separate trip for that experience.
> So, the choice depends on whether convenience or the viewing setting matters more.

**学习提示：** 家庭观影来自原素材，影院条件是常识性比较，不捏造个人影院经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe your least favourite movie](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20your%20least%20favourite%20movie.md)

论点回查：[E2](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#2-technology--efficiency-and-over-reliance-科技提高效率--过度依赖)、[E4](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#4-building-design)、[E34](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#34-why-real-interaction-builds-relationships-真实互动如何建立关系) · [审计 P255](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p255)。

<a id="p309"></a>

### P309 · B｜Are there any differences between the food people eat today and the food people ate in the past?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响 ＋ T08 艺术、文化与传统

机制：M02 时间与精力成本

题型：Q3

逻辑链：[本节 AB13 主链 / 对照](#ab13)；辅助链：[AB19](#ab19)

**串法：** 家庭传统延续，而当前日常也可选择方便的外卖 → 获取方式改变但并非旧饮食全消失。

**答案：**

> I wouldn’t describe today’s food as completely different from food in the past.
> **Convenient ways of getting meals can change daily choices**, while familiar family traditions continue.
> For example, takeaway meals have featured in my adult routine, but my family still makes dumplings together for Lunar New Year.
> So, changes in convenience can exist alongside continuity in traditional food.

**学习提示：** 只比较个人日常和延续的家庭传统；不虚构全国饮食结构的历史数据。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when you saved money to buy something you wanted](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20saved%20money%20to%20buy%20something%20you%20wanted.md) · [Describe a kind of food that you ate at special occasions](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20kind%20of%20food%20that%20you%20ate%20at%20special%20occasions.md)

论点回查：[E3](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#3-technology--internet-reshaping-habits-互联网重塑习惯)、[E9](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#9-historical-sites-and-cultural-heritage) · [审计 P309](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p309)。

<a id="p310"></a>

### P310 · B｜Do people today prefer eating at home or in a restaurant?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响 ＋ T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M02 时间与精力成本

题型：Q2

逻辑链：[本节 AB13 主链 / 对照](#ab13)；辅助链：[AB07](#ab07)

**串法：** 外出就餐省准备，家中一起做饭有共享过程 → 不同场合偏好不同。

**答案：**

> I don’t think people always prefer either home or a restaurant.
> **Eating out can reduce preparation**, while cooking at home can give family or friends meaningful time together.
> For example, I might go to a mall for dinner with Stella but enjoy making dumplings with relatives during a festival.
> So, the occasion and the experience people want can change their preference.

**学习提示：** 例子取自下列 Part 2 素材或其明确标示的情境迁移；不据此推断全国比例。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a crowded place you went to](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20crowded%20place%20you%20went%20to.md) · [Describe a kind of food that you ate at special occasions](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20kind%20of%20food%20that%20you%20ate%20at%20special%20occasions.md)

论点回查：[E2](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#2-technology--efficiency-and-over-reliance-科技提高效率--过度依赖)、[E34](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#34-why-real-interaction-builds-relationships-真实互动如何建立关系) · [审计 P310](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p310)。

<a id="ab14"></a>

## AB14 核实信息并比较 → 更合适的判断

**怎么串：** 用电脑、岗位或课程选择说明核对什么、怎么比较。决定前的判断和决定后的执行计划分清。

- **主链：** reliable information from varied sources → informed judgement → better decisions
- **反向 / 对照：** misinformation or overload → distorted judgement → poor choices

| 原题 | 等级 | 这题必须补出的桥梁 |
|---|---|---|
| [P186](#p186) | B | 了解岗位要求 → 选相关经历并练习解释 → 面试时能清楚展示匹配。 |
| [P194](#p194) | A | 核实信息、比较优先级→较好决定；日常小事可加轻量快速决策限定。 |
| [P196](#p196) | B | 核对新旧岗位的真实待遇、工作和成长条件 → 比较是否符合需要 → 换工作好坏取决于匹配。 |
| [P197](#p197) | B | 晋升可能伴随不同待遇与责任 → 核对实际条件 → 判断是否符合本人需要。 |
| [P208](#p208) | B | 正在处理事情或回复需要查证 → 暂时无法提供可靠答案 → 不立即回复。 |
| [P228](#p228) | B | 家庭与学校问题不同 → 父母和老师掌握的相关经验不同 → 按问题选意见来源。 |
| [P248](#p248) | B | 儿童也可能面临影响较久的学校选择 → 需要理解选项并求助 → 有时确实要做重要决定。 |
| [P249](#p249) | B | 毕业后继续学习、培训或工作 → 核对各自要求和本人目标 → 作下一步选择。 |
| [P253](#p253) | A | 搜集可靠信息、比较重要条件→作出选择；再安排执行，区分判断与计划。 |
| [P266](#p266) | B | 本地新闻服务身边决策，全国新闻提供更广背景 → 根据所需信息选择 → 不概括全国偏好。 |
| [P286](#p286) | A | 选择可靠、清楚的信息作为最重要因素→合理判断与信任。 |

<a id="p186"></a>

### P186 · B｜What should people do to prepare for a job interview?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响 ＋ T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q6

逻辑链：[本节 AB14 主链 / 对照](#ab14)；辅助链：[AB01](#ab01)

**串法：** 了解岗位要求 → 选相关经历并练习解释 → 面试时能清楚展示匹配。

**答案：**

> People should study the job requirements and prepare examples that relate to them.
> **Reliable information about the role** helps them decide which experience to explain, and practice helps them explain it clearly.
> For example, a software applicant could review one project and rehearse discussing the problem they solved and their own contribution.
> So, useful preparation combines understanding the job with explaining relevant evidence.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe an important decision that you made](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20an%20important%20decision%20that%20you%20made.md) · [Describe a time when you worked in a group](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20worked%20in%20a%20group.md)

论点回查：[E22](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#22-what-is-the-most-important-factor-in-learning-well)、[E27](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#27-what-helps-people-make-an-informed-decision--version-2--example)、[E15](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#15-why-should-consumers-fact-check-advertisements) · [审计 P186](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p186)。

<a id="p194"></a>

### P194 · A｜Is it better to make a decision quickly or to take time to think it through?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响 ＋ T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q2

逻辑链：[本节 AB14 主链 / 对照](#ab14)；辅助链：[AB03](#ab03)

**串法：** 核实信息、比较优先级→较好决定；日常小事可加轻量快速决策限定。

**答案：**

> For an important decision, I would usually take time to think it through rather than decide immediately.
> **A quick choice allows less checking, while careful comparison helps people understand the information and their priorities.**
> For example, someone choosing between two jobs can compare the salary, location and growth opportunities before accepting an offer.
> So, important choices usually deserve some careful thought.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when you received good service from a staff member in a shop](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20received%20good%20service%20from%20a%20staff%20member%20in%20a%20shop.md) · [Describe an advertisement with a famous person in it](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20an%20advertisement%20with%20a%20famous%20person%20in%20it.md)

论点回查：[E15](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#15-why-should-consumers-fact-check-advertisements)、[E27](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#27-what-helps-people-make-an-informed-decision--version-2--example) · [审计 P194](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p194)。

<a id="p196"></a>

### P196 · B｜Do you think it is good to change jobs?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响 ＋ T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB14 主链 / 对照](#ab14)

**串法：** 核对新旧岗位的真实待遇、工作和成长条件 → 比较是否符合需要 → 换工作好坏取决于匹配。

**答案：**

> Changing jobs can be good, but it depends on the actual options.
> **Comparing the duties, location and opportunities carefully** helps someone judge whether a new role is a better fit.
> For example, a software engineer could compare two offers instead of assuming any new job will improve their situation.
> That said, a different job isn’t automatically a better one.
> Overall, the decision should follow a realistic comparison.

**学习提示：** 不把换工作等同失业，也不假设新岗位一定培训或加薪。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe an important decision that you made](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20an%20important%20decision%20that%20you%20made.md) · [Describe a long-term goal or ambition you would like to achieve](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20long-term%20goal%20or%20ambition%20you%20would%20like%20to%20achieve.md)

论点回查：[E27](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#27-what-helps-people-make-an-informed-decision--version-2--example)、[E55](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#55-how-can-losing-a-job-affect-peoples-standard-of-living) · [审计 P196](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p196)。

<a id="p197"></a>

### P197 · B｜Is it good for people to get a job promotion?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响 ＋ T05 经济与就业

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB14 主链 / 对照](#ab14)

**串法：** 晋升可能伴随不同待遇与责任 → 核对实际条件 → 判断是否符合本人需要。

**答案：**

> A promotion can be good, but I’d look at what it actually changes.
> **The new duties and rewards need to fit the person’s needs**, rather than the title alone making it worthwhile.
> For example, someone could compare the extra responsibility with the pay and the amount of time the role requires.
> That said, greater responsibility can also bring extra pressure.
> Overall, a promotion is valuable when the overall change is a suitable one.

**学习提示：** 具体晋升待遇未在素材中提供，采用评价标准，不声称所有晋升加薪。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a well-organized person you know](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20well-organized%20person%20you%20know.md) · [Describe a long-term goal or ambition you would like to achieve](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20long-term%20goal%20or%20ambition%20you%20would%20like%20to%20achieve.md)

论点回查：[E55](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#55-how-can-losing-a-job-affect-peoples-standard-of-living)、[E50](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#5051-constant-pressure-and-well-being-持续压力与身心健康)、[E51](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#5051-constant-pressure-and-well-being-持续压力与身心健康) · [审计 P197](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p197)。

<a id="p208"></a>

### P208 · B｜In what situations do people not respond to messages right away?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响 ＋ T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB14 主链 / 对照](#ab14)

**串法：** 正在处理事情或回复需要查证 → 暂时无法提供可靠答案 → 不立即回复。

**答案：**

> People may wait when they are busy or need to check something before answering.
> **A useful reply sometimes requires information they don’t yet have**.
> For example, a colleague asked about a technical detail may need to inspect the project before sending a clear answer.
> So, not replying immediately can be a reasonable part of giving an accurate response.

**学习提示：** 技术查证为从项目消息素材迁移的假设；不改写 Stella 实际迟回的原因。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when you sent a message or an email to someone but received no reply for a long time](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20sent%20a%20message%20or%20an%20email%20to%20someone%20but%20received%20no%20reply%20for%20a%20long%20time.md)

论点回查：[E26](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#26-what-helps-people-make-an-informed-decision--version-1)、[E31](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#31-effects-of-unclear-communication-沟通不清的影响) · [审计 P208](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p208)。

<a id="p228"></a>

### P228 · B｜Whose opinions are more important to children, their parents' or teachers'?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响 ＋ T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q2

逻辑链：[本节 AB14 主链 / 对照](#ab14)

**串法：** 家庭与学校问题不同 → 父母和老师掌握的相关经验不同 → 按问题选意见来源。

**答案：**

> Parents’ and teachers’ opinions can matter in different situations.
> **Relevant experience is more useful than giving one role automatic priority**.
> For example, a teacher may explain course choices well, while a parent may better understand a child’s responsibilities at home.
> So, whose opinion matters more depends on the decision the child is facing.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when a person did something to help you solve a problem](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20a%20person%20did%20something%20to%20help%20you%20solve%20a%20problem.md) · [Describe a person who taught you a new skill](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20person%20who%20taught%20you%20a%20new%20skill.md)

论点回查：[E30](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#30-example-of-active-listening-积极倾听的例子)、[E5](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#5-older-vs-younger-people) · [审计 P228](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p228)。

<a id="p248"></a>

### P248 · B｜Do you think children sometimes have to make important decisions?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响 ＋ T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB14 主链 / 对照](#ab14)

**串法：** 儿童也可能面临影响较久的学校选择 → 需要理解选项并求助 → 有时确实要做重要决定。

**答案：**

> Yes, children sometimes face decisions that matter to their daily lives or learning.
> **Understanding the options with suitable advice** helps them make a choice rather than guess.
> For example, a child choosing a school activity for the coming term could discuss the commitment with a parent and teacher.
> That said, adults should help with choices beyond a child’s experience.
> Overall, children can make meaningful decisions with appropriate support.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。
