<a id="p249"></a>

### P249 · B｜What important decisions do teenagers need to make after graduation?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响 ＋ T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB14 主链 / 对照](#ab14)

**串法：** 毕业后继续学习、培训或工作 → 核对各自要求和本人目标 → 作下一步选择。

**答案：**

> Teenagers may need to choose between further study, training and looking for work.
> **Comparing what each option involves** helps them choose a route that fits their goals.
> For example, a school leaver interested in technology could compare a course with a training opportunity before deciding.
> So, graduation often brings choices about the next stage of learning or work.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

论点回查：[E27](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#27-what-helps-people-make-an-informed-decision--version-2--example) · [审计 P249](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p249)。

<a id="p253"></a>

### P253 · A｜How do people usually make important decision?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响 ＋ T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q6

逻辑链：[本节 AB14 主链 / 对照](#ab14)；辅助链：[AB03](#ab03)

**串法：** 搜集可靠信息、比较重要条件→作出选择；再安排执行，区分判断与计划。

**答案：**

> People usually make important decisions by collecting information and comparing what matters most to them.
> **Reliable sources and clear priorities can help them choose an option that fits their needs.**
> For example, someone comparing two job offers can check the salary, location and expected work before deciding.
> So, careful information gathering and comparison can lead to better decisions.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

论点回查：[E15](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#15-why-should-consumers-fact-check-advertisements)、[E27](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#27-what-helps-people-make-an-informed-decision--version-2--example) · [审计 P253](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p253)。

<a id="p266"></a>

### P266 · B｜Do people in your country prefer following local news or national news?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响 ＋ T09 年龄与代际

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q2

逻辑链：[本节 AB14 主链 / 对照](#ab14)

**串法：** 本地新闻服务身边决策，全国新闻提供更广背景 → 根据所需信息选择 → 不概括全国偏好。

**答案：**

> I wouldn’t assume people in China always prefer either local or national news.
> **The information they need at a particular moment** can make one more useful than the other.
> For example, a local beach report may matter when planning a visit, while wider news may help someone understand a broader issue.
> So, the preference can change with the purpose of following the news.

**学习提示：** 例子取自下列 Part 2 素材或其明确标示的情境迁移；不据此推断全国比例。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a piece of local news that people are interested in](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20piece%20of%20local%20news%20that%20people%20are%20interested%20in.md)

论点回查：[E5](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#5-older-vs-younger-people)、[E15](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#15-why-should-consumers-fact-check-advertisements) · [审计 P266](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p266)。

<a id="p286"></a>

### P286 · A｜What is the most important factor in an advertisement?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB14 主链 / 对照](#ab14)

**串法：** 选择可靠、清楚的信息作为最重要因素→合理判断与信任。

**答案：**

> I think reliable information is the most important factor in an advertisement.
> **People need a clear picture of the product to judge whether it meets their needs.**
> For example, an advertisement for a course should explain what learners will study rather than promise unrealistic results.
> That said, consumers should still check other sources.
> Overall, accurate information makes an advertisement more useful for making decisions.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

<a id="ab15"></a>

## AB15 曝光与榜样 → 关注、模仿和机会

**怎么串：** 本节取曝光与说服分支：谁被看见、谁受影响、产生什么选择。隐私压力单列 AB16，不因同属 T06 就混成一节。

- **主链：** visibility and credibility → greater public attention → wider influence or opportunity
- **反向 / 对照：** persuasive advertising or constant public exposure → less careful judgement or loss of privacy → misleading choices or stress

| 原题 | 等级 | 这题必须补出的桥梁 |
|---|---|---|
| [P007](#p007) | B | 过去主要接触身边受众 → 现在作品可以面向更大范围展示 → 更容易被注意，但不保证持久受欢迎。 |
| [P068](#p068) | B | 看到医生帮助他人 → 把医生当榜样 → 向往相同职业。 |
| [P154](#p154) | A | 有说服力的广告诱导购买非必需品 → 浪费钱和资源 → 鼓励谨慎消费。 |
| [P181](#p181) | B | 运动员等公众人物和相关专家 → 受众关注其经历或信息 → 获得采访机会。 |
| [P184](#p184) | B | 公众关注作品、赛事或经历 → 名人谈准备、困难和选择 → 满足受众对具体经历的关注。 |
| [P251](#p251) | A | 说服与名人示范→购买超出需要→影响选择。 |
| [P252](#p252) | A | 曝光带来机会；夸大诱导→差选择，核实信息作限定。 |
| [P284](#p284) | A | 曝光带来商业机会，夸大或盲从导致差选择；已有完整利弊。 |
| [P285](#p285) | A | 名人关注与粉丝模仿→购买影响；可用已练代言例子说明有效但有限。 |
| [P288](#p288) | A | 增加产品曝光→注意力与销售机会→企业收入。 |
| [P289](#p289) | B | 线上可接触远方目标受众，线下可触达当地人 → 看受众在哪里 → 曝光方式须匹配。 |

<a id="p007"></a>

### P007 · B｜Is it easier to become popular nowadays?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响

机制：M05 社会影响与模仿

题型：Q3

逻辑链：[本节 AB15 主链 / 对照](#ab15)

**串法：** 过去主要接触身边受众 → 现在作品可以面向更大范围展示 → 更容易被注意，但不保证持久受欢迎。

**答案：**

> In some ways, it’s easier to get noticed now than it was in the past.
> **Sharing work online can put it in front of people beyond someone’s immediate circle**.
> For example, a young singer can post a performance for distant listeners instead of relying only on a small local audience.
> That said, being noticed briefly doesn’t guarantee lasting popularity.
> So, the opportunity to reach people is greater, but popularity still takes more than exposure.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a popular person](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20popular%20person.md)

论点回查：[E3](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#3-technology--internet-reshaping-habits-互联网重塑习惯)、[E14](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#14-what-are-the-advantages-and-disadvantages-of-advertising) · [审计 P007](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p007)。

<a id="p068"></a>

### P068 · B｜Why do some children want to become doctors?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响 ＋ T02 健康、习惯与休闲

机制：M05 社会影响与模仿

题型：Q1

逻辑链：[本节 AB15 主链 / 对照](#ab15)

**串法：** 看到医生帮助他人 → 把医生当榜样 → 向往相同职业。

**答案：**

> Some children want to become doctors because they admire a doctor they have seen helping someone.
> **A positive role model can shape what they want to do themselves**.
> For example, a child who sees a doctor caring for a relative might want to offer similar help in the future.
> So, admiration for a doctor can develop into interest in the profession.

**学习提示：** 使用 M05 榜样模仿分支；不把“医疗重要”当作儿童择业的直接原因。

<a id="p154"></a>

### P154 · A｜Should parents encourage their children to spend money freely or to be careful with it?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T07 环境与动物 ＋ T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB15 主链 / 对照](#ab15)；辅助链：[AB17](#ab17)

**串法：** 有说服力的广告诱导购买非必需品 → 浪费钱和资源 → 鼓励谨慎消费。

**答案：**

> I think parents should encourage children to be careful with money.
> **Persuasive advertisements can encourage them to buy unnecessary things, wasting money and resources.**
> For example, a child may spend all their pocket money on an advertised toy they do not really want.
> That said, careful spending does not mean never buying something enjoyable.
> Overall, children should learn to spend within their needs and means.

**串法：** 运动员等公众人物和相关专家 → 受众关注其经历或信息 → 获得采访机会。

**答案：**

> Public figures and people with relevant expertise are common interview subjects.
> **Their work or experience attracts attention**, giving an audience a reason to hear from them.
> For example, a badminton player may be interviewed after an important match to explain how they approached it.
> So, people are often interviewed because others want to understand their experience.

<a id="p184"></a>

### P184 · B｜What kinds of things do famous people often say in an interview?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响

机制：M05 社会影响与模仿

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB15 主链 / 对照](#ab15)

**串法：** 公众关注作品、赛事或经历 → 名人谈准备、困难和选择 → 满足受众对具体经历的关注。

**答案：**

> Famous people may talk about their work, recent achievements and the difficulties they faced.
> **Explaining a particular experience** gives the audience more than a list of results.
> For example, a badminton player might describe changing tactics after losing the first game of a final.
> So, interviews often focus on what happened and how the person responded.

<a id="p251"></a>

### P251 · A｜Do you think advertisements can influence our decisions when shopping?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响

机制：M05 社会影响与模仿

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB15 主链 / 对照](#ab15)

**串法：** 说服与名人示范→购买超出需要→影响选择。

**答案：**

> Yes, advertisements can influence what people decide to buy.
> **Persuasive messages and celebrity endorsements can encourage people to choose products they might not otherwise consider.**
> For example, a fan may buy shoes promoted by a favourite athlete even though they do not need another pair.
> That said, checking independent information can lead to a more careful choice.
> Overall, advertising can strongly influence shopping decisions without determining every purchase.

<a id="p252"></a>

### P252 · A｜Do you think the influence of advertising is good?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响

机制：M05 社会影响与模仿

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB15 主链 / 对照](#ab15)

**串法：** 曝光带来机会；夸大诱导→差选择，核实信息作限定。

**答案：**

> I think advertising can have both positive and negative effects.
> **It brings products to people's attention, but persuasive claims can also lead to less careful choices.**
> For example, someone may buy an item because an advertisement makes it sound better than it really is.
> That said, reliable information from other sources can help people judge the claims.
> Overall, advertising is more helpful when people consider it carefully.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe an advertisement with a famous person in it](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20an%20advertisement%20with%20a%20famous%20person%20in%20it.md) · [Describe a time you watched a famous person being interviewed](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20time%20you%20watched%20a%20famous%20person%20being%20interviewed.md)

论点回查：[E14](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#14-what-are-the-advantages-and-disadvantages-of-advertising)、[E15](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#15-why-should-consumers-fact-check-advertisements)、[E54](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#54-how-can-advertising-lead-to-excessive-consumption) · [审计 P252](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p252)。

<a id="p284"></a>

### P284 · A｜What are the advantages and disadvantages of advertisements?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响

机制：M05 社会影响与模仿

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB15 主链 / 对照](#ab15)

**串法：** 曝光带来商业机会，夸大或盲从导致差选择；已有完整利弊。

**答案：**

> Advertisements can help businesses attract attention, but they can also mislead consumers.
> **Making a product better known can give a company more opportunities to sell it.**
> For example, an advertisement can introduce a useful local service to people who had not heard of it before.
> However, exaggerated claims can encourage customers to buy things they do not really need.
> Overall, advertising has benefits, but consumers need to judge its claims carefully.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe an advertisement with a famous person in it](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20an%20advertisement%20with%20a%20famous%20person%20in%20it.md) · [Describe a time you watched a famous person being interviewed](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20time%20you%20watched%20a%20famous%20person%20being%20interviewed.md)

论点回查：[E14](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#14-what-are-the-advantages-and-disadvantages-of-advertising)、[E15](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#15-why-should-consumers-fact-check-advertisements)、[E54](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#54-how-can-advertising-lead-to-excessive-consumption) · [审计 P284](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p284)。

<a id="p285"></a>

### P285 · A｜Why are many advertisements endorsed by celebrities? How useful are they?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响

机制：M05 社会影响与模仿

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB15 主链 / 对照](#ab15)

**串法：** 名人关注与粉丝模仿→购买影响；可用已练代言例子说明有效但有限。

**答案：**

> Companies use celebrities because their popularity can attract attention and influence fans' choices.
> **A familiar public figure can make people more interested in the product being promoted.**
> For example, a fan may buy a drink because their favourite athlete appears in its advertisement.
> That said, celebrity support does not guarantee that the product suits the buyer.
> Overall, these advertisements can be influential, but their usefulness has limits.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe an advertisement with a famous person in it](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20an%20advertisement%20with%20a%20famous%20person%20in%20it.md) · [Describe a time you watched a famous person being interviewed](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20time%20you%20watched%20a%20famous%20person%20being%20interviewed.md)

论点回查：[E14](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#14-what-are-the-advantages-and-disadvantages-of-advertising)、[E54](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#54-how-can-advertising-lead-to-excessive-consumption) · [审计 P285](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p285)。

<a id="p288"></a>

### P288 · A｜Is advertising important for a company? Why?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响 ＋ T05 经济与就业

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB15 主链 / 对照](#ab15)；辅助链：[AB12](#ab12)

**串法：** 增加产品曝光→注意力与销售机会→企业收入。

**答案：**

> Yes, advertising can be important because it helps a company get noticed.
> **Greater attention can bring opportunities to sell useful products and earn revenue.**
> For example, a new cleaning service can advertise locally so residents know that the service is available.
> That said, advertising cannot replace a product or service people actually need.
> Overall, it can support a business by connecting its offer with potential customers.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe an advertisement with a famous person in it](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20an%20advertisement%20with%20a%20famous%20person%20in%20it.md) · [Describe a time you watched a famous person being interviewed](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20time%20you%20watched%20a%20famous%20person%20being%20interviewed.md)

论点回查：[E14](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#14-what-are-the-advantages-and-disadvantages-of-advertising)、[E16](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#16-how-can-successful-companies-contribute-to-society--the-local-economy) · [审计 P288](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p288)。

<a id="p289"></a>

### P289 · B｜Which is more effective, online advertising or offline advertising?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响

机制：M05 社会影响与模仿

题型：Q2

逻辑链：[本节 AB15 主链 / 对照](#ab15)

**串法：** 线上可接触远方目标受众，线下可触达当地人 → 看受众在哪里 → 曝光方式须匹配。

**答案：**

> Online advertising can reach people beyond one location, while offline advertising can be useful for a local audience.
> **The more effective choice is the one that reaches people likely to care about the offer**.
> For example, a sports-shoe video may reach fans online, while a nearby shop could use a notice where local customers pass.
> So, effectiveness depends on the audience rather than the channel alone.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe an advertisement with a famous person in it](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20an%20advertisement%20with%20a%20famous%20person%20in%20it.md) · [Describe a person you know who has a successful business](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%20Describe%20a%20person%20you%20know%20who%20has%20a%20successful%20business.md)

论点回查：[E3](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#3-technology--internet-reshaping-habits-互联网重塑习惯)、[E14](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#14-what-are-the-advantages-and-disadvantages-of-advertising) · [审计 P289](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p289)。

<a id="ab16"></a>

## AB16 过度公开私人信息 → 暴露感与压力

**怎么串：** 本节只取 T06 的公开曝光—隐私—压力分支。拍照和采访都要说明什么私人情境被暴露。

- **主链：** persuasive advertising or constant public exposure → less careful judgement or loss of privacy → misleading choices or stress

| 原题 | 等级 | 这题必须补出的桥梁 |
|---|---|---|
| [P023](#p023) | B | 私人场景被拍摄 → 当事人感到暴露 → 不应把所有场所都视作适合拍照。 |
| [P182](#p182) | A | 采访追问私人生活 → 感到暴露和压力 → 不愿被采访。 |

<a id="p023"></a>

### P023 · B｜Do you think it's ok to take photos everywhere?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB16 主链 / 对照](#ab16)

**串法：** 私人场景被拍摄 → 当事人感到暴露 → 不应把所有场所都视作适合拍照。

**答案：**

> No, I don’t think taking photos is appropriate everywhere.
> **Photographing people in private situations** can make them feel exposed and uncomfortable.
> For example, taking a close-up of a stranger resting in a hospital could intrude on a moment they want to keep private.
> That said, permission can make some situations more comfortable for everyone.
> Overall, the place and the people involved should guide the decision.

**学习提示：** 讨论隐私和礼貌，不把例子说成具体法律禁令。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time you watched a famous person being interviewed](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20time%20you%20watched%20a%20famous%20person%20being%20interviewed.md)

论点回查：[E52](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#52-how-can-technology-affect-peoples-privacytrust)、[E53](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#53-technologyprivacy--developed-version)、[E56](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#56-what-are-the-disadvantages-of-being-a-celebrity) · [审计 P023](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p023)。

<a id="p182"></a>

### P182 · A｜Why do some people dislike being interviewed?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q1

逻辑链：[本节 AB16 主链 / 对照](#ab16)；辅助链：[AB15](#ab15)

**串法：** 采访追问私人生活 → 感到暴露和压力 → 不愿被采访。

**答案：**

> Some people dislike interviews because they feel uncomfortable with constant public attention.
> **Questions about their private lives can make them feel exposed and stressed.**
> For example, a celebrity may feel overwhelmed when an interviewer keeps asking about private family matters.
> So, concerns about privacy can make people reluctant to be interviewed.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time you watched a famous person being interviewed](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20time%20you%20watched%20a%20famous%20person%20being%20interviewed.md)

论点回查：[E56](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#56-what-are-the-disadvantages-of-being-a-celebrity) · [审计 P182](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p182)。

<a id="ab17"></a>

## AB17 便利绿色选择、减少浪费 → 环境改善

**怎么串：** 替代用品或设施怎样改变行为必须明确。已有素材中的海岸塑料禁令是提议，不能据此回答现行法。

- **主链：** practical infrastructure and incentives → encourage greener choices → lower pollution and resource waste
- **反向 / 对照：** excessive consumption → waste and pollution → long-term environmental damage

| 原题 | 等级 | 这题必须补出的桥梁 |
|---|---|---|
| [P033](#p033) | A | 企业减少一次性用品或提供绿色选择→少浪费与污染。 |
| [P110](#p110) | B | 骑行设施更方便 → 骑车需要的时间精力降低 → 使用可能增加；设施差则限制预测。 |
| [P134](#p134) | B | 游客活动产生垃圾 → 清理压力和环境干扰增加 → 居民承担代价。 |
| [P306](#p306) | B | Part 2 只有希望颁布的海岸一次性塑料禁令；还缺至少一部本人知道的现行环保法律或规则及其实际要求。 |

<a id="p033"></a>

### P033 · A｜How can companies help protect the environment?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T07 环境与动物

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q6

逻辑链：[本节 AB17 主链 / 对照](#ab17)

**串法：** 企业减少一次性用品或提供绿色选择→少浪费与污染。

**答案：**

> Companies can help by reducing waste and making greener choices easier for customers.
> **Practical changes in daily operations can lower pollution and unnecessary resource use.**
> For example, a shop can encourage customers to bring reusable bags instead of handing out single-use plastic bags.
> So, businesses can protect the environment through small but consistent changes.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe an interesting video](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20an%20interesting%20video.md) · [Describe an environmental law you would like your country to introduce](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20an%20environmental%20law%20you%20would%20like%20your%20country%20to%20introduce.md)

论点回查：[E1](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#1-environmental-protection-环境保护)、[E54](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#54-how-can-advertising-lead-to-excessive-consumption) · [审计 P033](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p033)。

<a id="p110"></a>

### P110 · B｜Do you think people will use bicycles more or less frequently in cities in the future?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T07 环境与动物

机制：M02 时间与精力成本

题型：Q4

逻辑链：[本节 AB17 主链 / 对照](#ab17)

**串法：** 骑行设施更方便 → 骑车需要的时间精力降低 → 使用可能增加；设施差则限制预测。

**答案：**

> I think cycling could become more common in cities where practical routes make it easier.
> **Connected cycle lanes and convenient parking** can make a bicycle a more workable daily choice.
> For example, a commuter facing slow car journeys might switch if a direct cycling route becomes available.
> That said, inconvenient routes would discourage that change.
> So, future cycling depends partly on how well cities support it.

**学习提示：** 借用堵车通勤场景；车道改善是条件预测，不假装已经建设。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a short trip you often take but do not enjoy](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20short%20trip%20you%20often%20take%20but%20do%20not%20enjoy.md)

论点回查：[E1](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#1-environmental-protection-环境保护) · [审计 P110](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p110)。

<a id="p134"></a>

### P134 · B｜What are the disadvantages for people living in popular tourist cities?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T07 环境与动物 ＋ T04 公平、规则与公共资源

机制：M02 时间与精力成本

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB17 主链 / 对照](#ab17)

**串法：** 游客活动产生垃圾 → 清理压力和环境干扰增加 → 居民承担代价。

**答案：**

> One disadvantage is the waste that can build up around busy visitor areas.
> **Extra rubbish creates more cleaning work and can damage local surroundings**.
> For example, the coastal clean-up in my Zhanjiang news story followed a busy holiday that left plastic on the beach.
> That said, visitors who reduce their waste create less pressure.
> Overall, tourist areas need to manage the environmental costs of being popular.

**学习提示：** 引用现有地方新闻练习素材，不当作本轮核实的新闻报道。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a piece of local news that people are interested in](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20piece%20of%20local%20news%20that%20people%20are%20interested%20in.md)

论点回查：[E54](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#54-how-can-advertising-lead-to-excessive-consumption)、[E21](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#21-how-should-governments-spend-public-money) · [审计 P134](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p134)。

<a id="p306"></a>

### P306 · B｜What environmental laws does your country already have?

**状态：待补关键事实；暂不生成英文答案。**

主题：T07 环境与动物

机制：M02 时间与精力成本

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB17 主链 / 对照](#ab17)

**串法：** Part 2 只有希望颁布的海岸一次性塑料禁令；还缺至少一部本人知道的现行环保法律或规则及其实际要求。

**学习提示：** 当前素材不能自然覆盖原题所需事实；保留题目与缺口，不把假设写成现行事实。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe an environmental law you would like your country to introduce](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20an%20environmental%20law%20you%20would%20like%20your%20country%20to%20introduce.md)

论点回查：[E1](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#1-environmental-protection-环境保护)、[E54](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#54-how-can-advertising-lead-to-excessive-consumption) · [审计 P306](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p306)。

<a id="ab18"></a>

## AB18 照料与陪伴 → 情感支持和责任

**怎么串：** 宠物优势和照料负担放在同一节。Part 2 仅有动物故事与自然素材，具体养宠场景采用假设，不冒充已有养宠经历。

- **主链：** daily care and companionship → empathy and emotional support / build a strong bond with → stronger responsibility
- **反向 / 对照：** cost and long-term care needs → pressure on owners → possible neglect

| 原题 | 等级 | 这题必须补出的桥梁 |
|---|---|---|
| [P278](#p278) | B | 认识动物的日常需要 → 理解照料责任 → 可培养同理心，不要求每人养宠物。 |
| [P279](#p279) | A | 陪伴责任的好处与长期照料成本两面，城市场景加适当条件。 |
| [P280](#p280) | A | 日常照料与陪伴→情感纽带→视作家人。 |
| [P281](#p281) | B | 素材缺养宠数量观察 → 只作有限判断并解释陪伴价值 → 不声称有数量证据。 |
| [P282](#p282) | A | 陪伴→情感支持；日常照料→责任感。 |

<a id="p278"></a>

### P278 · B｜Should schools teach children about animals?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T07 环境与动物

机制：M03 自主权与责任感

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB18 主链 / 对照](#ab18)

**串法：** 认识动物的日常需要 → 理解照料责任 → 可培养同理心，不要求每人养宠物。

**答案：**

> Yes, schools can teach children about animals in a practical and considerate way.
> **Understanding that an animal has daily needs** can help them think about care and responsibility.
> For example, a class could discuss who would provide food, water and attention for an animal throughout the week.
> That said, learning about care doesn’t require every child to own a pet.
> Overall, animal lessons can encourage thoughtful responsibility.

**学习提示：** 从动物与自然素材作教育场景迁移，不编写学生实际养宠经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a story or book with animals in it](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20story%20or%20book%20with%20animals%20in%20it.md) · [Describe an interesting video](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20an%20interesting%20video.md)

论点回查：[E11](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#11-pets--emotional-support)、[E12](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#12-pets--responsibility) · [审计 P278](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p278)。

<a id="p279"></a>

### P279 · A｜Some people think pets should not be kept in cities. What do you think?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T07 环境与动物

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB18 主链 / 对照](#ab18)

**串法：** 陪伴责任的好处与长期照料成本两面，城市场景加适当条件。

**答案：**

> I don't think pets should be ruled out simply because their owners live in cities.
> **Daily care and companionship can give people emotional support and a stronger sense of responsibility.**
> For example, someone caring for a cat can build a close bond through feeding it and spending time with it every day.
> However, the cost and long-term care needs can put pressure on owners.
> Overall, keeping a pet should depend on the owner's ability to care for it.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a story or book with animals in it](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20story%20or%20book%20with%20animals%20in%20it.md) · [Describe an interesting video](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20an%20interesting%20video.md)

论点回查：[E11](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#11-pets--emotional-support)、[E12](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#12-pets--responsibility)、[E13](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#13-pets--drawback) · [审计 P279](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p279)。

<a id="p280"></a>

### P280 · A｜Many people regard pets as members of their family. What do you think?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T07 环境与动物

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB18 主链 / 对照](#ab18)

**串法：** 日常照料与陪伴→情感纽带→视作家人。

**答案：**

> I can understand why many people see pets as family members.
> **Daily care and companionship help owners build a strong bond with their animals.**
> For example, someone who feeds a dog and spends time with it every day may rely on that companionship for comfort.
> That said, people differ in how close they feel to animals.
> Overall, a lasting bond can make a pet feel like part of the family.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a story or book with animals in it](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20story%20or%20book%20with%20animals%20in%20it.md) · [Describe an interesting video](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20an%20interesting%20video.md)

论点回查：[E11](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#11-pets--emotional-support)、[E12](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#12-pets--responsibility) · [审计 P280](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p280)。

<a id="p281"></a>

### P281 · B｜Do many people keep pets in your country?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T07 环境与动物

机制：M03 自主权与责任感

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB18 主链 / 对照](#ab18)

**串法：** 素材缺养宠数量观察 → 只作有限判断并解释陪伴价值 → 不声称有数量证据。

**答案：**

> I think keeping pets is familiar in China, but I can’t confidently say how many people do it.
> **Daily companionship can make a pet feel important to its owner**.
> For example, someone who spends time caring for a cat each day may come to see it as part of the family.
> So, I can explain the appeal more confidently than estimate how common it is.

**学习提示：** Part 2 无养宠统计或个人养宠记录；本题为有限判断与假设例子，不作为已核实事实。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a story or book with animals in it](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20story%20or%20book%20with%20animals%20in%20it.md)

论点回查：[E11](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#11-pets--emotional-support)、[E13](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#13-pets--drawback) · [审计 P281](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p281)。

<a id="p282"></a>

### P282 · A｜What are the advantages of keeping a pet?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T07 环境与动物

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB18 主链 / 对照](#ab18)

**串法：** 陪伴→情感支持；日常照料→责任感。

**答案：**

> Keeping a pet can provide companionship and help people develop responsibility.
> **Looking after its daily needs builds a strong bond and gives owners a regular caring role.**
> For example, feeding and spending time with a cat can bring comfort while making its owner responsible for another living thing.
> That said, this responsibility continues even when the owner is busy.
> Overall, pets can offer emotional support alongside a meaningful commitment.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a story or book with animals in it](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20story%20or%20book%20with%20animals%20in%20it.md) · [Describe an interesting video](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20an%20interesting%20video.md)

论点回查：[E11](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#11-pets--emotional-support)、[E12](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#12-pets--responsibility) · [审计 P282](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p282)。

<a id="ab19"></a>

## AB19 参与文化与了解过去 → 理解和传承

**怎么串：** 旧建筑、历史、旅行与节日食物共用文化参与链。个人家里的习惯不自动代表家乡或全国。

- **主链：** participation and cultural exposure → stronger identity and understanding → pass down traditions in a living way
- **反向 / 对照：** commercialisation poses a threat to our traditional customs/  or dominant global content → weaker local engagement → cultural loss

| 原题 | 等级 | 这题必须补出的桥梁 |
|---|---|---|
| [P041](#p041) | A | 手工艺呈现传统→文化理解；有用产品销售→收入与就业。 |
| [P049](#p049) | B | Part 2 只写叔叔制作木制家具，未确认它是湛江传统工艺；还缺一种本人了解的家乡传统工艺及其本地联系。 |
| [P051](#p051) | A | 早期文化接触→了解传统与身份；举适合儿童的故事即可，不断言最佳年龄。 |
| [P052](#p052) | B | 能听懂简单故事时接触历史 → 认识过去生活 → 建立文化理解；内容按理解能力调整。 |
| [P053](#p053) | B | 家人讲故事、看旧照片或参观 → 接触具体过去生活 → 学前儿童形成初步历史理解。 |
| [P054](#p054) | A | 了解过去的生活→理解历史传统→身份与传承。 |
| [P056](#p056) | B | 普通生活、衣食和习俗 → 看到历史如何影响日常 → 更完整理解文化。 |
| [P099](#p099) | A | 历史建筑→文化记忆与身份→保存传统。 |
| [P100](#p100) | A | 文化价值与实际功能→决定保留或改造；不把所有旧建筑一概而论。 |
| [P126](#p126) | B | 列城市文化游与海边休闲两类 → 不同目的对应不同活动 → 用现有北京、海边素材。 |
| [P144](#p144) | B | 春节等特殊日子 → 家庭共同准备食物和相聚 → 传统在参与中延续。 |
| [P145](#p145) | B | 本人和 Stella 的海外旅行 → 接触不同文化和生活 → 部分人喜欢；不推全国比例。 |
| [P148](#p148) | B | 旅行亲身参与，书籍提供文字背景 → 理解方式不同 → 按想了解的内容选择。 |
| [P257](#p257) | A | 电影呈现当地生活与传统 → 观众获得文化背景 → 更好理解，但一部电影不能代表全国。 |
| [P269](#p269) | A | 理解共同历史传统→身份归属→文化延续。 |
| [P270](#p270) | A | 参与文化活动、了解遗产→增强身份理解。 |
| [P307](#p307) | A | 共同食物习俗→文化身份与家庭联系→传统延续。 |
| [P308](#p308) | B | 日常食物侧重一餐需要，节日食物有共同准备与传统意义 → 活动和意义不同。 |
| [P311](#p311) | B | 家庭中的饺子等熟悉食物 → 与共同习俗相关 → 举具体例子不概括全国最流行。 |

<a id="p041"></a>

### P041 · A｜Are traditional handicrafts important to tourism?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T08 艺术、文化与传统 ＋ T05 经济与就业

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB19 主链 / 对照](#ab19)；辅助链：[AB12](#ab12)

**串法：** 手工艺呈现传统→文化理解；有用产品销售→收入与就业。

**答案：**

> Yes, traditional handicrafts can be an important part of tourism.
> **They help visitors understand local traditions and can provide income for the people who make them.**
> For example, a visitor can buy a handmade bowl after seeing how a local craftsperson produces it.
> That said, not every visitor will be interested in handicrafts.
> Overall, these products can offer both cultural and economic value.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

论点回查：[E9](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#9-historical-sites-and-cultural-heritage)、[E16](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#16-how-can-successful-companies-contribute-to-society--the-local-economy) · [审计 P041](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p041)。

<a id="p051"></a>

### P051 · A｜Do you think children should begin learning history from an early age?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T08 艺术、文化与传统

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB19 主链 / 对照](#ab19)

**串法：** 早期文化接触→了解传统与身份；举适合儿童的故事即可，不断言最佳年龄。

**答案：**

> Yes, children can start learning simple history from an early age.
> **Learning about past lives and traditions can help them understand where their culture comes from.**
> For example, parents can take children to a local heritage site and explain one custom connected with it.
> That said, the material should be simple enough for children to understand.
> Overall, early history learning can build a basic connection with the past.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a kind of food that you ate at special occasions](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20kind%20of%20food%20that%20you%20ate%20at%20special%20occasions.md) · [Describe someone you know who enjoys learning about history](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20someone%20you%20know%20who%20enjoys%20learning%20about%20history.md)

论点回查：[E9](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#9-historical-sites-and-cultural-heritage)、[E10](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#10-heritage-sites--simplified-version) · [审计 P051](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p051)。

<a id="p052"></a>

### P052 · B｜At what age do you think people should begin to learn history?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T08 艺术、文化与传统

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB19 主链 / 对照](#ab19)

**串法：** 能听懂简单故事时接触历史 → 认识过去生活 → 建立文化理解；内容按理解能力调整。

**答案：**

> I think people can begin learning simple history before primary school.
> **Short stories about past lives and familiar traditions** can give children an accessible connection with the past.
> For example, a parent could explain how grandparents celebrated a festival using a few old family pictures.
> That said, there’s no need to start with complicated dates or political events.
> Overall, early history learning should begin simply and grow with the child.

**学习提示：** 这是教育建议，不把某一年龄说成科学统一门槛。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe someone you know who enjoys learning about history](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20someone%20you%20know%20who%20enjoys%20learning%20about%20history.md) · [Describe a kind of food that you ate at special occasions](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20kind%20of%20food%20that%20you%20ate%20at%20special%20occasions.md)

论点回查：[E9](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#9-historical-sites-and-cultural-heritage)、[E10](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#10-heritage-sites--simplified-version) · [审计 P052](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p052)。

<a id="p053"></a>

### P053 · B｜How do children learn history before they attend school?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T08 艺术、文化与传统

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB19 主链 / 对照](#ab19)

**串法：** 家人讲故事、看旧照片或参观 → 接触具体过去生活 → 学前儿童形成初步历史理解。

**答案：**

> Before school, children can learn history through family stories, old photographs and simple visits to heritage sites.
> **Connecting the past with people or places they recognise** can make it easier to understand.
> For example, a grandparent might use an old family photo to describe what home life was like years ago.
> So, everyday family activities can provide a first introduction to history.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe someone you know who enjoys learning about history](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20someone%20you%20know%20who%20enjoys%20learning%20about%20history.md) · [Describe a kind of food that you ate at special occasions](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20kind%20of%20food%20that%20you%20ate%20at%20special%20occasions.md)

论点回查：[E9](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#9-historical-sites-and-cultural-heritage)、[E10](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#10-heritage-sites--simplified-version) · [审计 P053](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p053)。

<a id="p054"></a>

### P054 · A｜Is it important to learn how people in the past lived their lives?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T08 艺术、文化与传统

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB19 主链 / 对照](#ab19)

**串法：** 了解过去的生活→理解历史传统→身份与传承。

**答案：**

> Yes, I think learning about ordinary life in the past is important.
> **It helps people understand their history and the traditions that have been passed down.**
> For example, learning how families celebrated a festival can explain why certain customs still matter today.
> That said, we do not need to copy every old custom.
> Overall, understanding past lives can give us a clearer sense of our culture.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

论点回查：[E9](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#9-historical-sites-and-cultural-heritage)、[E10](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#10-heritage-sites--simplified-version) · [审计 P054](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p054)。

<a id="p056"></a>

### P056 · B｜Besides big historical events, what else is important when learning history?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T08 艺术、文化与传统

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB19 主链 / 对照](#ab19)

**串法：** 普通生活、衣食和习俗 → 看到历史如何影响日常 → 更完整理解文化。

**答案：**

> Ordinary people’s lives and customs are important alongside major historical events.
> They help us **understand how the past shaped everyday life**, rather than just remembering dates and leaders.
> For example, learning how families prepared food can reveal traditions that still matter to their descendants.
> So, social customs and daily experiences deserve attention in history lessons too.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe someone you know who enjoys learning about history](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20someone%20you%20know%20who%20enjoys%20learning%20about%20history.md)

论点回查：[E9](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#9-historical-sites-and-cultural-heritage)、[E10](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#10-heritage-sites--simplified-version) · [审计 P056](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p056)。

<a id="p099"></a>

### P099 · A｜Do you think old buildings are valuable?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T08 艺术、文化与传统

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB19 主链 / 对照](#ab19)

**串法：** 历史建筑→文化记忆与身份→保存传统。

**答案：**

> Yes, some old buildings are valuable because they connect people with local history.
> **They can remind us of traditional ways of life and help us understand our cultural identity.**
> For example, an old family home opened to visitors can show how people lived and celebrated important occasions.
> That said, age alone does not make every building valuable.
> Overall, old buildings are worth protecting when they preserve meaningful history.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

论点回查：[E9](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#9-historical-sites-and-cultural-heritage)、[E10](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#10-heritage-sites--simplified-version) · [审计 P099](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p099)。

<a id="p100"></a>

### P100 · A｜Do you think all old buildings should be demolished?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T08 艺术、文化与传统 ＋ T10 地点、建筑与设施

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB19 主链 / 对照](#ab19)；辅助链：[AB21](#ab21)

**串法：** 文化价值与实际功能→决定保留或改造；不把所有旧建筑一概而论。

**答案：**

> No, I don't think all old buildings should be demolished.
> **Some preserve cultural traditions, while others still provide useful spaces for daily life.**
> For example, an old public library can remain a place for reading while reminding residents of their town's history.
> That said, some buildings may no longer be suitable for use.
> Overall, we should consider each building's history and usefulness before demolishing it.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

论点回查：[E9](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#9-historical-sites-and-cultural-heritage)、[E4](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#4-building-design) · [审计 P100](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p100)。

<a id="p126"></a>

### P126 · B｜Where do people in your country often go for holidays?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T08 艺术、文化与传统 ＋ T02 健康、习惯与休闲

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB19 主链 / 对照](#ab19)；辅助链：[AB05](#ab05)

**串法：** 列城市文化游与海边休闲两类 → 不同目的对应不同活动 → 用现有北京、海边素材。

**答案：**

> People may choose city breaks, coastal trips or visits to places with cultural interest.
> **What they want to experience** helps determine the destination.
> For example, a visitor might explore old streets in Beijing, while someone wanting outdoor relaxation could choose a coastal area.
> So, there isn’t one destination type that suits every holiday.

**学习提示：** 仅列类型与示意场景，不作全国出游量排名。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a person you know who really likes taking photos](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20person%20you%20know%20who%20really%20likes%20taking%20photos.md) · [Describe a place you have travelled to that you would like to recommend to others](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20place%20you%20have%20travelled%20to%20that%20you%20would%20like%20to%20recommend%20to%20others.md)

论点回查：[E38](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#38-what-are-the-benefits-of-parks--why-are-parks-important-in-cities-公园的益处)、[E9](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#9-historical-sites-and-cultural-heritage) · [审计 P126](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p126)。

<a id="p144"></a>

### P144 · B｜What special events are celebrated in your country?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T08 艺术、文化与传统 ＋ T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB19 主链 / 对照](#ab19)

**串法：** 春节等特殊日子 → 家庭共同准备食物和相聚 → 传统在参与中延续。

**答案：**

> Spring Festival is a clear example of a special event celebrated in China.
> **Preparing food and spending time together** help families keep familiar customs alive.
> For example, my family makes dumplings together for Lunar New Year, with different relatives helping with the preparation.
> So, celebrations can connect a special date with shared family traditions.

**学习提示：** 沿用家庭饺子素材，不声称湛江每家都这样过节。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a kind of food that you ate at special occasions](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20kind%20of%20food%20that%20you%20ate%20at%20special%20occasions.md)

论点回查：[E9](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#9-historical-sites-and-cultural-heritage)、[E34](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#34-why-real-interaction-builds-relationships-真实互动如何建立关系) · [审计 P144](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p144)。

<a id="p145"></a>

### P145 · B｜Do Chinese people like travelling abroad?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T08 艺术、文化与传统 ＋ T02 健康、习惯与休闲

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB19 主链 / 对照](#ab19)

**串法：** 本人和 Stella 的海外旅行 → 接触不同文化和生活 → 部分人喜欢；不推全国比例。

**答案：**

> Some Chinese people enjoy travelling abroad, and I’m one of them.
> **Experiencing a different place directly** can make its food and daily life more interesting to explore.
> For example, Stella and I visited Malaysia and enjoyed talking with a market vendor about unfamiliar ingredients.
> That said, one trip doesn’t tell us how everyone feels.
> Overall, foreign travel appeals to people who enjoy those experiences.

**学习提示：** 例子取自下列 Part 2 素材或其明确标示的情境迁移；不据此推断全国比例。

<a id="p148"></a>

### P148 · B｜Which is a better way to learn about a country: travelling there or reading books about it?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T08 艺术、文化与传统

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q2

逻辑链：[本节 AB19 主链 / 对照](#ab19)

**串法：** 旅行亲身参与，书籍提供文字背景 → 理解方式不同 → 按想了解的内容选择。

**答案：**

> Travelling offers direct experience, while books can provide background that a short visit may miss.
> **Taking part in local life and learning about its history** contribute to understanding in different ways.
> For example, a market visit lets someone discuss food with a vendor, while a book can explain the traditions behind it.
> So, combining travel and reading can offer a fuller understanding.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a plan that you had to change recently](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20plan%20that%20you%20had%20to%20change%20recently.md) · [Describe someone you know who enjoys learning about history](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20someone%20you%20know%20who%20enjoys%20learning%20about%20history.md)

论点回查：[E7](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#7-why-families-visit-museums)、[E8](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#8-museums-vs-passive-entertainment)、[E9](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#9-historical-sites-and-cultural-heritage) · [审计 P148](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p148)。

<a id="p257"></a>

### P257 · A｜Can movies help people better understand the cultural background of a country?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T08 艺术、文化与传统

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB19 主链 / 对照](#ab19)；辅助链：[AB02](#ab02)

**串法：** 电影呈现当地生活与传统 → 观众获得文化背景 → 更好理解，但一部电影不能代表全国。

**答案：**

> Yes, films can help people understand a country's cultural background.
> **Watching stories about local life can introduce traditions and give viewers a different perspective.**
> For example, a film showing a family festival can help viewers understand how relatives celebrate and spend time together.
> That said, one film cannot represent an entire country.
> Overall, films can be a useful starting point for cultural understanding.

论点回查：[E7](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#7-why-families-visit-museums)、[E9](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#9-historical-sites-and-cultural-heritage) · [审计 P257](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p257)。

<a id="p269"></a>

### P269 · A｜Do you think it is important to have a national identity?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T08 艺术、文化与传统

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB19 主链 / 对照](#ab19)

**串法：** 理解共同历史传统→身份归属→文化延续。

**答案：**

> Yes, I think a sense of national identity can be important.
> **Understanding shared history and traditions helps people recognise where their culture comes from.**
> For example, learning about a traditional festival can connect someone with customs passed down through their family.
> That said, people can value their identity without rejecting other cultures.
> Overall, national identity can give people a meaningful connection with their heritage.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

论点回查：[E9](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#9-historical-sites-and-cultural-heritage)、[E10](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#10-heritage-sites--simplified-version) · [审计 P269](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p269)。

<a id="p270"></a>

### P270 · A｜How can people develop their national identity?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T08 艺术、文化与传统

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q6

逻辑链：[本节 AB19 主链 / 对照](#ab19)

**串法：** 参与文化活动、了解遗产→增强身份理解。

**答案：**

> People can develop their national identity by learning about history and taking part in cultural traditions.
> **Active involvement can make these traditions feel connected to everyday life rather than just words in a book.**
> For example, a family can visit a heritage site and discuss the customs associated with it.
> So, learning and participation can strengthen people's understanding of their cultural identity.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

论点回查：[E9](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#9-historical-sites-and-cultural-heritage)、[E10](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#10-heritage-sites--simplified-version) · [审计 P270](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p270)。

<a id="p307"></a>

### P307 · A｜Why do people eat special food on special occasions or festivals?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T08 艺术、文化与传统 ＋ T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q1

逻辑链：[本节 AB19 主链 / 对照](#ab19)；辅助链：[AB07](#ab07)

**串法：** 共同食物习俗→文化身份与家庭联系→传统延续。

**答案：**

> People often eat special food on festivals because it connects them with family traditions.
> **Preparing and sharing familiar dishes can strengthen relationships and keep customs part of everyday experience.**
> For example, relatives can make a traditional festival dish together and explain the custom to younger family members.
> So, special food can help families maintain both their traditions and their connection.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

论点回查：[E9](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#9-historical-sites-and-cultural-heritage)、[E10](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#10-heritage-sites--simplified-version)、[E34](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#34-why-real-interaction-builds-relationships-真实互动如何建立关系) · [审计 P307](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p307)。

<a id="p308"></a>

### P308 · B｜What are the differences between everyday food and festival food?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T08 艺术、文化与传统 ＋ T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q2

逻辑链：[本节 AB19 主链 / 对照](#ab19)

**串法：** 日常食物侧重一餐需要，节日食物有共同准备与传统意义 → 活动和意义不同。

**答案：**

> Everyday food mainly fits an ordinary meal, while festival food can carry a stronger connection with shared traditions.
> **Preparing a familiar dish together** gives the special occasion meaning beyond simply eating.
> For example, a quick weekday dinner feels different from my family gathering to fold dumplings for Lunar New Year.
> So, the occasion and shared preparation can make the main difference.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

论点回查：[E9](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#9-historical-sites-and-cultural-heritage)、[E34](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#34-why-real-interaction-builds-relationships-真实互动如何建立关系) · [审计 P308](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p308)。

### P311 · B｜What kinds of food are popular in your country?

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB19 主链 / 对照](#ab19)

**串法：** 家庭中的饺子等熟悉食物 → 与共同习俗相关 → 举具体例子不概括全国最流行。

**答案：**

> Dumplings are one familiar example I can discuss, especially in connection with a family celebration.
> **Food linked with shared customs** can feel meaningful as well as enjoyable.
> For example, my family prepares dumplings together for Lunar New Year and talks while everyone helps.
> So, familiar dishes provide examples, but preferences vary across China.

**学习提示：** 例子取自下列 Part 2 素材或其明确标示的情境迁移；不据此推断全国比例。

<a id="ab20"></a>

## AB20 经验、熟悉度与责任不同 → 需要和习惯不同

**怎么串：** 比较两侧的经验或日程，补具体活动；不把年龄当作能力、兴趣或偏好的唯一原因。第二条是可能的变化方向，不是必然预测。

- **主链：** different experience, different levels of digital familiarity and responsibilities → different needs and habits
- **反向 / 对照：** easier technology and wider education →  generational gap might gradually narrow

| 原题 | 等级 | 这题必须补出的桥梁 |
|---|---|---|
| [P001](#p001) | B | 年长者可能更有健康问题的切身体会 → 更重视日常习惯；年轻人也可能因运动目标关注健康。 |
| [P057](#p057) | B | 年轻人与长者经验不同 → 可分别分享数字操作和生活经验 → 双向学习。 |
| [P059](#p059) | A | 经历、数字熟悉度与责任不同→需求习惯不同；再补变化限定。 |
| [P060](#p060) | B | 是否工作、照顾家庭及个人习惯不同 → 日常活动不同 → 列举时不把老人都当退休者。 |
| [P078](#p078) | B | 部分儿童学习时间较固定，成人受工作家庭占用 → 练习机会不同 → 有时儿童学起来较容易。 |
| [P127](#p127) | B | 年轻人的兴趣与可用假期不同 → 城市、海边等选择不同 → 用本人及 Stella 的旅行说明。 |
| [P146](#p146) | B | 旅行兴趣、经验和责任不按年龄固定分布 → 青年与长者都可能喜欢 → 不能用年龄单独排名。 |
| [P163](#p163) | B | 儿童可能有固定练习时段，成人可能有工作家庭责任 → 练习机会不一 → 不按年龄保证速度。 |
| [P164](#p164) | B | 儿童多从基础任务开始，成人常围绕工作或生活需要 → 学习目标和安排不同。 |
| [P179](#p179) | B | 工作、退休与家庭安排不同 → 晚间可用时间和活动不同 → 给两侧但保留重叠。 |
| [P193](#p193) | B | 二十多岁可能涉及工作等选择，后续年龄也有职业家庭变化 → 不能推重要决定多数集中该阶段。 |
| [P199](#p199) | B | 年轻人可能经历学习到工作的转换 → 日程更易变化；固定工作或退休者可能较稳定 → 有限比较。 |
| [P259](#p259) | B | 生活经历与当前关切不同 → 可能选择不同书籍；兴趣也重叠 → 不绝对按年龄分。 |
| [P265](#p265) | B | 长者可能更关注固定社区，年轻人也关心影响自己的本地问题 → 地方联系和需要决定关注。 |
| [P274](#p274) | B | 儿童探索兴趣，成人兼顾收入和责任 → 目标内容与判断条件不同 → 不绝对化。 |
| [P275](#p275) | B | 学习或初入职业阶段的需要 → 技能、工作或独立生活目标 → 用软件发展素材。 |
| [P300](#p300) | B | 经历和生活需要不同 → 视频选择可能不同 → 兴趣重叠，不按年龄锁定类型。 |

<a id="p001"></a>

### P001 · B｜Which group pays more attention to health, young people or old people?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T09 年龄与代际 ＋ T02 健康、习惯与休闲

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q2

逻辑链：[本节 AB20 主链 / 对照](#ab20)

**串法：** 年长者可能更有健康问题的切身体会 → 更重视日常习惯；年轻人也可能因运动目标关注健康。

**答案：**

> I’d expect older people to pay more attention to health, although that isn’t true of everyone.
> **Their experience of health problems** may make daily habits feel more important than they do to someone younger.
> For example, an older person may prioritise a regular bedtime, while a young athlete also takes sleep seriously for training.
> So, I’d lean towards older people, with plenty of individual exceptions.

**学习提示：** 比较是有限判断，不声称健康关注度的全国统计。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a person who loves to grow plants (e.g. vegetables, flowers) at home or in the garden](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20person%20who%20loves%20to%20grow%20plants%20%28e.g.%20vegetables%2C%20flowers%29%20at%20home%20or%20in%20the%20garden.md) · [Describe a person whose health and fitness you have helped to improve](🧡🥕%20Describe%20a%20person%20whose%20health%20and%20fitness%20you%20have%20helped%20to%20improve.md)

论点回查：[E5](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#5-older-vs-younger-people)、[E39](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#3940-balanced-routines-and-health-均衡作息与健康)、[E40](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#3940-balanced-routines-and-health-均衡作息与健康) · [审计 P001](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p001)。

<a id="p057"></a>

### P057 · B｜What can young and old people learn from each other?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T09 年龄与代际 ＋ T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q2

逻辑链：[本节 AB20 主链 / 对照](#ab20)；辅助链：[AB01](#ab01)

**串法：** 年轻人与长者经验不同 → 可分别分享数字操作和生活经验 → 双向学习。

**答案：**

> Young and old people can learn different things from each other because their experience isn’t identical.
> Younger people may share **digital skills**, while older people may offer experience of work or family responsibilities.
> For example, a student could help a grandparent use a messaging app and ask them how they handled a difficult work conversation.
> So, useful learning can go in both directions.

**学习提示：** 用 may 避免把数字能力或生活智慧按年龄固定划分。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a person who is good at learning and speaking new languages](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20person%20who%20is%20good%20at%20learning%20and%20speaking%20new%20languages.md) · [Describe someone who is older than you that you admire](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20someone%20who%20is%20older%20than%20you%20that%20you%20admire.md)

论点回查：[E5](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#5-older-vs-younger-people)、[E6](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#6-generational-gap)、[E22](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#22-what-is-the-most-important-factor-in-learning-well) · [审计 P057](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p057)。

<a id="p059"></a>

### P059 · A｜Why is there a generation gap between the young and the old?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T09 年龄与代际

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q1

逻辑链：[本节 AB20 主链 / 对照](#ab20)

**串法：** 经历、数字熟悉度与责任不同→需求习惯不同；再补变化限定。

**答案：**

> A generation gap often comes from different life experiences and responsibilities.
> **Different levels of familiarity with technology can also shape people's needs and habits.**
> For example, a younger person may prefer exchanging messages online, while an older relative may feel more comfortable talking in person.
> So, differences in experience can lead to different ways of communicating and living.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe someone who is older than you that you admire](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20someone%20who%20is%20older%20than%20you%20that%20you%20admire.md) · [Describe a person who loves to grow plants (e.g. vegetables, flowers) at home or in the garden](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20person%20who%20loves%20to%20grow%20plants%20%28e.g.%20vegetables%2C%20flowers%29%20at%20home%20or%20in%20the%20garden.md)

论点回查：[E5](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#5-older-vs-younger-people)、[E6](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#6-generational-gap) · [审计 P059](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p059)。

<a id="p060"></a>

### P060 · B｜What do old people usually do in their daily life?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T09 年龄与代际 ＋ T02 健康、习惯与休闲

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB20 主链 / 对照](#ab20)

**串法：** 是否工作、照顾家庭及个人习惯不同 → 日常活动不同 → 列举时不把老人都当退休者。

**答案：**

> Older people may spend their days working, looking after family, exercising or meeting friends.
> **Their responsibilities and interests** shape the routine, so retirement doesn’t describe everyone.
> For example, one older person might have a morning job, while another takes a walk and then helps care for a grandchild.
> So, older people’s daily lives can vary quite a lot.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a person who loves to grow plants (e.g. vegetables, flowers) at home or in the garden](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20person%20who%20loves%20to%20grow%20plants%20%28e.g.%20vegetables%2C%20flowers%29%20at%20home%20or%20in%20the%20garden.md) · [Describe someone who is older than you that you admire](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20someone%20who%20is%20older%20than%20you%20that%20you%20admire.md)

论点回查：[E5](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#5-older-vs-younger-people)、[E39](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#3940-balanced-routines-and-health-均衡作息与健康)、[E40](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#3940-balanced-routines-and-health-均衡作息与健康) · [审计 P060](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p060)。

<a id="p078"></a>

### P078 · B｜Why is it easier for children to learn new things than for adults?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T09 年龄与代际 ＋ T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q1

逻辑链：[本节 AB20 主链 / 对照](#ab20)；辅助链：[AB01](#ab01)

**串法：** 部分儿童学习时间较固定，成人受工作家庭占用 → 练习机会不同 → 有时儿童学起来较容易。

**答案：**

> It isn’t always easier for children, but some have more regular time available for learning.
> Adults may need to **fit practice around work and family responsibilities**, which can interrupt their progress.
> For example, a child may practise an instrument after school every day, while an adult can only manage occasional sessions.
> So, differences in time and responsibilities can help explain the gap.

**学习提示：** 先限制题目前提；不引入脑发育或儿童必然学得快的说法。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a new skill you learned when you were a child](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20new%20skill%20you%20learned%20when%20you%20were%20a%20child.md) · [Describe a long-term goal or ambition you would like to achieve](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20long-term%20goal%20or%20ambition%20you%20would%20like%20to%20achieve.md)

论点回查：[E5](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#5-older-vs-younger-people)、[E22](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#22-what-is-the-most-important-factor-in-learning-well) · [审计 P078](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p078)。

<a id="p127"></a>

### P127 · B｜Where do young people in your country often go for holidays?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T09 年龄与代际 ＋ T02 健康、习惯与休闲

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB20 主链 / 对照](#ab20)

**串法：** 年轻人的兴趣与可用假期不同 → 城市、海边等选择不同 → 用本人及 Stella 的旅行说明。

**答案：**

> I can speak more confidently about people I know than about all young people in China.
> **Their interests and available time** lead them to different places, from nearby cities to coastal destinations.
> For example, Stella and I chose Semporna because we wanted to explore the sea and try outdoor activities.
> So, the destination depends on what someone wants from the trip.

**学习提示：** 例子取自下列 Part 2 素材或其明确标示的情境迁移；不据此推断全国比例。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a place you have travelled to that you would like to recommend to others](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20place%20you%20have%20travelled%20to%20that%20you%20would%20like%20to%20recommend%20to%20others.md)

论点回查：[E5](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#5-older-vs-younger-people)、[E38](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#38-what-are-the-benefits-of-parks--why-are-parks-important-in-cities-公园的益处) · [审计 P127](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p127)。

<a id="p146"></a>

### P146 · B｜Who prefers travelling abroad, younger people or older people?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T09 年龄与代际

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q2

逻辑链：[本节 AB20 主链 / 对照](#ab20)

**串法：** 旅行兴趣、经验和责任不按年龄固定分布 → 青年与长者都可能喜欢 → 不能用年龄单独排名。

**答案：**

> I wouldn’t confidently say that either younger or older people prefer foreign travel as a whole.
> **Their experience, responsibilities and interests** can matter more than age alone.
> For example, a young worker may lack leave, while an older traveller has more time to explore other cultures.
> So, I’d compare their circumstances rather than choose one age group.

**学习提示：** 常识性两侧场景，不把旅行次数与偏好直接等同；不声称年龄组调查结论。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a place you have travelled to that you would like to recommend to others](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20place%20you%20have%20travelled%20to%20that%20you%20would%20like%20to%20recommend%20to%20others.md) · [Describe a person who loves to grow plants (e.g. vegetables, flowers) at home or in the garden](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20person%20who%20loves%20to%20grow%20plants%20%28e.g.%20vegetables%2C%20flowers%29%20at%20home%20or%20in%20the%20garden.md)

论点回查：[E5](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#5-older-vs-younger-people) · [审计 P146](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p146)。

<a id="p163"></a>

### P163 · B｜Do you think it is easier for children to learn new skills than for adults?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T09 年龄与代际 ＋ T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q2

逻辑链：[本节 AB20 主链 / 对照](#ab20)；辅助链：[AB01](#ab01)

**串法：** 儿童可能有固定练习时段，成人可能有工作家庭责任 → 练习机会不一 → 不按年龄保证速度。

**答案：**

> Children may find some skills easier to learn when they have more regular time to practise.
> Adults can face **work and family demands that interrupt learning**, although they may also organise practice very effectively.
> For example, a child might cycle with a parent each afternoon, while an adult can only practise at weekends.
> So, time and practice opportunities matter more than assuming children always learn faster.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a new skill you learned when you were a child](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20new%20skill%20you%20learned%20when%20you%20were%20a%20child.md) · [Describe a long-term goal or ambition you would like to achieve](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20long-term%20goal%20or%20ambition%20you%20would%20like%20to%20achieve.md)

论点回查：[E5](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#5-older-vs-younger-people)、[E22](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#22-what-is-the-most-important-factor-in-learning-well) · [审计 P163](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p163)。

<a id="p164"></a>

### P164 · B｜What are the differences between children learning skills and adults learning skills?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T09 年龄与代际 ＋ T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q2

逻辑链：[本节 AB20 主链 / 对照](#ab20)

**串法：** 儿童多从基础任务开始，成人常围绕工作或生活需要 → 学习目标和安排不同。

**答案：**

> Children and adults may learn skills for different reasons and fit them into different routines.
> Children might explore basic activities, while adults often **focus on a particular work or daily-life need**.
> For example, a child could learn to ride a bike with a parent, while an adult practises software skills around a job.
> So, their goals and responsibilities shape how they learn.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a new skill you learned when you were a child](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20new%20skill%20you%20learned%20when%20you%20were%20a%20child.md) · [Describe an important decision that you made](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20an%20important%20decision%20that%20you%20made.md)

论点回查：[E5](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#5-older-vs-younger-people)、[E22](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#22-what-is-the-most-important-factor-in-learning-well) · [审计 P164](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p164)。

<a id="p179"></a>

### P179 · B｜What are the differences between what old people and young people do in the evening?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T09 年龄与代际 ＋ T02 健康、习惯与休闲

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q2

逻辑链：[本节 AB20 主链 / 对照](#ab20)

**串法：** 工作、退休与家庭安排不同 → 晚间可用时间和活动不同 → 给两侧但保留重叠。

**答案：**

> Evening activities can differ because people have different responsibilities and routines.
> A younger worker may still be **fitting rest around deadlines**, while a retired person may have more flexibility earlier in the day.
> For example, one person might only have time for a late chat, while another has already finished gardening and visits family.
> So, the routine matters, and there is plenty of overlap between ages.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a person who loves to grow plants (e.g. vegetables, flowers) at home or in the garden](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20person%20who%20loves%20to%20grow%20plants%20%28e.g.%20vegetables%2C%20flowers%29%20at%20home%20or%20in%20the%20garden.md) · [Describe a well-organized person you know](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20well-organized%20person%20you%20know.md)

<a id="p193"></a>

### P193 · B｜Do you think most of the important decisions in people’s lives are made in their twenties?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T09 年龄与代际 ＋ T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB20 主链 / 对照](#ab20)

**串法：** 二十多岁可能涉及工作等选择，后续年龄也有职业家庭变化 → 不能推重要决定多数集中该阶段。

**答案：**

> I wouldn’t say most important decisions are necessarily made in people’s twenties.
> **Changing responsibilities can create important choices at several stages of life**.
> For example, someone may choose an early career in their twenties but reconsider their work after years in the same industry.
> So, important decisions aren’t confined to one decade.

**学习提示：** 题目已经包含不同阶段两侧，四句直接回应 most；不作年龄比例断言。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe an important decision that you made](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20an%20important%20decision%20that%20you%20made.md)

论点回查：[E5](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#5-older-vs-younger-people)、[E27](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#27-what-helps-people-make-an-informed-decision--version-2--example) · [审计 P193](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p193)。

<a id="p199"></a>

### P199 · B｜Who tends to change their daily routine more, young people or old people?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T09 年龄与代际 ＋ T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q2

逻辑链：[本节 AB20 主链 / 对照](#ab20)

**串法：** 年轻人可能经历学习到工作的转换 → 日程更易变化；固定工作或退休者可能较稳定 → 有限比较。

**答案：**

> I’d expect some younger people to change routines more as they move between study and work.
> **Changes in responsibilities can require a new daily schedule**, while someone in a settled situation may keep familiar habits.
> For example, starting a first full-time job may change a student’s day more than another ordinary week changes a retired person’s routine.
> So, life transitions may matter more than age on its own.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe an important decision that you made](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20an%20important%20decision%20that%20you%20made.md) · [Describe a person who loves to grow plants (e.g. vegetables, flowers) at home or in the garden](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20person%20who%20loves%20to%20grow%20plants%20%28e.g.%20vegetables%2C%20flowers%29%20at%20home%20or%20in%20the%20garden.md)

论点回查：[E5](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#5-older-vs-younger-people)、[E26](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#26-what-helps-people-make-an-informed-decision--version-1) · [审计 P199](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p199)。

<a id="p259"></a>

### P259 · B｜What kinds of books do young and older people enjoy?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T09 年龄与代际 ＋ T08 艺术、文化与传统

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q2

逻辑链：[本节 AB20 主链 / 对照](#ab20)

**串法：** 生活经历与当前关切不同 → 可能选择不同书籍；兴趣也重叠 → 不绝对按年龄分。

**答案：**

> Young and older readers may choose different books because their experiences and current concerns differ.
> **What feels relevant to them** can shape whether they pick fiction, history or something more practical.
> For example, a young reader might prefer career advice and an older friend history, but both could enjoy the same novel.
> So, age can influence interests without creating a fixed divide.

**学习提示：** 只作常识性示意，不把比本人年长的 Stella 直接归为老年人。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe an exciting book that you enjoyed reading](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20an%20exciting%20book%20that%20you%20enjoyed%20reading.md) · [Describe someone you know who enjoys learning about history](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20someone%20you%20know%20who%20enjoys%20learning%20about%20history.md)

<a id="p265"></a>

### P265 · B｜Do young people or the elderly care more about local news?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T09 年龄与代际 ＋ T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q2

逻辑链：[本节 AB20 主链 / 对照](#ab20)

**串法：** 长者可能更关注固定社区，年轻人也关心影响自己的本地问题 → 地方联系和需要决定关注。

**答案：**

> Older people may follow local news closely when their daily life is strongly connected with the neighbourhood.
> Younger people can be equally interested when **the story affects something they use or care about**.
> For example, an older resident and a young visitor may both follow news about a beach their family regularly visits.
> So, local connections may explain the interest better than age alone.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。
