论点回查：[E5](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#5-older-vs-younger-people)、[E34](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#34-why-real-interaction-builds-relationships-真实互动如何建立关系) · [审计 P265](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p265)。

<a id="p274"></a>

### P274 · B｜Is there any difference between children's ambitions and those of grown-ups?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T09 年龄与代际 ＋ T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q2

逻辑链：[本节 AB20 主链 / 对照](#ab20)

**串法：** 儿童探索兴趣，成人兼顾收入和责任 → 目标内容与判断条件不同 → 不绝对化。

**答案：**

> Children’s ambitions may focus on activities they enjoy, while adults often consider more practical responsibilities.
> **Experience of work and money** can change what someone wants from a goal.
> For example, a child might want to make games, while an adult wants software work that also provides a stable income.
> So, growing responsibilities can make ambitions more specific and practical.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a new skill you learned when you were a child](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20new%20skill%20you%20learned%20when%20you%20were%20a%20child.md) · [Describe a long-term goal or ambition you would like to achieve](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20long-term%20goal%20or%20ambition%20you%20would%20like%20to%20achieve.md)

论点回查：[E5](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#5-older-vs-younger-people)、[E27](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#27-what-helps-people-make-an-informed-decision--version-2--example) · [审计 P274](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p274)。

<a id="p275"></a>

### P275 · B｜What goals do young people usually have?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T09 年龄与代际 ＋ T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB20 主链 / 对照](#ab20)

**串法：** 学习或初入职业阶段的需要 → 技能、工作或独立生活目标 → 用软件发展素材。

**答案：**

> Young people may aim to finish their studies, develop useful skills or find suitable work.
> **The stage of life they are entering** helps explain which goals feel most urgent.
> For example, someone beginning a software career might focus on completing a few projects to demonstrate their ability.
> So, many goals relate to preparing for the next stage of adult life.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe an important decision that you made](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20an%20important%20decision%20that%20you%20made.md)

论点回查：[E5](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#5-older-vs-younger-people)、[E22](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#22-what-is-the-most-important-factor-in-learning-well)、[E27](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#27-what-helps-people-make-an-informed-decision--version-2--example) · [审计 P275](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p275)。

<a id="p300"></a>

### P300 · B｜Are there any differences between the videos that young people and old people like to watch?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T09 年龄与代际 ＋ T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q2

逻辑链：[本节 AB20 主链 / 对照](#ab20)

**串法：** 经历和生活需要不同 → 视频选择可能不同 → 兴趣重叠，不按年龄锁定类型。

**答案：**

> Younger and older people may prefer different videos because their everyday needs and experiences differ.
> **What they find useful or interesting** can matter more than age by itself.
> For example, a young worker might watch career advice and an older gardener plant care, while both enjoy nature documentaries.
> So, there can be differences without a fixed boundary between the age groups.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a person who loves to grow plants (e.g. vegetables, flowers) at home or in the garden](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20person%20who%20loves%20to%20grow%20plants%20%28e.g.%20vegetables%2C%20flowers%29%20at%20home%20or%20in%20the%20garden.md) · [Describe an interesting video](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20an%20interesting%20video.md)

论点回查：[E5](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#5-older-vs-younger-people)、[E3](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#3-technology--internet-reshaping-habits-互联网重塑习惯) · [审计 P300](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p300)。

<a id="ab21"></a>

## AB21 设计与设施匹配用途 → 使用方便舒适

**怎么串：** 建筑、商店和聚会场地按使用需要比较；具体说电梯、通风、位置、座位或空间，不只说漂亮。

- **主链：** practical design and accessible facilities → easier daily use → better quality of life
- **反向 / 对照：** appearance without function or poor maintenance → wasted resources → limited public value

| 原题 | 等级 | 这题必须补出的桥梁 |
|---|---|---|
| [P089](#p089) | B | 大店便于集中满足需求，小店可能适合简单购物 → 取决于本次需求 → 不声称多数统一偏好。 |
| [P106](#p106) | A | 绿色空间→运动与放松→生活质量。 |
| [P108](#p108) | B | 用北京车站和商场的拥挤场景 → 人流超过空间舒适使用量 → 限定地点和时段，不排名城市。 |
| [P109](#p109) | B | 居民需要休闲空间 → 城市有动力规划绿地 → 预测可能增加，但受土地与投入限制。 |
| [P111](#p111) | B | 拥挤地点仍提供需要的商店和餐饮 → 一次满足多种用途 → 愿意前往。 |
| [P122](#p122) | B | 通风、交通和日常设施满足需要 → 住起来方便舒适 → 用具体住宅例子而不概括全国。 |
| [P123](#p123) | B | 市区公寓可能依赖共用设施，乡村住宅可有不同空间布局 → 使用需求不同 → 比较具体条件。 |
| [P135](#p135) | B | 北京已有高层住宅和办公楼例子 → 满足不同用途 → 承认存在，不估计全国数量。 |
| [P136](#p136) | B | 住宅楼服务日常居住，办公楼服务工作会议 → 功能需求不同 → 内部设施不同。 |
| [P137](#p137) | B | 使用者需要不同 → 空间和道路布局不同 → 更方便实际使用。 |
| [P140](#p140) | B | 风格不能代表多数偏好 → 用简洁实用设计作一个选项 → 解释舒适和使用价值。 |
| [P142](#p142) | B | 公共场地设施便利，私人场地适合熟人相处 → 按人数和活动选 → 不虚构多数偏好。 |
| [P143](#p143) | B | 餐厅服务聚餐，酒吧适合以喝东西为主的聚会 → 功能和谈话环境不同 → 看活动需要。 |
| [P167](#p167) | B | 同一商场提供服装、餐厅和咖啡馆 → 一次出行满足多种需要 → 对顾客有吸引力。 |
| [P169](#p169) | B | 小店现有产品或设施不满足这次需求 → 比较和使用不方便 → 部分顾客不选。 |
| [P237](#p237) | B | 内容容易理解且场地设施可用 → 儿童能方便参与 → 适合到现场。 |

<a id="p089"></a>

### P089 · B｜Do most people prefer shopping at big stores or small stores?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T10 地点、建筑与设施 ＋ T05 经济与就业

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q2

逻辑链：[本节 AB21 主链 / 对照](#ab21)

**串法：** 大店便于集中满足需求，小店可能适合简单购物 → 取决于本次需求 → 不声称多数统一偏好。

**答案：**

> I wouldn’t say most people always prefer one size of shop.
> **The facilities and products they need** can make a large store useful for one trip and a small shop suitable for another.
> For example, someone comparing several laptops may want a larger selection, while a quick purchase may need only a small local shop.
> So, the purpose of the shopping trip matters more than size alone.

**学习提示：** 例子取自下列 Part 2 素材或其明确标示的情境迁移；不据此推断全国比例。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when you received good service from a staff member in a shop](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20received%20good%20service%20from%20a%20staff%20member%20in%20a%20shop.md) · [Describe a crowded place you went to](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20crowded%20place%20you%20went%20to.md)

论点回查：[E4](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#4-building-design)、[E55](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#55-how-can-losing-a-job-affect-peoples-standard-of-living) · [审计 P089](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p089)。

<a id="p106"></a>

### P106 · A｜Do you think there should be many natural places in cities?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T10 地点、建筑与设施 ＋ T02 健康、习惯与休闲

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB21 主链 / 对照](#ab21)；辅助链：[AB05](#ab05)

**串法：** 绿色空间→运动与放松→生活质量。

**答案：**

> Yes, I think cities should have plenty of natural spaces.
> **Parks give people places to exercise and relax, which can improve their quality of life.**
> For example, residents of a crowded neighbourhood can walk in a nearby park after work instead of staying indoors all evening.
> That said, these spaces need to be accessible and properly maintained.
> Overall, natural areas can make city life more enjoyable.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a tall building you like or dislike](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20tall%20building%20you%20like%20or%20dislike.md) · [Describe a home that you like to visit but do not want to live in](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20home%20that%20you%20like%20to%20visit%20but%20do%20not%20want%20to%20live%20in.md)

论点回查：[E38](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#38-what-are-the-benefits-of-parks--why-are-parks-important-in-cities-公园的益处) · [审计 P106](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p106)。

<a id="p108"></a>

### P108 · B｜Which cities in your country are particularly overcrowded?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T10 地点、建筑与设施

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB21 主链 / 对照](#ab21)

**串法：** 用北京车站和商场的拥挤场景 → 人流超过空间舒适使用量 → 限定地点和时段，不排名城市。

**答案：**

> Beijing is one city where particular places can feel overcrowded, especially at busy times.
> **Too many people using the same space** can make moving around and accessing facilities difficult.
> For example, Beijing South Railway Station can feel packed during a holiday, and popular malls can be crowded at weekends.
> So, I’d describe specific parts of Beijing as crowded rather than rank whole cities.

**学习提示：** 依据现有北京车站与商场故事；不补其他城市或全国拥挤排名。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a noisy place you have been to](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20noisy%20place%20you%20have%20been%20to.md) · [Describe a crowded place you went to](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20crowded%20place%20you%20went%20to.md)

论点回查：[E38](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#38-what-are-the-benefits-of-parks--why-are-parks-important-in-cities-公园的益处) · [审计 P108](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p108)。

<a id="p109"></a>

### P109 · B｜Do you think cities will have more or less green space in the future?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T10 地点、建筑与设施 ＋ T04 公平、规则与公共资源

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q4

逻辑链：[本节 AB21 主链 / 对照](#ab21)

**串法：** 居民需要休闲空间 → 城市有动力规划绿地 → 预测可能增加，但受土地与投入限制。

**答案：**

> I’d expect some cities to add more green space because residents need places to exercise and relax.
> **Including parks in practical urban design** can make crowded neighbourhoods more pleasant to use.
> For example, a lakeside walking area like the one in Olympic Forest Park gives people space away from traffic.
> That said, land and funding can limit new projects.
> So, more green space seems possible, but it won’t grow equally everywhere.

**学习提示：** 这是由需求与设计价值作出的条件预测，不声称已知规划或必然趋势。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a natural place in your city that you enjoy visiting](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20natural%20place%20in%20your%20city%20that%20you%20enjoy%20visiting.md)

论点回查：[E21](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#21-how-should-governments-spend-public-money)、[E38](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#38-what-are-the-benefits-of-parks--why-are-parks-important-in-cities-公园的益处) · [审计 P109](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p109)。

<a id="p111"></a>

### P111 · B｜Why do people go to crowded places?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T10 地点、建筑与设施 ＋ T05 经济与就业

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q1

逻辑链：[本节 AB21 主链 / 对照](#ab21)

**串法：** 拥挤地点仍提供需要的商店和餐饮 → 一次满足多种用途 → 愿意前往。

**答案：**

> People may go to crowded places because those places offer services they need.
> **Having shops and places to eat together** can make a trip convenient despite the crowds.
> For example, Stella and I went to a busy Beijing mall because we wanted to buy clothes and have dinner.
> So, people often accept the crowds in order to use what the place offers.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a crowded place you went to](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20crowded%20place%20you%20went%20to.md)

论点回查：[E4](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#4-building-design)、[E16](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#16-how-can-successful-companies-contribute-to-society--the-local-economy) · [审计 P111](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p111)。

<a id="p122"></a>

### P122 · B｜What kind of place do people in your country like to live in?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T10 地点、建筑与设施 ＋ T02 健康、习惯与休闲

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB21 主链 / 对照](#ab21)

**串法：** 通风、交通和日常设施满足需要 → 住起来方便舒适 → 用具体住宅例子而不概括全国。

**答案：**

> I’d say homes with convenient access and comfortable everyday conditions appeal to many people.
> **Useful facilities, fresh air and easy access** can matter more than an impressive appearance.
> For example, Stella’s high-rise apartment has a lovely view, but its crowded lifts would be a drawback for daily living.
> So, I’d focus on practical comfort rather than name one nationally preferred housing type.

**学习提示：** 用 I’d say 表示判断；不把单套公寓体验当住房偏好调查。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a home that you like to visit but do not want to live in](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20home%20that%20you%20like%20to%20visit%20but%20do%20not%20want%20to%20live%20in.md) · [Describe a tall building you like or dislike](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20tall%20building%20you%20like%20or%20dislike.md)

论点回查：[E4](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#4-building-design)、[E38](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#38-what-are-the-benefits-of-parks--why-are-parks-important-in-cities-公园的益处) · [审计 P122](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p122)。

<a id="p123"></a>

### P123 · B｜What’s the difference between homes in cities and those in the countryside?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T10 地点、建筑与设施 ＋ T02 健康、习惯与休闲

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q2

逻辑链：[本节 AB21 主链 / 对照](#ab21)

**串法：** 市区公寓可能依赖共用设施，乡村住宅可有不同空间布局 → 使用需求不同 → 比较具体条件。

**答案：**

> City homes are often part of larger buildings, while a countryside home may offer a different layout and more direct outdoor access.
> **Shared facilities and available space** affect how residents use each kind of home.
> For example, a high-rise flat may require a lift journey, whereas a house with a garden may open straight onto outdoor space.
> So, the practical differences depend on the building and its surroundings.

**学习提示：** 城市侧借公寓素材，乡村侧为常识性假设对照，不声称所有乡村住房都大。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a home that you like to visit but do not want to live in](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20home%20that%20you%20like%20to%20visit%20but%20do%20not%20want%20to%20live%20in.md) · [Describe a person who loves to grow plants (e.g. vegetables, flowers) at home or in the garden](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20person%20who%20loves%20to%20grow%20plants%20%28e.g.%20vegetables%2C%20flowers%29%20at%20home%20or%20in%20the%20garden.md)

论点回查：[E4](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#4-building-design)、[E38](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#38-what-are-the-benefits-of-parks--why-are-parks-important-in-cities-公园的益处) · [审计 P123](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p123)。

<a id="p135"></a>

### P135 · B｜Are there many tall buildings in your country?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T10 地点、建筑与设施

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB21 主链 / 对照](#ab21)

**串法：** 北京已有高层住宅和办公楼例子 → 满足不同用途 → 承认存在，不估计全国数量。

**答案：**

> Tall buildings are certainly a visible part of cities such as Beijing.
> **They provide homes and workplaces**, although their everyday usefulness depends on their design.
> For example, Stella lives in a high-rise apartment, and we used to attend meetings in a nearby office tower.
> So, I can give clear city examples, even though I don’t know the national total.

**学习提示：** 例子取自下列 Part 2 素材或其明确标示的情境迁移；不据此推断全国比例。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a tall building you like or dislike](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20tall%20building%20you%20like%20or%20dislike.md) · [Describe a home that you like to visit but do not want to live in](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20home%20that%20you%20like%20to%20visit%20but%20do%20not%20want%20to%20live%20in.md)

论点回查：[E4](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#4-building-design) · [审计 P135](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p135)。

<a id="p136"></a>

### P136 · B｜What are the differences between those tall buildings in your country?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T10 地点、建筑与设施

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q2

逻辑链：[本节 AB21 主链 / 对照](#ab21)

**串法：** 住宅楼服务日常居住，办公楼服务工作会议 → 功能需求不同 → 内部设施不同。

**答案：**

> Some tall buildings are mainly residential, while others are designed for office work.
> **Different daily needs lead to different facilities and layouts**.
> For example, Stella’s building contains apartments, while the tower we visited for work has offices, cafés and shared meeting rooms.
> So, their purpose shapes the main differences between them.

**学习提示：** 例子取自下列 Part 2 素材或其明确标示的情境迁移；不据此推断全国比例。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a tall building you like or dislike](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20tall%20building%20you%20like%20or%20dislike.md) · [Describe a home that you like to visit but do not want to live in](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20home%20that%20you%20like%20to%20visit%20but%20do%20not%20want%20to%20live%20in.md)

论点回查：[E4](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#4-building-design) · [审计 P136](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p136)。

<a id="p137"></a>

### P137 · B｜Why are different places laid out and designed differently?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T10 地点、建筑与设施

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q1

逻辑链：[本节 AB21 主链 / 对照](#ab21)

**串法：** 使用者需要不同 → 空间和道路布局不同 → 更方便实际使用。

**答案：**

> Places are designed differently because the people using them need different things.
> **A layout works best when it fits the activities taking place there**.
> For example, an office building needs spaces for meetings, while a park needs paths and open areas where people can walk.
> So, different functions call for different designs.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a tall building you like or dislike](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20tall%20building%20you%20like%20or%20dislike.md) · [Describe a natural place in your city that you enjoy visiting](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20natural%20place%20in%20your%20city%20that%20you%20enjoy%20visiting.md)

论点回查：[E4](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#4-building-design) · [审计 P137](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p137)。

<a id="p140"></a>

### P140 · B｜What kind of interior design style do most people like?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T10 地点、建筑与设施 ＋ T08 艺术、文化与传统

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB21 主链 / 对照](#ab21)

**串法：** 风格不能代表多数偏好 → 用简洁实用设计作一个选项 → 解释舒适和使用价值。

**答案：**

> I wouldn’t claim that most people prefer one interior style.
> A simple, practical style can appeal because **it makes a room comfortable and easy to use**.
> For example, an uncluttered seating area with good light could make a flat pleasant for coffee and conversation.
> So, I’d talk about useful features rather than assume one style suits everyone.

**学习提示：** 例子取自下列 Part 2 素材或其明确标示的情境迁移；不据此推断全国比例。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a home that you like to visit but do not want to live in](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20home%20that%20you%20like%20to%20visit%20but%20do%20not%20want%20to%20live%20in.md)

论点回查：[E4](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#4-building-design)、[E7](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#7-why-families-visit-museums) · [审计 P140](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p140)。

<a id="p142"></a>

### P142 · B｜Do most people prefer to hold a party in a public place or a private place?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T10 地点、建筑与设施 ＋ T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q2

逻辑链：[本节 AB21 主链 / 对照](#ab21)

**串法：** 公共场地设施便利，私人场地适合熟人相处 → 按人数和活动选 → 不虚构多数偏好。

**答案：**

> I don’t think there is one choice that most people make for every party.
> **A public venue may provide more facilities**, while a private home can suit a small, relaxed gathering.
> For example, three close friends may be comfortable on Stella’s sofa, whereas a much larger group may need a separate venue.
> So, the group’s size and activities help determine the better setting.

**学习提示：** 例子取自下列 Part 2 素材或其明确标示的情境迁移；不据此推断全国比例。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a party for a special event that impressed you a lot](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20party%20for%20a%20special%20event%20that%20impressed%20you%20a%20lot.md)

论点回查：[E4](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#4-building-design)、[E34](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#34-why-real-interaction-builds-relationships-真实互动如何建立关系) · [审计 P142](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p142)。

<a id="p143"></a>

### P143 · B｜Do most people prefer to hold a party in a restaurant or in a pub?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T10 地点、建筑与设施 ＋ T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q2

逻辑链：[本节 AB21 主链 / 对照](#ab21)

**串法：** 餐厅服务聚餐，酒吧适合以喝东西为主的聚会 → 功能和谈话环境不同 → 看活动需要。

**答案：**

> I’d choose a restaurant for a meal together, but a pub may suit a gathering centred on drinks.
> **The kind of activity and the ease of conversation** matter more than the label of the venue.
> For example, friends wanting a quiet catch-up might choose a place where they can hear each other over dinner.
> So, I wouldn’t assume the same venue suits most parties.

**学习提示：** 给出条件比较，不把个人选择冒充全国偏好。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a crowded place you went to](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20crowded%20place%20you%20went%20to.md) · [Describe a party for a special event that impressed you a lot](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20party%20for%20a%20special%20event%20that%20impressed%20you%20a%20lot.md)

论点回查：[E4](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#4-building-design)、[E34](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#34-why-real-interaction-builds-relationships-真实互动如何建立关系) · [审计 P143](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p143)。

<a id="p167"></a>

### P167 · B｜Why are shopping malls so popular in China?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T10 地点、建筑与设施 ＋ T05 经济与就业

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q1

逻辑链：[本节 AB21 主链 / 对照](#ab21)

**串法：** 同一商场提供服装、餐厅和咖啡馆 → 一次出行满足多种需要 → 对顾客有吸引力。

**答案：**

> Shopping malls can be attractive because they bring several useful facilities into one place.
> **Combining shopping, food and somewhere to sit** makes a visit serve more than one purpose.
> For example, Stella and I went to a Beijing mall to look for clothes and have dinner together.
> So, that mix of facilities helps explain their appeal.

**学习提示：** 以北京商场故事解释吸引力，不量化全国受欢迎程度。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a crowded place you went to](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20crowded%20place%20you%20went%20to.md)

论点回查：[E4](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#4-building-design)、[E16](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#16-how-can-successful-companies-contribute-to-society--the-local-economy) · [审计 P167](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p167)。

<a id="p169"></a>

### P169 · B｜Why do some people not like shopping in small shops?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T10 地点、建筑与设施 ＋ T05 经济与就业

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q1

逻辑链：[本节 AB21 主链 / 对照](#ab21)

**串法：** 小店现有产品或设施不满足这次需求 → 比较和使用不方便 → 部分顾客不选。

**答案：**

> Some people avoid small shops because the available products or facilities don’t meet their needs.
> **A limited selection can make it harder to compare suitable options** in one visit.
> For example, someone looking for a lightweight laptop may want to try several models that a particular small shop doesn’t stock.
> So, customers may avoid a shop whose selection doesn’t fit their needs.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when you received good service from a staff member in a shop](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20received%20good%20service%20from%20a%20staff%20member%20in%20a%20shop.md)

论点回查：[E4](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#4-building-design)、[E16](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#16-how-can-successful-companies-contribute-to-society--the-local-economy) · [审计 P169](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p169)。

<a id="p237"></a>

### P237 · B｜What types of sports matches are suitable for children to attend?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T10 地点、建筑与设施 ＋ T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB21 主链 / 对照](#ab21)

**串法：** 内容容易理解且场地设施可用 → 儿童能方便参与 → 适合到现场。

**答案：**

> A local match with clear rules and a comfortable venue can be a good choice for children to attend.
> **Easy access and usable facilities** help them enjoy the event without becoming overwhelmed.
> For example, a small badminton event with clear seating and nearby toilets may be more manageable than a packed arena.
> So, the venue matters as well as the sport when children attend in person.

**学习提示：** 小型赛事是假设对照，区分内容适合与到场条件。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a live sport match you have ever watched](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95Describe%20a%20live%20sport%20match%20you%20have%20ever%20watched.md) · [Describe a noisy place you have been to](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20noisy%20place%20you%20have%20been%20to.md)

论点回查：[E35](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#35-what-does-effective-teamwork-mean-有效团队合作)、[E4](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#4-building-design) · [审计 P237](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p237)。

<a id="ab22"></a>

## AB22 兴趣和需要匹配 → 内容值得投入

**怎么串：** 采用现有 C01 补充链，只承接本文件中的 A/B 原题。聊天、书籍和视频都解释对这个人有什么价值；不以少数人的兴趣推全国排名。

- **主链：** content matches personal interests or needs → greater value to the person → willingness to spend time or money
- **反向 / 对照：** irrelevant or repetitive content → little new value → boredom or a feeling of wasted time

| 原题 | 等级 | 这题必须补出的桥梁 |
|---|---|---|
| [P115](#p115) | B | 长者分享的内容与年轻人当前关切不匹配 → 看不到个人价值 → 不愿继续听。 |
| [P118](#p118) | B | 双方兴趣或话题不同 → 难理解为什么值得听 → 感到无聊；不是年龄本身造成。 |
| [P158](#p158) | B | 用朋友聊天范围说明书籍、电影、工作等 → 内容关联兴趣和需要 → 不概括所有青年。 |
| [P254](#p254) | B | 用讨论或观看过的电影作有限例子 → 兴趣和内容价值不同 → 不推全国最热类型。 |
| [P258](#p258) | B | 本人及朋友读心理叙事、文学和历史 → 内容契合不同关切 → 举范围明确的类别。 |
| [P296](#p296) | B | 本人和朋友观看自然、动物或体育内容 → 兴趣与当前疑问匹配 → 愿意看。 |

<a id="p115"></a>

### P115 · B｜Why aren't young people willing to listen to the experiences of older people?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响 ＋ T09 年龄与代际

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q1

逻辑链：[本节 AB22 主链 / 对照](#ab22)

**串法：** 长者分享的内容与年轻人当前关切不匹配 → 看不到个人价值 → 不愿继续听。

**答案：**

> Some young people may struggle to see how an older person’s experience relates to their own concerns.
> When **the topic feels distant from what they need**, they can lose interest before understanding its value.
> For example, someone worried about work might initially switch off during a detailed story about growing tomatoes.
> That said, a connection can emerge if they listen a little longer.
> So, the problem can be relevance rather than the speaker’s age.

**学习提示：** 借用同事讲种植的故事；将讲述者换成长者属于示意迁移，不增写人物事实。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when someone talked about something you were not interested in, but you continued listening](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20someone%20talked%20about%20something%20you%20were%20not%20interested%20in%2C%20but%20you%20continued%20listening.md)

论点回查：[E5](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#5-older-vs-younger-people)、[E30](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#30-example-of-active-listening-积极倾听的例子) · [审计 P115](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p115)。

<a id="p118"></a>

### P118 · B｜Why do some young people feel bored when talking with old people?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响 ＋ T09 年龄与代际

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q1

逻辑链：[本节 AB22 主链 / 对照](#ab22)

**串法：** 双方兴趣或话题不同 → 难理解为什么值得听 → 感到无聊；不是年龄本身造成。

**答案：**

> Some young people feel bored because the topic doesn’t match their interests at that moment.
> **Not seeing a connection with their own life** makes it harder to stay engaged.
> For example, a young listener may find a long explanation of soil and plant pots dull while worrying about a work deadline.
> So, a mismatch in the conversation matters more than age alone.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when someone talked about something you were not interested in, but you continued listening](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20someone%20talked%20about%20something%20you%20were%20not%20interested%20in%2C%20but%20you%20continued%20listening.md)

论点回查：[E5](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#5-older-vs-younger-people)、[E30](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#30-example-of-active-listening-积极倾听的例子) · [审计 P118](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p118)。

<a id="p158"></a>

### P158 · B｜What kinds of topics do young people like to talk about nowadays?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响 ＋ T09 年龄与代际

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB22 主链 / 对照](#ab22)

**串法：** 用朋友聊天范围说明书籍、电影、工作等 → 内容关联兴趣和需要 → 不概括所有青年。

**答案：**

> Among people I know, conversations include books, films, work and future plans.
> **Topics connected with their interests or current concerns** give them a reason to keep talking.
> For example, Stella and I can move from a novel we’ve read to worries about a demanding week at work.
> So, the topics depend on what matters to the people having the conversation.

**学习提示：** 例子取自下列 Part 2 素材或其明确标示的情境迁移；不据此推断全国比例。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe an enjoyable evening with your friends](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20an%20enjoyable%20evening%20with%20your%20friends.md)

论点回查：[E5](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#5-older-vs-younger-people)、[E34](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#34-why-real-interaction-builds-relationships-真实互动如何建立关系) · [审计 P158](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p158)。

<a id="p254"></a>

### P254 · B｜What kinds of movies are the most popular in China?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T08 艺术、文化与传统

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB22 主链 / 对照](#ab22)

**串法：** 用讨论或观看过的电影作有限例子 → 兴趣和内容价值不同 → 不推全国最热类型。

**答案：**

> I can give examples of films people discuss, but I wouldn’t claim one genre is the most popular across China.
> **Different viewers look for different things**, such as an engaging story or a chance to relax.
> For example, friends may enjoy discussing a story like Harry Potter, while an action film may appeal to someone wanting excitement.
> So, I’d describe a range of interests rather than claim a national favourite.

**学习提示：** Part 2 只有个人观影与聊天素材，不能据此生成票房或全国偏好结论。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe your least favourite movie](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20your%20least%20favourite%20movie.md) · [Describe an enjoyable evening with your friends](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20an%20enjoyable%20evening%20with%20your%20friends.md)

论点回查：[E7](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#7-why-families-visit-museums) · [审计 P254](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p254)。

<a id="p258"></a>

### P258 · B｜What kinds of books do people in your country enjoy reading?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T08 艺术、文化与传统

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB22 主链 / 对照](#ab22)

**串法：** 本人及朋友读心理叙事、文学和历史 → 内容契合不同关切 → 举范围明确的类别。

**答案：**

> People I know enjoy fiction, history and books that help them think about everyday concerns.
> **A book feels worth reading when it connects with their interests or needs**.
> For example, I found Counselling for Toads engaging, while Stella also enjoys reading about history.
> So, I can describe different interests within my circle without naming one national preference.

**学习提示：** 例子取自下列 Part 2 素材或其明确标示的情境迁移；不据此推断全国比例。

论点回查：[E7](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#7-why-families-visit-museums)、[E9](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#9-historical-sites-and-cultural-heritage) · [审计 P258](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p258)。

<a id="p296"></a>

### P296 · B｜What kind of videos do people in your country like to watch?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T08 艺术、文化与传统 ＋ T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB22 主链 / 对照](#ab22)

**串法：** 本人和朋友观看自然、动物或体育内容 → 兴趣与当前疑问匹配 → 愿意看。

**答案：**

> People I know watch nature videos, sports clips and programmes connected with their hobbies.
> **A video is more engaging when it answers a question or matches an existing interest**.
> For example, seeing a sea turtle in Malaysia made me interested in a documentary about plastic pollution in the ocean.
> So, I’d describe those interests rather than claim one national viewing preference.

**学习提示：** 例子取自下列 Part 2 素材或其明确标示的情境迁移；不据此推断全国比例。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe an interesting video](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20an%20interesting%20video.md) · [Describe something you do regularly that you think is a waste of time](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20something%20you%20do%20regularly%20that%20you%20think%20is%20a%20waste%20of%20time.md)

论点回查：[E7](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#7-why-families-visit-museums)、[E3](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#3-technology--internet-reshaping-habits-互联网重塑习惯) · [审计 P296](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p296)。

## 按原题编号查找

<a id="practice-errors"></a>

## 口述错误统一归纳（Error Log）

### 2026-09-10｜AB01：老师、运动员与儿童技能

**记录口径：** 只收录本轮自行组织的 original answers 中出现的问题，不记录单纯复述 Band 7 范文的回答。10 次原创回答对应 8 道题；正文更新为本轮提供的最新 Band 7 版本，P018、P036 使用 Version 2。以下同时保留 Version 1 与 Version 2 的错误来源；修正尽量基于原表达做最小改动。正文将 sport person 统一为题目中的 sportsperson。

#### 语法与句子结构

| 错误点 | 本轮原表达 | 最小修正 / 复练规则 | 对应题目 |
|---|---|---|---|
| feedback 不可数 | useful feedbacks; those feedbacks; those feedback | useful feedback; that feedback。不能加 -s，也不用 those 修饰。 | [P005](#p005)、[P018 V2](#p018)、[P036 V2](#p036) |
| advice 不可数 | give a advice | give advice / give some advice；一条建议用 a piece of advice。 | [P036 V2](#p036) |
| willing 前用 be | students don't willing | students aren't willing to hear it / students don't want to hear it。 | [P005](#p005) |
| 动名词短语作主语 | learning skills help | learning new skills helps。对比：Regular practice and useful feedback help…（两个并列主语）。 | [P035](#p035) |
| 并列结构保持一致 | are being disciplined and have willingness | What truly matters is being disciplined and willing to accept feedback。 | [P018 V2](#p018) |
| 并列结构与谓语一致 | the willingness to improve and accepting suggestions … matters | a willingness to improve and accept suggestions … matters；主语中心词为单数 willingness。 | [P018 V2](#p018) |
| 冠词 | disciplined and organized approach | a disciplined and organized approach。 | [P018 V1](#p018) |
| 动词不能直接叠加 | make adjust their solutions and approach | adjust their approach / make adjustments to their approach。 | [P036 V2](#p036) |
| 不定式后的并列动词 | could practice cooking … or learning them | could practice cooking … or learn a new skill。 | [P036 V1](#p036) |
| 泛指多项技能 | teach new skill | teach new skills / teach a new skill。 | [P037](#p037) |
| 代词保持一致 | his serve … their coach; he … their | 同一人物统一用 their / they：their serve … their coach。 | [P019](#p019)、[P018 V2](#p018) |
| 句子结构断裂、重复起句 | First, regular practice… as for discipline and consistent practice, which builds practical skills through regular practice. | First, discipline and consistent practice help them build practical skills. 先说完整主谓宾，避免重复 regular practice。 | [P018 V2](#p018) |

#### 搭配与表达优化（不全部属于语法错误）

| 本轮原表达 | 更自然 / 更清楚的表达 | 对应题目 |
|---|---|---|
| a class … their weak essay | students … their weak essays；多人各自修改作文时用复数更清楚。 | [P005](#p005) |
| may help them improve, but receive complaints | may help them improve but may also receive complaints；原句可共用主语与 may，并非必然有语法错误，补出 may also 更清楚。 | [P005](#p005) |
| give useful feedback, which is the most important, I think | most importantly, give useful feedback；明确强调反馈。 | [P006](#p006) |
| how to improve it | how they can improve；避免 it 指代不清。 | [P006](#p006) |
| plan the training | plan their training。 | [P010](#p010) |
| adjust their techniques / his techniques | adjust their technique；指跑步整体技术时单数更自然，多种不同技巧才用复数。 | [P010](#p010)、[P018 V2](#p018) |
| sensible planning schedule; important athlete qualities | sensible planning / a sensible training schedule; important qualities for an athlete。 | [P010](#p010) |
| patience with practice | patience, consistent practice；分别表达耐心和持续练习。 | [P018 V1](#p018) |
| gives them statistics, something specific to improve | gives them something specific to improve next time；去掉连接不清的 statistics。 | [P019](#p019) |
| practice the part; feedback and improvements | work on the part; feedback and improvement；这里泛指改进过程。 | [P019](#p019) |
| not that kind of on the books | beyond textbooks。 | [P035](#p035) |
| improve what they can actually do | improve step by step；原表达可以成立，简化后更易口述。 | [P035](#p035) |
| at schools; in an activity class | at school; in activity classes；泛指学习场景时更自然，in an activity class 本身正确。 | [P036 V1/V2](#p036) |
| several settings can work well | 原句正确；as long as they provide practical guidance 更明确地表达条件。 | [P036 V1](#p036) |
| adjust their solutions（做饭语境） | adjust their approach / adjust what they did。 | [P036 V2](#p036) |
| give children regular practice | give children enough time / opportunities to practice。 | [P037](#p037) |
| rigid instructions by others; via their original method | rigid instructions; using their own method / ideas。 | [P037](#p037) |

#### 例子如何接回核心观点

| 对应题目 | 本轮问题 | 最小补强方式 |
|---|---|---|
| [P035](#p035) | 前句提到 feedback，但原来的做饭例子只说明独立生活，没有呈现反馈。 | 在例子中加入 get tips from a parent, and adjust next time，再接独立性这一结果。 |
| [P037](#p037) | 纸桥 / 自己解决问题的例子没有说明为什么能提升技能。 | 补出 test their ideas, see what works, and make adjustments，让探索与修正过程具体化。 |

**复练重点：** feedback / advice 不可数；learning new skills helps；adjust 或 make adjustments to；并列结构保持一致。例子需要体现核心机制；若例子已经说清机制与结果，就不必机械地再补一句解释。
